
Chapter 1 I'm out of prison!



Su Mu's Diary, 30,025 years after transmigrating, overcast weather. Does this place even have a concept of weather?

In a chaotic darkness, a line of conspicuous, faintly glowing white characters suddenly appeared in mid-air.

Or perhaps not mid-air, since it was all black, nothing at all.

Su Mu mentally made a self-deprecating remark.

Then the line of white characters began to recede, disappearing into the depths of this unknown space.

He was a transmigrator, but now he only remembered that he had transmigrated, and that his name before transmigrating was Su Mu; nothing else.

Over 30 million years, he had been here for so long, and now he didn't even remember what he looked like in his previous life!

In these 30 million years of waiting, he had experienced madness, anger, and emptiness.

Now he felt he had no desires, had completely attained enlightenment.

Just then, his vision, or rather his senses—after all, he wasn't human now, and didn't have eyes—...

Of course, that's not the point. The point is:

Something changed in this boundless space.

—

In a dark forest, a dozen or so people in tattered coats were talking in a clearing.

At the center of the group was an intricate pattern drawn in blood.

An elderly man with a white beard stood directly in front of the pattern.

The old man raised his hands and shouted, "Great Nemori Solka, we offer you fresh flesh and blood, praying for your arrival to lead us to control this world!"

The others remained silent, watching this scene.

As the old man finished reciting a difficult incantation, a burly man who had been standing behind him stepped forward.

He was expressionless, offering no resistance, and dragged a girl who was struggling violently.

The girl's hands and feet were bound, and her mouth was gagged with a rag; she could only writhe and whimper.

Among the crowd, one man, seeing the girl's fearful eyes, felt a pang of pity and whispered to his companion, "Will this really work? So many sacrifices have already been made..."

"Shut up! If the High Priest says it's okay, then it is! Lord Nemori Solka will definitely descend this time!"

The companion rebuked him righteously, then glanced around in the shadow of his hat brim before muttering, "Now you're overflowing with sympathy? You've had your share of those before."

Stunned by his words, the man immediately retorted, "That, that's different! Those old people were already nearing their end; we're just letting them fulfill their final purpose!"

"Heh."

The girl was tossed into the center of the pattern. The old man glanced at her, his cloudy eyes filled with compassion, but his actions were swift and decisive.

He drew a slightly rusty dagger from his waist.

“Lord, I will offer the blood of my pure son to gain your favor!”

Then he slowly stepped forward, stopping in front of the girl, and pointed the dagger at her neck.

—

The girl's name was Alice, she was 18 years old, just an ordinary village girl.

She had come out today hoping to find truffles or something nice to eat on her birthday, but due to carelessness, she was captured by this group of cultists.

Her heart was filled with terror.

No, I don't want to die! Please, anyone, save me!

*Crack*

Just before the dagger touched the girl's neck,

a sudden sound, like ice cracking, rang in everyone's minds.

Then, in the center of the intricate pattern drawn with blood, directly above where the girl stood,

a crack appeared in that space.

*Crack*

The crack began to widen.

The old man, terrified by the bizarre scene before him, stumbled back several steps and fell to the ground.

His eyes widened, his face a mask of disbelief, as he stared at the ever-expanding crack.

"This, how is this possible?"

*Crack!*

That space was completely shattered, the scattered fragments retaining their original colors.

It looked like fragments torn from a canvas.

The shattered space was a deep, dark abyss.

Nothing could be seen inside, only ominous black mist slowly seeping out from the cracks.

Everyone present was stunned by this bizarre scene; no one dared to move, even their breathing stopped.

A few more seconds passed, and the space remained unchanged except for the continued emergence of black mist.

This time, the old man shakily stood up, his eyes warily fixed on the interior of the shattered space.

Then he looked at a young man beside him.

“You, go up and personally offer the sacrifice to the Lord.”

The young man snapped out of his daze upon hearing this and waved his hands repeatedly.

“High, High Priest, I…”

Before the young man could finish speaking, the old man snapped in a hoarse voice, “Shut up! Do you want the great Lord to wait?”

“If Lord Nemori Solka is angered, none of us will survive.”

As the old man finished speaking, the tense crowd turned their gazes to the young man.

Their eyes held no pity, only threat.

They didn't care what the consequences of bloodletting would be, but they were unwilling to accept anything detrimental to them.

The young man's face paled, but he still stepped forward. He knew very well that if he dared not go, he might become food in the next second.

The old man handed the young man the dagger and said grandly, “This is a blessing from the Lord; accept it with gratitude!”

The old man then stepped back a few paces from the center.

The young man's hand holding the dagger trembled as he cautiously approached the black mist, beads of cold sweat forming on his forehead. He hesitated, unable to make his move.

The old man, growing impatient, snapped, "What are you waiting for? You should know the consequences of disrespecting my lord!"

Hearing this, the young man gritted his teeth, closed his eyes, and plunged the dagger into the girl shrouded in black mist.

...

...

?

Something feels off?

It doesn't feel like stabbing flesh, it feels like stabbing...water?

The young man peeked out, trying to see what was happening.

Then he witnessed an unbelievable scene: the dagger in his hand was stuck at the girl's neck, unable to move an inch, as if it were jammed by something.

How could this be? There's clearly nothing here!

Could it be that the black mist is blocking it?

In that instant, an eye appeared before him.

It hovered there, only an inch away.

The eye had no form, nor was it an eyeball; it appeared abruptly, as if space itself had opened its eyes.

It was a crimson pupil.

The moment their eyes met, the young man felt a wave of dizziness, as if his mind and body no longer belonged to him.

Then, in a daze, the young man pulled the dagger that had been embedded in the black mist, and suddenly began running and jumping inexplicably, like a crazed madman, yet his face remained eerily calm.

The old man and the other cultists, witnessing this, broke out in a cold sweat and retreated from the black mist that had stopped spreading and only covered the area of ​​the pattern.



Chapter 2: Stumbled upon a cult gathering just outside the door?


The moment Su Mu sensed the change in space, he rushed to the affected area to investigate.

When he reached the only crack, he was excited.

Although he didn't know why the crack had appeared, what if it connected to Earth?

As the crack gradually widened, Su Mu couldn't resist reaching out his "hand" to try and break it open.

Of course, he didn't have a hand; it was just a metaphor, or rather, he was trying to shatter the crack with his will.

As he wished, the crack shattered immediately after his thought arose, allowing him to see his surroundings.

And for the first time, he learned that the space he was in wasn't just darkness but also a mass of black mist.

But none of that mattered.

The important thing was that he had reappeared in this world!

He saw living, moving humans again!

Su Mu observed his surroundings through his blood-red eye.

It was still a panoramic view.

He could see the entire landscape in 360 degrees.

Seeing a murder scene right off the bat was quite thrilling.

As a former human with basic moral principles… I definitely had to intervene when something like this happened before my eyes.

Then, as if controlling an arm, he used the cloud of black mist to block the young man's dagger.

At the same time, he discovered that those touched by his black mist seemed to be under his control.

—

While controlling the young man's body, he observed the cultists.

As for Alice standing to the side, he paid her no attention.

Her blond hair clearly indicated she wasn't a good student; dyeing her hair at such a young age, she'd regret it later if she went bald.

Hmm, a group of people dressed in rags, their emotions were tense and fearful.

He controlled the young man's actions as if familiarizing himself with a character, while curiously observing the group.

Although he didn't know why, he could sense their emotions.

Moreover, he could control the creatures touched by this black mist.

Thinking of this, Su Mu tried to control the young man to plunge the dagger into his left arm.

Under his control, the youth moved without hesitation, immediately plunging the dagger deep into his flesh. Blood soaked his outer garment, and the stench of blood filled the air.

Yes, the control was thorough; even so, there was no sign of resistance.

The cultists nearby felt a chill run down their spines as they watched the youth begin self-mutilation after being merely glanced at by those eyes.

The old man was the first to react, immediately kneeling and shouting, "Kneel before my Lord, God of Blood and Life, Lord Nemori Solka!"

He knew what that shattered space was.

It was a rift in the warp, and the warp was where those evil gods lived.

Although he didn't know why, even though these rituals were clearly fake, they had truly summoned the evil god Nemori Solka.

But now, immediately expressing submission was definitely the right thing to do.

The remaining cultists immediately knelt down as well, shouting in unison, "Kneel before our Lord, God of Blood and Life, Lord Nemori Solka!"

Su Mu's eyes fell on the group of cultists, somewhat puzzled by their chanting.

Are these guys speaking Chinese?

Could I have ended up in another world?

That seems plausible...

Just as Su Mu was struggling with how to communicate with them, the black mist seemed to understand his thoughts, and then all of it gathered around the young man.

Immediately afterwards, the major events in the young man's life flashed through Su Mu's mind.

Orphaned at 16, abandoned by relatives after dividing the inheritance, then met the High Priest, who helped him find a decent job, and three months ago, personally killed those relatives.

Su Mu blinked.

This black mist has this function?

Su Mu tried to speak.

Then a voice began to emanate from the black mist.

"Who are you?"

Advanced; he didn't even need to speak, just thought it.

The voice emanating from the black mist was emotionless, and indistinguishable in gender.

Even to the cultists, these voices couldn't be called language, yet they could understand their meaning.

He was curious.

The elderly man at the head of the group spoke tremulously, “We…we are your faithful followers, begging for your protection. For this, we offer up that pure maiden!”

“Great Lord, we spread your faith, yet we were expelled by the church and banished to this border forest, where we suffer at the hands of wild beasts. We beg you to protect your faithful followers!”

A lie.

Su Mu was certain this man wasn't telling the truth. This wasn't just what he sensed; he'd seen it through the young man's memories.

These cult members weren't banished for spreading their cult beliefs. Although he felt that even if they were, they deserved it—what era are we living in, still practicing human sacrifice? They should be grateful they weren't shot!

Even their presence here wasn't due to exile.

It was because this was their gathering place.

A cannibalistic dinner.

No embellishment, just the literal meaning of cannibalism.

These guys held a banquet in this forest every month.

They laughed and talked while devouring fresh human flesh.

In the young man's memory, this seemed to be related to some kind of ritual.

By continuously eating people, one could gain greater power.

This was the literal meaning.

That's right, this world wasn't Earth; supernatural powers truly existed.

The old man before him was someone who possessed supernatural power.

But the young man didn't know what his abilities were.

However, Su Mu didn't care.

From his perspective, this old man was no different from anyone else except for his slightly more noticeable aura.

They were all just ants.

“Ed Dulacka”

The old man trembled.

That was the old man's name.

Even though he hadn't said anything, He already knew his name!

Ed's mind began to race, and then he said in an ecstatic voice, “Yes, I am Ed Dulacka! Thank God for remembering my name; this is my blessing in this life!”

…

This old man was quite good at flattery.

But Su Mu didn't need an old man to flatter him.

But he wanted to tease him.

"The offering I made, was it delicious?"

This time, all the cultists began to tremble.

They understood what that meant.

It was a question about the "offering" from their previous gathering.

Ed, his head pressed against the ground, was drenched in sweat, his lips trembling, unable to utter a word.

What to do? What to do? What to do? What to do? What to do? What to do? What to do?!

Confess and beg for forgiveness?

How could that be? This evil god could never forgive a human who dared to touch its sacrifice.

There was only one way left…

While this evil god was still weak from its recent descent, drive it back to the warp!

Calm down.

Nemori Solkar wasn't powerful among the gods. He almost never descended to earth, and his power came from flesh and blood. The only flesh and blood he had obtained now was that young man's—and it was only blood.

Besides, judging from the extent of his black mist, he couldn't expand too far.

Ed's body gradually stabilized, and he secretly looked up at the shattered space.

Only the size of a fist, he couldn't muster much power; all he needed was to destroy that eye…

Thinking this, Ed took a deep breath, then suddenly stood up. His hunched body rapidly expanded, thick fur sprouting from his skin.

His eyes turned blood red, his lips protruded, fangs grew from his mouth, and his fingers sharpened.

Then he let out a howl, like a wolf.

The instant the howl subsided, the cultists who had been kneeling and trembling underwent the same transformation.

Their bodies changed uncontrollably.

"What… what's happening? My body!"

The cultists screamed in terror, clearly unprepared for this.

This was Ed's extraordinary ability; he could enhance his physical body by transforming into a werewolf. His enhanced body could even withstand rifle fire and tear a person apart with a single claw.

Furthermore, he could influence those who had come into contact with his blood through his unique howl.

The cultists here all drank a few drops of his blood under his watchful eye when they first joined the "gathering."

He hadn't told anyone about this ability.

Now it was time for these slaves to make their due contribution.

"Tear that eye apart!"

At Edward's command, the werewolves charged at Su Mu's eye like madmen.

They didn't stop even upon contact with the black mist.

Ed, naturally, didn't rush in; he remained hidden at a distance, watching everything.

He breathed a sigh of relief after seeing the mad werewolves emerge unscathed from the black mist.

Sure enough, Nemori Solka, who had never devoured flesh and blood, was no match for a group of superhumans. Even if these superhumans were merely inferior creations he had artificially created, the remaining spirituality within them could still resist the black mist's corrosive influence!

Thinking this, Edward suddenly paused.

Wasn't Nemori Solka's defining characteristic a combination of rotting, flexible branches and the ability to devour flesh and blood?

What are these black mists...?



Chapter 3: Trying to Escape?


Alice felt a profound despair.

Initially, after discovering the black mist had protected her, driven the young man who had intended to kill her into a frenzied state, and witnessed the cultists' humility towards the mist, she thought she was saved.

She even felt an impulse to become a follower of this god named Nemori Solka.

But when she saw Ed suddenly rise and instantly transform the other cultists into werewolves, her heart skipped a beat.

It's alright, Lord Nemori Solka's black mist can even block a dagger…

Just as Alice was comforting herself with this thought, she saw the black mist utterly defenseless against the werewolves' onslaught.

It was dispersed as easily as ordinary fog.

It was over.

Alice's last shred of hope was shattered by the reality before her.

The black mist couldn't stop the werewolves' attack, and that floating eye certainly couldn't survive being surrounded by so many werewolves.

After the eye dies, it will be her turn…

That eye had been staring at her without any change since the beginning.

Alice wanted to speak to the eye, to warn it of the crowd behind it.

But all she could manage was a muffled whimper.

"Turn around! The werewolves are charging!"

"Danger!"

—

Something's wrong."

"This isn't right."

"Nemori Solka is an evil god who controls rotting roots."

"His characteristic is that he lies buried underground, suddenly sprouting roots to trap his prey and drain the life force from their blood and flesh."

"In all the secret historical texts he knew, there was no description of him being surrounded by black mist!"

"There's only one possibility."

He wasn't Nemori Solka at all, but a god he didn't know!

Then he turned his gaze to the youth he had chosen.

That youth also drank my blood, he should also…

Ed's heart sank.

He saw the youth raising his left arm, looking around curiously.

The youth was also transforming into a werewolf.

But his transformation was limited to only one arm.

Even under Ed's watchful gaze, that arm actually reverted back to its original form!

This was all beyond his comprehension.

His accelerated maturation was irreversible; once it began, these people would only become monsters.

But that youth could abruptly interrupt this maturation and even revert to his original state!

Thinking this, Ed didn't hesitate for a moment, immediately turning back and running like a madman.

His powerful physique offered him no sense of security.

He didn't know what kind of god this was; he only knew he had to run.

Even if running might be futile, he dared not stop.

He was gambling.

Gambling that this god, like other gods, couldn't remain in the mortal realm for long.

However, the instant he turned to flee,

the howling werewolves behind him suddenly fell silent.

The world seemed to have been muted; Ed could hear nothing but his own heartbeat and panting.

The eerie silence set off alarm bells in Ed's mind. He glanced back as he fled.

Then he witnessed a sight he would never forget.

The werewolves who had charged towards Eyes had stopped, halting a step away from Eyes.

They all remained in their initial charging stance, some even falling to the ground, their forward-leaning center of gravity shifting, lacking support after stopping.

They were still in their charging motion when they collapsed.

Even so, the werewolves remained motionless, like… lifelike statues being toppled.

Through those “statues,” Ed could clearly see that one eye was still fixed on him.

The eerie silence didn't last long.

At the same time, the werewolves turned their heads in unison, locking their gaze on Ed.

The werewolves' bodies didn't move; their heads were turned 180 degrees to look directly at him.

Ed's breath caught in his throat. He instantly wanted to kneel down and beg for mercy, but a powerful will to survive made him continue to drag his trembling legs and run for his life.

Behind him, he heard a dense thud of footsteps.

He knew it was the werewolves.

Those superhumans he had accelerated were now under the control of this god.

And their target was him.

Ed's breathing quickened; he dared not look back, fearing that a turn would slow him down and allow the werewolves to catch up.

Calm down, calm down.

These werewolves were merely pseudo-superhumans he had accelerated with his abilities; they lacked the power and physical strength of a normal ninth-tier superhuman.

Their speed couldn't possibly surpass that of someone who had already reached the ninth tier halfway through his life.

And the fact that the eye had chosen to send these werewolves to hunt him proved his suspicions were correct.

This unknown god, having just descended, couldn't stray too far from the warp rift.

As long as he kept running like this...

He could survive!

Thump!

Just as Ed was declaring his survival, something struck his back, and he fell to the ground.

He turned his head in disbelief and found that the werewolves, who had previously been far too slow for him, were now about 10 meters behind him.

The impact he had just felt on his back was from one of the werewolves throwing its companion at him!

The werewolf remained in the throwing motion, while the others quickly formed a circle around him.

The werewolf retracted its throwing right hand, but in the process, its arm completely severed!

Ed watched in horror as the werewolf calmly picked up its right hand, shook it, and then casually tossed it aside.

It's over…

---

Su Mu's black mist remained stationary.

Ed was right; Su Mu indeed couldn't stray too far from the rift.

Although he tried to move the black mist further away, he couldn't.

Once the black mist moved too far from the rift, it would be pushed back as if rejected by the world, or simply dissipate.

But he wasn't completely trapped here.

He discovered that by allowing the black mist to enter the bodies of those cultists, he could move freely and escape undetected.

It's like wearing the enemy's clothes in a stealth game and becoming undetectable.

In short, he achieved the ability to move freely in this world by merging the black mist into the bodies of those cultists.

The only problem is that these cultists' physical abilities are rather poor.

While controlling the werewolves to chase Ed, he realized that if he didn't accelerate them, they would never catch up to Ed.

However, if he used the black mist to strengthen them, their bodies would suffer wear and tear due to the strain.

The werewolf with the severed arm was a prime example.

The black mist had strengthened his arm, allowing him to exert about three times his original strength, but the arm would soon be completely ruined due to muscle tears and broken bones.

In short, a three-fold enhancement made him a one-time consumable, and a two-fold enhancement would essentially halve his lifespan.

Su Mu silently calculated the benefits and costs of the black mist.



Chapter 4 Extraordinary Path


Alice felt that her experiences today were more thrilling than anything she had ever experienced in her life.

First, she was captured by cultists during the day, and then nearly sacrificed at night.

Then she was rescued by a mysterious black mist.

Next, she was almost eaten by werewolves.

Now she watched those werewolves drag back the werewolf they called the High Priest.

Am I still dreaming?

Haha.

"What are you thinking about?"

The black mist trembled slightly, and the words appeared in Alice's mind.

Alice instantly snapped back to reality, glanced at the eye that had been staring at her, then fearfully averted her gaze, shaking her head frantically, trying to say that she wasn't thinking anything.

Su Mu shifted his attention away from her.

Right now, the only one in this place still showing emotional fluctuations, besides Edward, was the girl in front of him, and Su Mu could clearly sense it.

But he wasn't going to untie the girl now.

Although he was probably, possibly, likely, in his past life he was a good person, he didn't want to just release the girl directly.

He knew this world wasn't simple.

Although he didn't know if God truly existed, he estimated he probably did.

If the girl went back and told the church people, he might be immediately expelled by a group of priests with holy water.

He didn't want to go back to that dark, sunless space to be imprisoned.

However, necessary communication was still required.

Su Mu manipulated the black mist to approach Alice, intending to remove the gag from her mouth.

Alice watched the approaching black mist, and thinking of the cultists who had been touched by it, tears welled up in her eyes.

She shook her head desperately, looking pitifully into those red eyes.

Alice knew very well that without the help of this evil god called Nemori Solka, she would have died long ago.

But he was an evil god!

The nuns of the church had said countless times that evil gods were unpredictable, and they absolutely couldn't let their guard down just because they had casually saved her.

Because they might inflict unbearable pain on humans at any moment, simply for fun!

"Why are you crying? It makes me sound like a bad person."

The girl's pitiful whimpers immediately stopped, afraid of displeasing Su Mu.

But in her heart, she still subconsciously thought:

Aren't evil gods bad people?

"If you slander me again, I'll turn you into an idiot."

"Waaah!"

Her inner thoughts were heard?!

Realizing this, Alice instantly cleared her mind, forcing herself to recall past events to avoid further inappropriate thoughts and angering Su Mu.

The black mist quickly reached her lips and entered her mouth.

Alice expected that, as the nuns had said, she would gag from the pain.

But unexpectedly, the black mist didn't cause her any discomfort.

The black mist, after enveloping the gauze in her mouth, was pulled out.

The gauze was still covered in her saliva.

"Cough, cough cough."

Alice coughed a few times immediately after the cloth was pulled out.

Having her mouth open for so long was too uncomfortable; now she was finally free.

Su Mu politely waited until she had recovered a little before controlling the black mist to speak: "Name."

"Cough, Alice! Alice Lidow!"

Alice didn't dare to be negligent and immediately used the most fluent Plitvice she had ever used to say her name.

"Hmm, Alice...can you dream of Wonderland?"

?

Dreaming of... Wonderland...?

Sensing the girl's confusion, Su Mu sighed inwardly.

Okay, it seems this isn't that Alice, but this appearance does fit his stereotype.

So, her hair color is natural?

Judging from other people's memories, the hair colors of people in this world are indeed quite diverse.

Why did I suddenly think of this?

Su Mu thought carefully for a moment, and seemed to remember something.

However, it was all too long ago, and it would be troublesome to dig through his memories.

So Su Mu chose to ignore it.

"Now we should get down to business, Alice."

"Yes, yes!"

When Alice heard that Su Mu wanted to talk to her about business, she immediately tensed up, wriggling up from the ground with a serious expression.

"As your savior, it's reasonable for me to make a request, isn't it?"

"Y-reasonable!"

You're an evil god, do you think I wouldn't listen to you?

"I'm not a bad person. I only have one request: keep what happened today a secret. If anyone else discovers my existence..."

Su Mu didn't finish speaking, but Alice already understood his meaning.

If anyone tells, I'm dead!

"Yes! I didn't see anything today. I just accidentally got lost in the forest!"

Su Mu was satisfied with Alice's pragmatism. He controlled the black mist to envelop the rope binding the girl and then turned it to ash.

Alice regained her freedom and immediately reached out to touch her body.

Completely unharmed, she hadn't suffered any damage from contact with the black mist.

The werewolves had already dragged Ed back.

And Ed stopped struggling. The moment he saw her eyes, he immediately began to beg for mercy.

"Great..."

Ed hesitated, unsure what kind of god this was.

But he quickly recovered.

"Great One God, please forgive my ignorance and foolishness, for attempting to escape your punishment! But...but I am still useful! I have money! I can help you understand the affairs of the present world. I also have remnants of items containing the spirit of the 'Cup' in my home; I can give you everything you need!"

Regardless of what kind of god it was, bestowing upon it the title of One God was certainly a safe bet.

In the church's records, the ancient wars of the gods were a struggle among the gods for the position of One God.

Su Mu was silent for a moment, then said, "Even if you die, aren't you mine?"

………………

…………

……

Seems...to make sense?

"My lord! I still have..."

Before Ed could finish speaking, the black mist completely enveloped him.

And the moment Ed was surrounded, his voice vanished.

A few seconds later, the black mist dissipated, leaving only a werewolf with lifeless eyes, kneeling in a devout posture.

Alice crouched in place, trying to minimize her presence; the scene before her made her legs weak with fear.

But He hadn't said she could leave, and Alice dared not move.

Besides, that eye was still fixed on her.

That emotionless gaze sent chills down her spine.

Su Mu paid no attention to Alice beside him, currently organizing the information he had gleaned from Ed's memories.

From Ed's memories, the most important thing he learned was that Ed was truly a beast.

From the age of 20, after discovering a book on advancement to superhuman status in the black market, he has been cannibalizing people.

Even now, in his fifties, he consumes at least one person every month without fail.

A true beast.

According to the book, there is a way to gain superhuman strength and regenerative abilities far exceeding human limits by cannibalism.

The book Ed bought on the black market appears on the surface to be nothing more than a sensationalist work.

But it actually teaches readers how to extract and absorb the spirituality of a human body through rituals.

Simply by transforming flesh and blood through rituals and then consuming them, one can accumulate a kind of spirituality called "the Cup" through the flesh and blood of the human body.

Once enough spirituality is accumulated, one can become a superhuman.

Su Mu is currently unsure whether cannibalism is the only path to superhuman status in this world.

However, based on the memories of these cultists, this world seems to be in the midst of Earth's Second Industrial Revolution, and the social atmosphere is similar to that of America at that time.

This method of entering the superhuman realm is definitely not the mainstream of society.

However, Su Mu doesn't need to consider these things. He is already a transcendent being, or rather, he is already an evil...good god!



Chapter 5 Protection Fees


And then there's Ed, who lied to himself.

This guy has no money at all!

He spent it all on drugs!

Sigh… isn't this Prit just another world version of America?!

The good news is that he does have some things related to the "Cup" in his house.

Ed hasn't just been eating people for decades; he's also been studying knowledge related to the supernatural.

For example, the "Cup" represents the spirituality of desire and senses. Generally, only living beings possess this spirituality, and humans are the most prominent among them.

Also, the history of this world is incomplete. The Church's records begin five thousand years ago, but there are no records of anything before that—no dinosaurs or anything like that.

However, according to a secret history book Ed obtained, there was a long, recorded history related to humans five thousand years ago, but it remains unknown.

Ed also kept several fingers rich in the spirituality of the Cup in his safe.

These things, of course, weren't something Ed could create himself; he bought them through a secret gathering.

And it's extremely expensive.

One costs 500,000!

How dare that old man spend my money like this!

Su Mu grew angrier the more he thought about it, and suddenly noticed Alice, who was still squatting nearby, peeking at him.

"What are you still doing? What time is it? Go home and sleep."

"Y-yes!"

Alice immediately stood up and bowed 90 degrees, then turned to leave quickly, but stopped after taking only two steps.

She cautiously turned back to look at Su Mu.

"Um, I was kidnapped, I don't know the way..."

...

Su Mu rolled his eyes at her, somewhat speechless.

She was rolling her eyes at him!

He was about to be killed!

Su Mu controlled a werewolf to approach Alice.

"Follow it."

"Okay, okay!"

---

After seeing Alice off, Su Mu also needed to confirm his next plan.

First, let's summarize the information we have so far.

I don't know why I was released from that place, which I think is called the subspace.

But my true body hasn't fully emerged; I'm currently only using a projection, and I can't stray far.

However, the creature I control can explore freely.

According to Ed's memories, if I want the projection to have a wider range of movement, I must contact the supernatural and prepare the necessary ritual items.

But a new problem arises.

Specific rituals can summon specific deities and extend their lifespan.

But Ed doesn't have any memories of a deity with similar characteristics to himself.

Oh well, maybe Ed's skills are lacking and he hasn't heard of me. I'll use Ed's body to go to that gathering and inquire.

—

The edge of the forest.

Alice stood on the path leading to the town, somewhat forlorn.

I'm still alive.

She glanced back; the werewolf had already disappeared into the depths of the forest.

She silently clasped her hands to her chest and prayed devoutly.

“Thank you, Holy Mother… no.”

“Thank you, Nemor… no, that’s not right either.”

She remembered that Ed hadn’t called Eye Nemori Solka since he returned from his escape.

That meant Eye wasn’t the god Ed knew.

Then what should he call her?

“Never mind, thank you, Lord Red-Eyed Black Mist, for saving me.”

After thanking Su Mu, Alice ran towards home.

It was already so dark; it must be 10 PM.

Her little sister was probably still waiting for her!

Unbeknownst to the girl, as she ran, an imperceptible black mist was gathering around the back of her head.

—

Su Mu nodded in satisfaction, observing the scene transmitted through the black mist.

"It can monitor without straining the brain; useful."

"However, this little brat's naming skills need improvement. At least she should call me Lord of Chaos."

Su Mu knew very well that one should always be wary.

Therefore, he couldn't entrust his existence to a girl's promise.

Although, based on his emotional perception, Alice didn't seem to be plotting anything,

emotions are easily manipulated.

—

Hall Town.

Alice ran back to town; the streets were deserted.

Alice, panting, pushed open her front door.

Alice's house was inherited from her parents.

It was a two-story self-built house.

Her parents were firefighters who died during a mission rescuing a child from a fire.

At the time, she and her sister were both under 10 years old.

If it weren't for the fact that the child was a lawyer's child, and that lawyer, to repay their parents, helped them win a case against an insurance company, earning enough money for both of them to reach adulthood, she and her sister might have been scavenging for a living before they were even adults.

The living room light was still on.

A girl in a blue floral dress was curled up asleep on the sofa.

That was her sister, Lorina, 16 years old, her last remaining family member in this world.

The door opened softly, but Lorina, a light sleeper, rubbed her eyes and sat up from the sofa.

Upon seeing Alice at the door, she immediately called out happily, "Sister!"

Then, without even putting on her shoes, she ran barefoot to Alice and threw herself into her arms.

"Sister, where have you been today? Lorina hasn't seen you since this morning! I was so scared!"

Lorina's voice was trembling with tears.

Alice hugged her tenderly, gently patting her back.

"I'm sorry, sister wanted to go to the forest to collect some truffles this morning, but... but sister got lost, making Lorina worry. I'll never do it again."

She would never go to the forest again.

Lorina cried for a while and then calmed down. Perhaps exhausted from crying, she fell asleep again in Alice's arms.

...

The next morning.

There was a loud banging on the door.

Alice rubbed her eyes and went to open the door.

"Coming...who..."

Alice snapped back to reality upon seeing who it was and tried to slam the door shut.

But the newcomer was faster, grabbing the doorframe.

"Why the rush, Miss Alice? We're old friends."

The newcomer was a tall, imposing young man.

He wore a suit and looked like a successful businessman, but his physique betrayed an undeniable thuggishness.

"Ryan, we've already paid this month's protection money."

Ryan was a notorious gangster in town.

In his younger days, he was a good-for-nothing, but his strength and ruthlessness caught the eye of the gang, and now he was exploiting the townspeople.

This is a small town, far from the big cities. Most of the young people have gone to work in the big cities, leaving only the elderly, women, and children, who are helpless against the gangs.

Even the town's police station has ties with the gangs.

“Protection money… I just paid it last week…”

“That was when you were a minor. Now you're an adult, so the fee is naturally higher.”

Ryan held up two fingers.

“Twenty thousand.”

“Twenty thousand! Why don't you just rob someone!”

Ryan ignored Alice's flushed face and continued smiling, “Alice, you can't say that. You know, this world isn't safe. Someone as pretty as you is easy to target.”

Ryan then looked meaningfully into the house.

“And your sister, you know, the big shots in town really like your sister’s type.”

Alice glared at Ryan through gritted teeth, but couldn't do anything about it.

“Give me another week…”

“Three days, it’s a deal. I’ll come collect it on Saturday.”

Ryan withdrew his hand and awkwardly imitated a gentleman’s bow.

“Happy birthday, Miss Alice.”

After seeing Ryan off, Alice crouched alone behind the door.

She felt like crying, but now was not the time to cry…



Chapter 6 A Second Visit, The First Believer


Twenty thousand yuan! She only earns 500 yuan a month as a waitress in a restaurant in town…

She has to borrow it, but where can she borrow so much…?

The grandparents in town definitely can't afford that much money, and even if they could, they wouldn't lend it to a stranger like her.

Contact the lawyer?

That won't work either. He's in a big city, and she's just a village girl without a phone. How can she contact him?

Have the postman deliver a letter? She doesn't have the lawyer's address…

What to do?

Alice isn't an naive girl; she knows the methods of those gangs very well.

But she doesn't have the ability to escape with her sister…

As the girl's despair deepened, the black halo behind her head began to stir.

…

Gangs still exist in the 21st century? You're either America or the British Empire.

Damn it, you've sent me to the latrine!

Wait, something's not right. According to this world, it seems to be the early 20th century?

Thinking about it, it might even be a parallel universe's America, which seems quite plausible?

Just as Su Mu was making these thoughts, some dark ideas also began to form in Alice's mind.

Maybe… she should ask Him to save her?

—

Meanwhile, Ryan returned to the gang after leaving.

He was in a great mood.

Even the blood-red, skinned human heads hanging outside their gang's hideout, which he usually found disgusting, didn't seem so repulsive anymore…

Okay, they were still pretty disgusting.

But it wasn't because of Ryan's sympathy or a change of heart.

It was simply because they smelled awful.

"The boss is really strange. Why hang this thing outside his own door? Shouldn't it be hanging outside the doors of those who owe the gang money?"

Ryan pinched his nose and walked into the gang.

Alice couldn't possibly come up with the 20,000.

And his goal wasn't the money, but Alice herself.

Their boss had said that he could use that woman after the deed was done.

A lewd smile crept across Ryan's face at the thought.

He'd been to big cities with his boss, but even there, few women were more beautiful than Alice.

And her sister was a beauty too…

—

Su Mu couldn't be a spy all the time; he had his own things to do.

For example, he was currently controlling Ed, bringing him back to his home.

Although Ed had spent almost all his wealth on drugs, his car and house were still intact. He wasn't from Hall Town; he'd specifically come from the city.

Because the city was quite strict.

At this moment, under Su Mu's control, Ed had returned to human form and was examining the three fingers imbued with the spirit of the cup.

Mainly, Su Mu was observing.

Aside from a slight red glow, there was nothing special about them.

That was his first impression; seemingly, in his subconscious, such things were as worthless as roadside stones.

Forget it, if it's useless, I'll just sell it at the gathering.

Right now, Su Mu wants to find a way to allow the black mist more freedom of movement. Right now, he's like an undocumented immigrant, his actions restricted everywhere.

As a god, I should at least wave my hand and cover the world with black mist!

Although doing so is a bit like that conch shell that's also 30 million years old…

In short, she needed to find a way to get a local ID card.

---

Hall Town, noon.

After lunch, Alice told Lorina to stay home obediently while she returned to the forest, guided by some memories.

Walking through the quiet forest, Alice's hands trembled slightly.

Alice carried nothing in her hands; she wanted to exchange herself.

The unknown god had saved her, hadn't taken her heart or soul, and seemed quite approachable.

She remembered the nuns who had come to town to preach saying that, apart from the "Three Saints"—the Virgin Mary, the Holy Light, and the Holy Son—all other gods were rejected by the world.

Therefore, they would try every means to induce humans to offer their souls and faith so that they could invade the world through this evil method.

Alice hadn't believed in these things before, but after yesterday's events, her worldview had been reshaped.

She had considered seeking help from the church, but it was too far; she couldn't afford the round-trip fare.

She hoped He wouldn't harm her own sister.

He probably wouldn't, He even spared me…

Just as she was lost in thought, a tall figure appeared before her.

It was a werewolf.

Alice trembled, but still mustered her courage and said, "Um, I want to ask you for help. I'm willing to offer my soul!"

The werewolf didn't speak, but turned its head behind it, gesturing for her to continue.

Although the werewolf's 180-degree turn made Alice feel nauseous, she didn't care, thinking this might be her only chance.

—

Su Mu controlled the black mist, arranging it in various shapes beside the spatial rift.

At the same time, he controlled two werewolves playing Go with stones and branches.

He had a better understanding of Alice's purpose in coming.

She probably couldn't find a way to collect the protection money and wanted to use his power to help her.

Considering the existence of extraordinary powers in this world, the girl's offer of her soul in exchange seemed quite sincere.

When Alice returned to the clearing deep in the forest, she was speechless with shock at the bizarre scene before her.

Two werewolves sat cross-legged, seemingly playing chess.

Their eyes remained fixed on her, while the black mist "painted" in the air like paint.

Besides normal cats and dogs, there were also some unknown creatures with short forelimbs and thick hind limbs, appearing to walk upright.

Is this how an evil god spends his leisure time?

Why does He seem different from the evil gods the church speaks of?

No, that's not the point.

Shaking off these somewhat disrespectful thoughts, Alice was about to speak when someone beat her to it.

"Do you want to deal with Ryan, or deal with the gang?"

So it really is mind reading, how terrifying… such a powerful ability!

Speaking of which, he immediately said he wanted to deal with the gang, as expected of the evil… Red-eyed Black Mist Lord!

Alice took a deep breath and knelt down, pressing her head to the ground.

"I...I don't ask that you solve everything for me. I only hope that you can protect my sister, Lorrina Lidauer, and ensure she lives a healthy life until...30 years old."

"For this, I am willing to offer you my body, soul, and faith as your extension, your vessel for walking in this world."

!

Su Mu immediately locked onto the keywords:

Extending, traversing the world.

Could it be that as long as I gain a soul and faith, my range of movement can expand?

Su Mu could hardly contain his excitement.

“I accept your deal.”

Alice's body relaxed.

At the very moment Su Mu agreed, he could clearly feel the girl's spirit instantly transported to the space he had previously resided in.

He curiously poked the humanoid light source emitting a faint blue glow in the space.

Then Alice suddenly let out a scream, immediately covering her mouth.

What happened? I felt like someone touched my head, it hurts so much…

Is this the price of offering my soul…?

Alice was still lost in thought when Su Mu withdrew his hand.

Although there was no evidence.

But Su Mu was certain that the girl's sincerity was truly immense.

Simply guaranteeing her sister's life until 30 had earned him an absolutely devout believer.

As long as that soul exists, he's like a cheat code for Alice, a human being. He can erase her emotions or add non-existent ones if he wants.

Furthermore, he can send a portion of his black mist into the world at any time through the girl's body.

In other words, he now has a mobile base in the mortal world. If the Church were to destroy his forest home with holy water,

he could use Alice to make a comeback.

However, Su Mu could clearly feel that the amount of black mist he could release through Alice was quite limited, and the effect of the black mist was also diminishing.

He felt that if he encountered a superhuman like Edward, he might not be able to control it so easily due to insufficient strength.

………………

…………

……

Oh no, am I really an evil god?!



Chapter 7 The Gang's Purpose


Thinking about his ability to always end up at the villain's table in every story, Su Mu became even more certain that he wasn't a good person...

No, even though my ability hasn't changed, at least I'm upright. I am a righteous god!

Pulling aside his chaotic thoughts, Su Mu turned his attention back to Alice.

"Get up. You can go directly to that gangster now. If you're worried about things getting out of hand and affecting your daily life, I suggest you go tonight."

That's it?

But didn't the nuns say that once you offer your soul, you'll disappear, your body possessed by an evil god?

Why does it feel the same as when I arrived?

Alice was puzzled, but she didn't dare ask further.

"Yes, um, may I know your title?"

"Lord of the Mist."

"Thank you, esteemed Lord of the Mist..."

Alice returned to Hall Town with a mix of excitement and trepidation.

—

At night, Alice crouched alone behind a trash can across the street from the gang's territory, nervously taking deep breaths.

Suddenly, Su Mu's voice echoed in her mind.

"Just go in, I'll handle it."

Alice was startled, then realized this was probably due to her offering her soul.

Now Su Mu could talk to her at any time.

Meanwhile, at the entrance to the bar compound occupied by the gang, two guards noticed Alice lurking suspiciously behind the trash can.

The guards immediately drew their pistols from their waists.

"Who's there! Come out, or I'll shoot!"

Discovered, Alice could no longer hesitate and obediently raised her hands and went out.

The guards approached, guns raised, and after recognizing Alice's face, they paused, then revealed a lewd smile.

"Hey, isn't this Alice? Came to us now? Got enough money?"

One of the guards, holstering his pistol, reached out to touch Alice's face.

"Or planning to make some 'extra cash'?"

Before he even got within a meter of Alice, the previously invisible black mist ring behind her head suddenly spun and became visible.

Then, a cloud of black mist instantly enveloped the guard.

"What's going on?! What is that?!"

The other guard, startled by the sudden turn of events, immediately drew his pistol from his waist, ready to fire, but completely lost consciousness before he could.

Hmm, loan sharking, forcing women into prostitution…

Are there any good people left here?

Su Mu quickly reviewed the guard's memories and couldn't help but sigh.

This is truly dark…

—

Inside the bar, in the basement office.

A bald man in his forties, wearing sunglasses and a sharp suit, sat on a leather sofa.

He swirled a wine glass in his left hand, his right hand gripping a small booklet, engrossed in reading it.

The booklet's cover read, "The Complete Guide to 300 Torture Methods."

The title sounded like a children's book, but it taught methods of torture that were incredibly bloody and violent.

These included, but were not limited to, dripping wax into someone's eyes, tearing off a person's face while they were still alive, and filling a person's stomach with sand.

It was every kind of perverted method imaginable.

But the man wasn't reading this because he was a pervert... well, actually, a little.

His extraordinary path was related to this.

His path to transcendence is through the "Cup," and he's currently a ninth-tier [Bartender].

His ability is to hypnotize targets with the "liquor" he concocts, while also enhancing his own physical functions and accelerating injury recovery.

He built his gang using this ability.

However, he's now hit a bottleneck.

Transcendent individuals need to completely digest the spiritual essence of potions to advance.

The fastest way to digest potions is to engage in activities aligned with their chosen path.

His [Bartender] skill requires him to frequently mix drinks to digest the spiritual essence; the higher the spiritual essence content of the ingredients, the better the drink's effect and the faster the digestion process.

But recently, the city has been cracking down, making it nearly impossible for him to obtain high-quality ingredients. Not only are he unable to obtain ingredients, but he's also afraid to show his face in the city lately.

Therefore, he desperately needs to acquire an ingredient rich in the spirit of the Cup.

And his target is Alice.

He noticed the girl a long time ago.

She was strong; even as a minor, she quickly recovered from the death of her parents.

Moreover, she was pure, without malice, and kind to everyone.

Such a girl's blood must be rich in spirit.

If her blood could be collected when she was in despair, the spirit within it would become even purer due to the intense emotional shift.

Therefore, he sent someone to make her a request she could never fulfill.

And she would surely come begging for mercy in despair.

But the despair he desired went beyond that.

He would give her hope, telling her that if she could endure his execution, he would spare her and give her a large sum of money.

She loved her sister dearly, so by using her sister as a trigger, he could surely keep her hope alive.

And he would congratulate her and give her the money at the final stage of the execution.

Alice would undoubtedly want to go home immediately after receiving the money, but when she returned home, that would be the moment of true despair.

He would have his men assault Alice's sister at her home during her execution, and then kill her sister in front of her upon her return.

Witnessing this, Alice would instantly lose the will to live, utterly despairing. The Cup Spirit within her blood would become even purer due to this extreme and complete sensory transformation!

If he could use such ingredients to make a drink, his potion-digesting speed would definitely increase dramatically!

Thinking of this perfect ritual plan, the man's smile grew even wider.

He elegantly sipped his glass of red wine.

The future looked bright.

Meanwhile, inside the bar, a group of people were completely drunk.

The door was pushed open, and the guards controlled by Su Mu entered.

Several gang members who weren't yet drunk noticed Su Mu.

One of them, Ryan, said somewhat displeasedly, "Didn't I tell you to guard outside?"

The guard didn't speak, but behind him followed a somewhat nervous Alice.

Lord of the Mist, do I really have to come too?

Just watch. If you're scared, you don't have to watch.

Um, these gang members all have guns, is that alright?

Alice felt she should remind Su Mu that, in her view, these evil...good gods were ancient beings from a bygone era, and they probably had no concept of firearms.

While she didn't think firearms could harm them, what if they did?

It was said that gunpowder could subdue demons and monsters.

Some storybooks even described how a small sailboat could ram a newly descended evil god back. What if the gunpowder dispersed this black mist?

Just as Alice was about to say this to Su Mu in her mind, Ryan noticed her.

The man paused for a moment, then chuckled and teased, "Miss Alice, have you saved up enough money?"

Hearing this, the other gang members around her looked at her and chuckled.

"Perhaps she knows she can't come up with that much money to plead for mercy!"

"Haha, Alice, why don't you come over and give me a shoulder massage? If you feel good, I'll give you 1000, how about that?"

The crowd roared with laughter, completely ignoring what Alice wanted to do.

What could a little girl do?

She couldn't just suddenly pull out a gun, could she? Even if she did, it wouldn't matter; there were over 50 of them.

Just as everyone was joking around, thick black mist poured out from the ring behind Alice's head.

In an instant, it filled the entire bar.

The men who had been nonchalantly telling dirty jokes suddenly became alert.

"Damn! What is that?!"



Chapter 8 The solution to a problem is to solve the person who raised it.


A thick black fog obscured everyone's vision.

No one could see anything but the fog.

The entire bar descended into chaos, filled with the sounds of shattering bottles and the cacophony of curses.

Some even pulled out handguns and began firing wildly.

But strangely, the bullets, upon hitting the fog, seemed to have their kinetic energy absorbed, flying less than half a meter before falling to the ground.

Ryan panicked.

No matter how many shots he fired, he could only watch helplessly as the bullets and casings hit the ground.

"Damn it, what is this black fog? I can't see anything!"

"Damn it, the bullets won't penetrate!"

"A ghost! Holy Mother, save me!"

He reacted quickly, stopping his pointless firing after confirming the bullets were useless, and sprinted towards the door he remembered.

As for the others, they didn't care about anything else now.

The bar wasn't large, and during his escape, his obstructed view caused him to bump into others, but being stronger, he was largely unaffected and quickly reached the entrance.

The bar's entrance was the only area not shrouded in black mist.

To ensure Alice's visibility and to see if he could extract a few more followers from among these people, Su Mu had deliberately left an open space.

When Ryan rushed over and saw Alice and the guards, his eyes immediately turned fierce as he glared at Alice.

Although he didn't know what had happened, Ryan was certain it was Alice's doing; the black mist had originated from the back of her head.

He immediately drew his gun, aimed at Alice, and fired two shots without hesitation.

Shooting in the black mist was useless; now that it's gone, I don't believe you're unharmed!

Ryan didn't believe anything in this world was immune to bullets, especially not a frail woman.

"I don't know what the hell this is, but die!"

However, the next second, the guard beside him actually raised his hand and blocked the bullet!

The bullet, which should have carried powerful kinetic energy, piercing through the guard's hand and striking Alice's forehead, was stopped by his hand and couldn't advance an inch.

How could this be!

Unconvinced, Ryan fired several more shots at the guard's head.

This time, the bullet easily passed through his forehead and struck the door behind him.

But something even more terrifying happened.

The guard, with three bullet holes in his head, steadily approached him!

The bizarre scene made Ryan's legs go weak, and he collapsed to the ground.

"Monster! Don't come any closer!"

Ryan's hands trembled as he gripped his pistol and fired several shots, but fear caused all the bullets to miss.

As the guard reached him, he mechanically kept pulling the trigger, completely unaware that he had run out of ammo.

The guard crouched down, his lifeless eyes calmly fixed on him.

Then, a completely emotionless voice echoed from the black mist.

"Offer your soul, or die."

Lyan was terrified and immediately cried out, "Offer! I'm willing to offer my soul! Don't kill me!"

Su Mu was satisfied and looked expectantly into the subspace.

...

?

Playing me?

Why is there nothing here?

Su Mu was a little angry and was about to teach Ryan a lesson when he realized something was wrong.

Judging from his demeanor, he wasn't lying at all.

In other words, Ryan was genuinely willing to offer himself, but Su Mu couldn't accept it.

After pondering for a while, Su Mu considered a possibility.

Forceful intimidation wouldn't work; it had to be voluntary.

Why was there a limit to the strength of this offer?!

Su Mu was furious. He had originally planned to recruit them all as his followers and then disperse them, easily gaining control of a city's territory.

But the standards for followers were surprisingly high.

Having confirmed that none of these people were worth recruiting, Su Mu lost interest and directly let the black mist enter their minds, completing the control.

The noisy hall instantly fell silent.

Su Mu wasn't worried about accidentally killing innocent people.

The black mist wasn't actually very aggressive; even if he controlled them, it wouldn't damage their nervous systems. Simply retracting the black mist would immediately restore them to their original state, and he possessed the ability to see their important memories.

Combining these two factors, he could simply retract the black mist if anything went wrong.

Unfortunately, she hadn't made a mistake yet.

Looking at the suddenly quiet hall, Alice had a new understanding of Su Mu's strength.

She had heard it; quite a few gang members had fired.

So the bullets were useless against the black mist.

Alice breathed a sigh of relief.

As expected, how could an evil god be injured by bullets? Stories are just stories.

Relaxing, Alice became somewhat afraid again.

"Um, Lord of the Mist, isn't this making a bit too much of a commotion?"

She was worried that if the Holy Light Church found out, Su Mu would definitely be banished back to the subspace.

"Don't worry, I've infiltrated the walls here with black mist; no sound can get out."

"Wow, amazing!"

The Lord of the Mist is practically omnipotent!

"These guys have pretty much money, huh? It's all yours."

As Su Mu finished speaking, all the gang members turned to Alice in unison and placed their money into her hands.

Alice hadn't even reacted before she was holding a wad of cash.

"W-This much, really for me?!"

Alice's voice trembled slightly, filled with barely concealed trepidation and excitement.

"I have no use for it, you and your sister can use it."

In Su Mu's eyes, this money was nothing more than worthless paper.

He was a god, what use was human currency to him? He'd rather use it to improve the favorability of his only current follower.

Although this follower was a bit slow, she was generous; she gave him a mobile base right off the bat.

Such a good backup base… ugh, this little follower can't go hungry.

"Buy more meat from now on, you've gotten so thin."

"Oh, thank you, sir!"

Alice looked at the banknotes in her hand, and hearing Su Mu's words, tears welled up in her eyes.

How long had it been since anyone cared about her since her parents passed away?

Her younger sister was still young and needed care, but wasn't she the same?

For so many years, she had been holding on, constantly pushing herself to the limit to provide a better life for her sister. Even when she couldn't hold on any longer, she only dared to hide alone in the bathroom at night, not even daring to cry too loudly, afraid of worrying her sister.

And now, someone has started to care about her.

Although the voice coming from the black mist was devoid of emotion, the concern in its words was genuine.

Ah, tears are streaming down!

The girl instinctively tried to wipe away her tears, but her hands were too full of cash to move.

Just as she was about to find a place to put the money down, a cloud of black mist brushed against her face.

The mist brushed against her face, washing away her tears.

Su Mu, sensing the overwhelming emotion and shyness in the girl's heart, felt somewhat confused.

Dude, even if you're rich now, there's no need to be so happy. There'll be plenty of times later when I'll take you out on shady business.



Chapter 9 [The Moth]


"Stop crying, their boss hasn't been dealt with yet."

Hearing Su Mu's reminder, Alice immediately became alert and began to observe her surroundings. She then noticed a closed wooden door behind the bar.

"Should I open it?"

Alice swallowed hard and volunteered.

"No need, just use F2 to charge in."

Alice blinked, somewhat puzzled.

F2? What's that?

While Alice was still wondering, she saw the gang members rush towards the wooden door and kick it open, then enter in unison.

Each person held a pistol in both hands, raised to the right of their head, as disciplined as an army that had fought for over a decade.

Behind the door was only a corridor, and at the end of the corridor was a staircase leading to the basement.

Su Mu guided the group down the stairs.

Just as Su Mu was about to lead the group down, a man walked towards them.

A bald man.

?

The bald man paused for a moment upon seeing the neatly arranged gang members, then asked in a low voice, "What are you doing here?"

"We've come to..."

?

Damn, I almost lost control.

Su Mu was somewhat surprised. His control over these people had loosened for a moment. It seemed he shouldn't have only stuffed a little black mist into their brains to save time; he should have filled them completely. But it wasn't a big problem.

This bald man was also a superhuman.

Su Mu instantly confirmed this.

However, perhaps due to using Alice's Descent, his perception was somewhat diminished, making it impossible to immediately determine a superhuman's identity.

Su Mu didn't intend to waste words with him.

He directly controlled the crowd to rush towards him.

The bald man reacted quickly, immediately shouting, "Stop!"

Su Mu's control was stopped again, albeit for only a moment, but the bald man immediately retreated to the bottom of the stairs, dodging the first wave of attacks.

After going downstairs, the bald man immediately locked the iron gate and pulled a bottle of liquor from his desk cabinet.

Forgetting all about elegance, he smashed the bottle against the corner of the desk.

A few drops of bright red liquid splattered onto the table after the bottle shattered. The bald man then took a swig from the bottle.

Bang!

A deep dent appeared in the sturdy iron gate!

How did he do that? Do I have anyone this powerful under my command?

“Listen up, those inside, surrender and you won’t be killed.”

Control doesn’t count as killing.

Su Mu controlled one of his men to punch the iron gate with a triple-strengthened fist, but only managed to create a dent. He then decided to outsmart the man.

Although he had dozens of men under his command, they were all important resources, and he cared a lot about the bald man.

These were high-caliber personnel; he couldn’t afford to damage them.

The bald man, however, didn’t believe a word from the outside.

Although he didn’t know why his men, who should have been under his tight control, had suddenly betrayed him, there was still good news.

Although his men could escape his control, he could still maintain it for a while.

Besides, he had just drunk the enhanced liquor; he could easily tank bullets and kill them all within the next hour.

But he couldn't accept the death of so many people at once.

These were people he had spent years training, and he still didn't know why they had suddenly betrayed him.

had they discovered the truth about being controlled by him?

Impossible. Even if he knew, they couldn't possibly escape his control.

Having ruled out the possibility of their individual betrayal, it could only mean someone helped them break free.

He couldn't sleep or eat without finding the mastermind.

A mysterious person who could break his control over the alcohol must also be a superhuman.

Although he didn't know their level, judging from their abilities, they were likely from the [Moth] lineage.

[Moths] excel at confusing and manipulating others. He, a [Cup] superhuman, was definitely no match for a [Moth], which explained why his control wasn't working.

A [Moth] superhuman must have used their abilities to control his subordinates without his knowledge.

Thinking this, the bald man spoke to the outside, "I know you're a [Moth]. If you want me to surrender, you'll have to show some sincerity. I want you to come see me in person."

If he could lure the person out, how could he, a [Cup], be no match for a [Moth] who might resort to underhanded tactics later?

Moreover, the staircase is very narrow; two people standing on a single step are practically unable to move.

A sound from outside has to be within three steps before it can reach in.

If I hear any unfamiliar voice, I'll just rush out.

Su Mu was silent for a moment.

What is a moth?

The code name of another superhuman?

Never mind, just have Alice come.

Su Mu directly instructed Alice to come downstairs in her head.

"Okay."

Alice didn't hesitate for a moment; she would do as Su Mu said.

She was now under the protection of the great Lord of the Mist; who could possibly harm her?

When Alice arrived at the door, the gang members in the staircase had already made way for her.

Alice was full of confidence, stepping down in her little leather shoes.

She felt no fear, but rather a curious look at the slightly gloomy staircase.

"So filthy, it's probably been a long time since anyone cleaned it."

Upon reaching the second flight of stairs, Alice noticed a dent the size of a bowl in the iron door facing the stairs.

Then she noticed two men blocking her path. One of them had his right hand pinned against the wall, seemingly numb, with large and small beads of blood seeping from his arm.

The smell of blood made Alice feel nauseous, but remembering that the Lord of the Mist had summoned her without assigning her any tasks, she couldn't afford to embarrass herself now!

After confirming Alice had calmed down, Su Mu mentally instructed her, "Now, speak to the person inside..."

Alice cleared her throat and then shouted, "I am [Moth], I have delivered my sincerity..."

Before she finished speaking, the iron door was smashed open with a bang.

Alice remained in the same position as when she spoke, the iron door now stopped right in front of her.

The man who had crippled his right hand was now crippled too, his left hand as well.

The iron gate was less than 10 centimeters from her eyes.

A gust of wind followed, making it hard for her to open her eyes. She instinctively looked down and rubbed them.

Just as she was caught off guard, a figure rushed out of the house—it was the bald man.

He glanced around, then his confidence surged.

"Great, that moth managed to get everyone else out of the way, the advantage is on my side!"

Then the bald man's target was Alice, his right fist already clenched and ready to strike.

But when he saw Alice, he couldn't help but freeze for a moment.

Alice?

How could this be… He'd fallen for a trap!

The bald man immediately realized that Alice was just bait, used to lure him out.

That despicable moth!

But the bald man couldn't care less about that now and immediately changed his strategy.

Kill Alice first, then escape. He couldn't worry about the mastermind behind it now; getting out of there was the priority.

The bald man threw a punch at Alice, but the punch missed.

His two henchmen, whom he hadn't taken seriously, were surprisingly fast enough to catch up with him. As he charged forward, they grabbed his shin and slammed him towards the door.

Alice, who had been rubbing her eyes, suddenly stopped, her body becoming uncontrollable. She immediately turned and ran towards the stairwell.

What's going on? What's wrong with my body?



Chapter 10 Character Creation


"Stop yelling, watch my micro-management."

Unable to bear Alice's fluctuating emotions, Su Mu explained to avoid her anxieties bothering him.

"So, the Lord of the Mist is controlling me now?"

"He shouldn't have noticed my legs were shaking just now, right?"

"I feel a little ashamed..."

Alice was torn. Under Su Mu's control, she quickly retreated into the bar. The other gang members weren't idle either; Su Mu dragged them all away, preparing to use a human wave tactic.

While Su Mu was completing these operations, in the underground office:

The bald man, thrown back into the room, looked utterly bewildered.

He hadn't expected anyone to keep up with his speed, especially after he had the initiative.

He looked at the subordinate who had grabbed his leg, only to find that the man's left arm had been completely severed and was lying on the ground.

The man, however, seemed unaffected, charging straight at him.

"A madman."

That was the bald man's only assessment.

The bald man's eyes hardened, and he rolled away to avoid the man's impact.

He stood up, drew his pistol, and was about to fire a few shots at the man's head when he saw the man slam his entire body against the wall, creating a dent.

He could even hear the sound of bones breaking; the entire dent was filled with the man's blood.

A madman! This man is a complete and utter madman!

Do I have such a ruthless person among my men?!

While he was stunned, the man moved again!

He took a few steps back, then slowly turned around.

His face was covered in blood, almost no patch of intact skin, his nose shattered and crookedly hanging from his face.

The bald man was terrified.

Not because of his monstrous face, but because of his eyes.

There was no fear in his eyes; it was as if he felt no pain, as if his injuries didn't exist.

Those eyes were empty and lifeless.

"Monster, don't come any closer!"

The bald man, unable to contain his fear any longer, fired several shots at the man until his magazine was empty.

The man had already fallen to the ground from the impact of the bullets, and just as the bald man felt a sense of relief,

the man on the ground moved.

He braced his head against the ground, arched his spine, and began to crawl towards the bald man.

The bald man felt a chill run down his spine. Forgetting all pretense of composure, he immediately turned and ran.

The guards Su Mu had assigned to watch the door immediately spread their arms to block the bald man's path.

"Get out of my way!"

The bald man roared and punched him in the head, knocking his head off in one blow.

His head slammed against the wall and then exploded from the impact.

His body fell to the ground.

Su Mu was also caught off guard by the bald man's actions.

Wow, that's some serious strength!

"I should have just used triple enhancement."

Watching the man with only double enhancement being kicked to death like a stray dog, Su Mu felt a pang of regret. He also gained an understanding of the limits of injury he could inflict on the person he controlled.

Because the black mist was directly attached to the head, if the brain were to completely detach from the body, Su Mu's control over the human body would be severed.

Although the bald man's strength surprised Su Mu, his troops were already assembled.

The bald man had just reached the middle of the stairs when people came down from above.

Even the gang members, one on each step, coordinated perfectly to block the bald man's path.

"I said, get out of my way!"

The bald man's control remained effective; the member at the front paused at his words.

But quickly recovered under Su Mu's control, throwing a punch at the bald man's face.

"You're asking for it!"

The bald man didn't dodge, returning the punch to his face.

Bang!

Bang!

The leader's head flew off, staining the wall even more vividly.

The bald man was also knocked back two steps by the punch.

Bruises appeared on his face, blood trickled from the corner of his mouth, and his ears rang, but he quickly recovered thanks to his enhanced regenerative abilities.

The bald man, somewhat stunned, wiped the blood from the corner of his mouth with his finger.

How could this be? A punch from an ordinary person could draw blood?

He glanced in horror at the headless corpse lying on the ground; its right arm, too, was bleeding profusely and completely severed.

It's happening again.

The hand of the monster writhing on the ground below is the same!

What's going on? Are they really controlled by [Moths]?

Before he could think further, the next person rushed forward.

"Stop!"

The bald man gritted his teeth and gave the order again.

This time, he planned to use that one second of pause to kill the man.

However, things didn't go as he wished.

In the instant the man was restrained, he saw what seemed like a cloud of black mist drift out from behind the man's head, then pull the man's head to the side, twisting it past his fist.

What is this now?!

The bald man felt completely numb; today's events were more thrilling than his entire life.

Before the bald man could recover, the other man broke free and punched him in the stomach.

Boom!

The bald man was knocked back into the room.

"Cough, cough cough!"

The bald man coughed up blood and bits of flesh.

The punch felt like his internal organs were dislocated.

He looked at the other man, horrified but relieved.

Thankfully, like the other two, this guy's hand was also broken.

There was still time. Even if he took two punches from each of them, he would recover completely under the effects of the enhanced alcohol.

Damn it, I'll never let you get away with this when I get out of here!

The bald man vowed in his heart that he would definitely get revenge.

Just as he was about to get up, the man who had been writhing earlier was right beside him, biting his shoulder.

—

Inside the bar.

Alice sat at the bar, looking nervously and curiously into the room behind it.

"Um, Lord of the Mist, what's going on in there? I think I heard someone shouting something..."

Before her, a cloud of black mist was shaping her into a human form.

"It's nothing, they're just greeting their new colleague."

After shaping the human form, the black mist stretched its body, then, as if remembering something, a pair of eyes sprouted from its face.

"Doesn't it look more human now?"

The blood-red eyes looked at Alice, devoid of any emotion.

"Yes...it looks even more like a human..."

It doesn't look human at all!

Su Mu ignored Alice's strong resistance; after all, how could this cloud of black mist possibly resemble a human? He simply felt he should find a way to remember his past life as a human.

However, this body still had many limitations.

The most important was that he couldn't leave Alice's body by more than 5 meters.

Just now on the stairs below, after he separated the black mist from that person's body, it quickly dissipated.

This also proved that he could only descend to the outside world through his followers; any attempt to forcibly control the outside world was thwarted in less than a second.

But the range of activity through his followers was too small.

Su Mu felt it was a difficult situation, but he quickly came to terms with it.

He had endured so many years; now that he could finally emerge, what more could he ask for?

He stood behind the bar, took out an unopened bottle of red wine, and asked, "Want a bottle?"



Chapter 11 Negotiation


"Then, thank you, Lord of the Mist..."

Alice wanted to refuse, after all, she dared not accept what the evil god offered, but thinking that Su Mu was such an easy-going evil god, maybe he really just wanted to invite her for a drink?

"Hmm... You've only just turned 18 and you're already drinking alcohol. You should drink milk instead; it's better for your health."

Su Mu put the wine glass back in its place and then took out a bottle of milk from the cabinet.

He took out a clean wine glass and poured a bottle for Alice.

"OK, thanks……"

Although Alice really wanted to complain about why adults aren't allowed to drink alcohol, she held back.

Lord of the Mist must have his reasons for saying that. By the way, why is there milk in a bar?

———

The bar was peaceful, and so was the basement.

The smell of blood filled the office that was originally filled with the aroma of alcohol.

The once pristine white tiles were now stained crimson with blood.

The bald man's face was also stained with blood.

He was standing in the center of the office at that moment.

His feet were surrounded by headless and handless corpses.

There wasn't even room to put your feet down.

The clothes on his body had lost their function of covering his body and hung on his shoulders like a tattered rag.

He was breathing heavily, his eyes bloodshot.

The physical wounds are healing quickly, but the mental exhaustion cannot be recovered.

"Now, it's finally resolved..."

The bald man glanced at the wall clock.

8:37

Thankfully, there are still 13 minutes left, plenty of time to escape Hall Town...

The bald man forced himself to cheer up and went up the steps to the bar.

He dared not let his guard down, for who knew if that mysterious superhuman might have another trick up his sleeve.

However, he wasn't too worried. Although he wasn't feeling well, his body was still in peak condition, and he didn't believe that the moth would dare to appear within 50 meters of him.

When the bald man came out and arrived at the bar counter, he admitted that he had never expected this situation.

Alice didn't run home; instead, she sat at the bar, sipping milk from a wine glass.

Just two steps away from him, a figure shrouded in black mist was mixing drinks like a bartender.

What's going on?

Don't they know that a battle has just taken place down below?

No, I get it now! This black-mist figure must be the "Moth." He probably thought I couldn't defeat those enhanced individuals, so he's waiting here to reap the benefits!

Hehe, I guess you could say it's no wonder it's called a "moth"?

Arrogant and conceited.

The moment he understood, he sprang into action, rushing towards the figure in the black mist, intending to kill him before he could react.

"Prepare to die, Moth! Daring to appear before me will be the biggest mistake of your life!"
