After the two girls left, Su Mu also controlled Edward to go out. Today's goal was to check out the gathering place for extraordinary individuals in the slums of the south of the city.

According to Edward's notes, today should be the day the gathering resumes.

So, as a god who will eventually conquer the world… no, bring good tidings to the world, he also needs to carefully examine the kind of opponents his first small goal will face.

Su Mu didn't choose to drive; instead, he put on an inconspicuous trench coat, donned a hat, and walked there.

Half an hour later, Su Mu arrived at his destination.

The streets of the slums were worlds apart from the city center.

The narrow alleyways were filled with the stench of coal smoke, rotting garbage, and cheap gin wafting from the rented rooms.

The path beneath his feet was no longer paved with flagstones, but rather a muddy, potholed track.

On either side stood four- or five-story rental apartments, their walls crammed together, crooked and blackened by coal smoke, almost obscuring the original brick color. Many windows lacked glass, covered only with rags or cardboard.

From a half-open window came the weak, feeble cries of a child, mingled with the hoarse curses of a woman.

He passed a side alley, where an even stronger stench assaulted his nostrils—a mixture of piled-up horse manure, rotten vegetable scraps, and even more repulsive things. In the darkness, several small, thin figures rummaged through the garbage, but upon hearing his heavy footsteps, they vanished like rats into the depths of the alley.

Su Mu frowned, withdrew his gaze, and deactivated his olfactory sharing with Edward.

He walked to a small alley, stopping at the deepest part before a shadowy iron gate. He took out a pure black mask, revealing only the eyes, hanging from his waist under his trench coat, put it on, and then knocked on the door.

After a moment, the door opened a crack.

A person wearing the same mask appeared before Su Mu.

The person stared at Edward without speaking.

Su Mu remained silent, instead taking out a peculiar coin from his pocket.

This coin was not common currency; the obverse read "Ferry Crossing."

This coin was what Su Mu had found at Edward's house; it was the ticket to the gathering of extraordinary individuals.

Upon seeing the coin, the person said nothing but immediately opened the iron gate to let Su Mu through, bowing respectfully and inviting him inside.

Su Mu put away the coins and stepped inside, subtly glancing around at his surroundings.

It looked like any other house, a typical rental in a slum: a crooked ceiling, peeling paint, blocked windows, and a secret passage leading to the basement. If it weren't for the kerosene lamp burning nearby, it would be completely dark.

Guided by the man, Su Mu entered the basement.

The stairs were steep, creaking underfoot, but the sound stopped halfway down.

The room was lower than expected. Taller people had to duck slightly to avoid hitting their heads on the doorframe.

Upon entering the basement, the first thing he saw was a round solid wood table.

Several people were already seated around the table, all wearing masks that completely obscured their faces.

However, some distinguishing features could be discerned from their figures:

An old man, a woman, and a man.

Seated in the main seat directly opposite the entrance was a figure clad in knightly armor, its gleaming silver catching Su Mu's eye for a second.

The others glanced at Su Mu before averting their gaze.

The armored figure spoke in a somewhat deep voice.

"You're quite punctual, sir. Perfect timing. Let's begin, a long-awaited gathering."

Su Mu observed the armored figure for a moment, then pulled up an empty chair and sat down.

Through the black mist surrounding Edward, he noticed that the armored figure possessed a higher level of spiritual energy than all the others present combined.

Thanks to the black mist branching off from the crack, Su Mu could see spirituality.

Most of the people present had a spirituality level similar to Edward's, a few were significantly higher, estimated to be eighth-tier transcendents, and the armor Su Mu deduced was at least sixth-tier.

"This is what I want to exchange."

A hoarse female voice came from the person to Su Mu's left.

The person took out a wooden box from her robes as she spoke.

"10g of Heart-Soothing Grass powder. Its value is self-evident. This 10g of powder can minimize interference from delirium during your advancement. At least 700,000."

Su Mu felt he might have misheard, but that was unlikely.

700,000 for 10g of powder... was that a bit...?

Su Mu felt it was a rip-off, but the others clearly didn't think so.

As soon as the woman finished speaking, someone made a bid.

"I'll offer 700,000."

"800,000."

...

In less than half a minute, the 10g of powder was sold for 1.24 million.

Su Mu began to wonder if the world's economy was experiencing inflation.

Soon, the next person began.

This time, it was a very calm male voice.

"What I'm selling is an opportunity for the Holy Communion Church to lend a hand."

The moment the man finished speaking, everyone in the room fell silent.



Chapter 23 The Church's Plans for Holy Communion


No one uttered a sound, whether from surprise or fear, it was unclear.

The first to break the silence was the knight in armor seated at the head of the table, his deep voice echoing through the basement.

"Heh, the Holy Communion Church's help? I doubt any of you here can afford such help."

No one responded, but everyone knew perfectly well that the Holy Communion Church was no good.

Moreover, they were notorious for double-crossing, specifically targeting those without connections or powerful backers.

If the Holy Communion Church discovered their identities, they would be drowned within a week.

Therefore, they dared not accept any of the Holy Communion Church's offer.

"I think there's a misunderstanding about our Holy Communion Church. We're not the kind of people who attack our fellow superhumans without reason. Most of the rumors you've heard are false. The few that are true are only because those despicable superhumans attempted to attack our Church, forcing us to retaliate."

The man's voice was sincere, sounding genuine.

Unfortunately, everyone present knew what kind of people the Holy Communion Church was; not a single word they said could be trusted.

Seeing no one respond, the man added, "Besides, our Holy Communion Church usually doesn't announce itself at these events. This time, we've even said that, showing our sincerity. If we were to resort to underhanded tactics, wouldn't we be ruining our reputation?"

What reputation does your Holy Communion Church have?

That thought echoed in everyone's minds, but no one dared to say it.

Just as the atmosphere became awkward, a hoarse, aged voice rang out.

"What is the scope of our assistance?"

All eyes turned towards the source of the voice.

Incredulity and mockery were evident in their eyes.

The speaker was naturally Edward, controlled by Su Mu.

Seeing that someone had actually responded, the Holy Communion Church man smiled and added, "Anyone below the seventh rank can be killed by our Holy Communion Church."

Several gasps were heard as soon as he finished speaking.

So arrogant?

The man seemed quite pleased with the crowd's astonished reaction, even straightening his back.

"Our Holy Communion Church is a secret church with a century-long history. I can say with certainty that we can even take down the Security Bureau within Dex City. For 2 million or an item containing sufficient [Cup] spiritual energy, we'll make a move."

The crowd gasped again.

This Holy Communion Church was incredibly arrogant, not even putting official organizations in their eyes.

Su Mu didn't understand the strong reaction from those around him, but it seemed they were all wary of the Security Bureau.

Was the Security Bureau the official supernatural organization of this world?

"Do you have [Heart] pathway potions and advancement rituals?"

The man paused for a moment, then glanced discreetly at the ring on his left hand.

"Where did this gentleman learn this information?"

"A guess."

Everyone present couldn't help but glance at Su Mu.

That excuse was too flimsy.

The man from the Holy Communion Church didn't refute anything.

"Although what our Holy Communion Church is selling this time is not a supernatural means, we can sell it if you can afford the price."

Without a word, Su Mu pulled the wooden box from his robes and opened it.

Three fingers imbued with the spirit of the [Cup].

The moment they were taken out, the intense [Cup] spirit within them caused the man's eyes to widen.

This guy actually possessed something this good?

This thing, so rich in [Cup] spirit and so pure, was an excellent ingredient for making potions.

The others also sensed the spirit contained within the three fingers, but since they weren't [Cup] pathway transcendents, they didn't have a strong desire for them.

The knight in armor on the main seat paused for a moment upon seeing the three fingers, but quickly recovered, unnoticed by anyone.

Those three fingers were the ones I sold last year. I didn't expect them to return to my gathering.

These three fingers were indeed the ones the knight in armor sold at the gathering last year, and now Su Mu was using them to exchange for transcendent pathways.

"Deal. However, I didn't bring the secret manual with me. Let's agree on a time and place."

The man suppressed his excitement, feigning nonchalance, and took out a pen and paper from his pocket.

So these legitimate organizations call books recording extraordinary methods "secret manuals"?

Su Mu thought to himself as he casually wrote down an address and time.

March 22nd, 7 PM, Sanko Street Factory.

This location was one he had just seen on his way here; it was a factory in the slums, but it looked like it had just closed down. Su Mu simply wrote it down, setting the time for tomorrow.

The man took back the pen and paper, glanced at it, and then put it back in his pocket.

"My transaction is over. You may continue."

The knight in the main seat glanced at Su Mu but said nothing.

He couldn't guarantee the safety of those conducting transactions outside.

Midway through, out of boredom, he even controlled the werewolves in the forest to catch some wild game to roast and eat, and also offered some opinions to the two sisters strolling around.

It was already 7 PM when it ended.

Su Mu returned home to find Alice preparing dinner.

Lorrina sat on the sofa, engrossed in a novel.

Su Mu couldn't help but think:

Children have it too easy. They need to experience the pain...and joy of school.

—

Meanwhile, the man claiming to be a member of the Holy Communion Church entered the courtyard of a lavishly decorated villa.

The man took out a white cloth with an inverted triangle drawn on it and a wavy line below.

The servant at the door, seeing this, said nothing and simply opened the door to let him in.

A few kerosene lamps were lit inside the villa, making it only slightly brighter than the sky outside.

As soon as he entered, an elderly man dressed as a butler greeted him.

"Sir, the master is waiting for you in the study on the second floor."

The man went upstairs and stopped at the door of the study at the far end of the corridor, knocking three times.

"Come in."

After entering, the man closed the door and then respectfully knelt on one knee.

"Sir, you've made a discovery."

The man he addressed as "Sir" was an elderly man who appeared to be in his fifties.

The old man had graying temples and wore glasses, but unlike other elderly men, he was not hunched over; his back remained upright.

His blue eyes were still bright.

The old man continued looking at the book in his hands and casually asked, "What did you find?"

"At the gathering, someone planned to use three fingers imbued with pure [Cup] spirit, along with the potion and ritual for our [Heart] exchange pathway."

The old man paused in his book-turning, turning his gaze from the page to the man for the first time.

"Three fingers imbued with pure [Cup] spirit? Are you sure?"

"Yes, I'm sure. I saw it with my own eyes. I could feel my spirit yearning for those three fingers."

The old man closed the book, lost in thought.

Their Holy Communion Church had lost many spiritual artifacts during the An Yin Bureau's siege, including several such fingers.



Chapter 24 Tracking Plan


Because of that incident, their Holy Communion Church suffered a severe blow and was now forced to live in seclusion. He himself was sent to Dex City to develop the church's influence.

According to intelligence from higher-ups, the Security Bureau here was also newly established and currently lacks strength. The higher-ups hoped he could find a way to infiltrate the Security Bureau.

So, our Holy Communion Church's property was picked up by these nobody—what a disgrace!

"What rank is he, and what's his path?"

"Reporting to you, sir, based on the ring's indication, he's ninth rank and should already be able to advance to eighth rank. His path is the same as ours—the [Cup]. By the way, his voice sounds like an old man."

The man's words carried a hint of doubt, and the old man also felt puzzled.

Another [Cup] level transcendent, and an old man at that—why would that person sell those three fingers?

Did he think there was no future and planned not to continue advancing?

That doesn't make sense either. If he didn't plan to advance, shouldn't he choose to exchange them for money? Why exchange them for secret techniques?

"It's a secret method different from my own. Could it be that some new secret society I don't know about has appeared in Dex City?"

The old man pondered for a moment before speaking, "Ensemble, tomorrow you, Miriam, and Zobert will go to the trade."

Ensemble immediately replied, "Yes, sir. Should I bring the secret method and the potion?"

"We don't need the [Heart] method anyway. Take the copy and the potion to him. Don't rush to kill him. Have you secretly investigate and see if there are any other organizations behind him. If so, let me know."

"Yes."

"Just leave the ring for your investigation here."

The old man tapped his index finger on the desk.

A hint of reluctance flashed in the man's eyes, but he still deftly removed the bone ring from his left index finger and respectfully placed it on the table the old man had indicated.

—

After leaving, Ensemble immediately went home and had his butler send a message to Miriam and Zobert.

A little over an hour later, a carriage stopped in front of Ensemble's house.

The carriage door opened, and a man and a woman stepped out.

The man was dressed in a suit, his face calm and resolute; the woman wore a burgundy dress, a hat, and a veil.

Both appeared to be around 25 years old; they were Zobert and Miria.

Miria stretched slightly after getting out of the carriage.

“A carriage is much more comfortable. Let me tell you, last week that young master from the Chris family took me for a ride in a car, and it was so bumpy I felt like my bones were going to fall apart, plus it was incredibly noisy.”

Miria’s tone was a mix of complaint and boasting.

Zobert ignored Miria’s Versailles-like tone, and instead, with impeccable manners, took a 20-cent note and a 5-cent coin from his jacket and placed them in the driver’s hand. He then pushed open the gate to the courtyard and headed straight for the Enns’ house.

Seeing that Zobert didn’t react, Miria grew bored and followed him inside.

The two were immediately escorted to the reception room by the butler upon entering.

They sat down on the sofa, and the butler closed the door behind them and left.

After they were seated, Miria spoke first, asking, "Ensemble, what brings you here all of a sudden?"

Ensemble, sitting opposite them, didn't mince words and replied directly, "It's a task from our mentor."

Ens recounted his conversation with his mentor, the old man, to the two of them.

Zobert listened and summarized, "So, the mentor wants the three of us to pretend to make a deal, but actually follow that old man to see if he has an organization?"

"Yes, my plan is that I'll be in charge of the deal tomorrow, and you guys stay in the shadows."

Miriam dismissed it, casually saying, "Is it really necessary to go through all that trouble? You said yourself that the old man is only at the ninth rank, while the three of us are all at the eighth rank [Cup]. Why not just capture him and control him?"

Ens frowned.

She was right. The three of them were all [Cup] pathway transcendents who had already reached the eighth rank.

In terms of combat power, they could completely overwhelm a ninth-rank transcendent. Although transcendents don't necessarily experience a qualitative leap with every advancement—some pathways don't show a significant difference in power between their first three rank, and some pathways can even be injured by firearms at the seventh rank—

they were noble [Cups].

Most [Cups], even those at the ninth rank, can temporarily ignore firearms, and each advancement allows them to easily kill those a rank lower, as the [Cup's] extraordinary abilities directly affect the body, with each advancement bringing physical enhancements.

Therefore, sending three eighth-rank individuals to secretly investigate a ninth-rank individual was somewhat like using a sledgehammer to crack a nut.

Although they had only advanced last month, the enhancements from the [Cup] were enormous, so the three never considered the possibility of being double-crossed and eliminated.

Furthermore, eighth-rank extraordinary individuals are extremely rare even in Dex City. They only reached the eighth rank because they were backed by the Holy Communion Church, a large organization. Even if the old man had some secret society behind him, Ens didn't believe they could send three eighth-rank extraordinary individuals.

No matter how you look at it, this was an absolutely easy mission for them, but Ens felt it necessary to emphasize it to prevent Miria from being careless and affecting the mission.

As for other possibilities of death, he hadn't even considered them.

“Miriah, although he's only at the ninth rank, he has three fingers on his hand imbued with the spirit of the [Cup]. Do you know what that means? He might be hiding a secret society behind him. If we act rashly, we might alert them.”

Although Ens was emphasizing the seriousness of the situation, Miria remained unconvinced.

“I didn’t say I was going to kill him outright. He’s only at the ninth rank; I can easily control him with my abilities.”

Before Ens could say anything, Zobert added.

“What if the secret society behind him has the ability to break free?”

Miriah scoffed, “Come on, how many superhumans in Dex City can break free of my control? The three of us are eighth rank. How many eighth-rank individuals have you seen in Dex City?”

Ens looked at her with disdain.

Arrogant just because she has some strength? She’ll die out there sooner or later.

While inwardly criticizing Ens, he said, “Miriah, you need to think carefully. If something really happens, how are you going to face your mentor’s punishment?”

Miria's smile froze upon hearing her mentor's punishment, but she quickly retorted defiantly, "I can't possibly fail. If I do fail, then I'm naturally willing to accept the mentor's punishment."

Ens rolled his eyes at her and said simply, "Fine, you said it."

Zobert added, "Just in case, I'll continue with the original tracking plan."

Ens nodded, "Then it's settled."

Although Ens didn't want Miria to cause trouble, given her personality, who knew what kind of trouble she might get into? He might as well let her have her way.

However, this was a solution Ens found acceptable after careful consideration.

If the old man truly had a secret organization behind him,

then with Zobert as insurance, even if Miria's actions alerted them, they could still obtain some intelligence. If the secret society wasn't strong enough, the three of them could simply go and take it down.



Chapter 25 The Invitation from the Security Bureau


7:43 AM, Dexterity, South Precinct.

Glenner sat in his office chair, a book covering his face to shield his eyes from the overhead light.

He had just finished dealing with all the follow-up matters. Cross had been dismissed and sentenced to three years in prison, but through his superior's behind-the-scenes maneuvering, an additional three years of probation had been added.

On the surface, Cross would still be under supervision for those three years, but given Glenner's understanding of the country's laws, it was practically the same as no sentence at all.

The only real harm to Cross might be that this stain on his record would prevent him from holding any position in the police force for the next ten years.

But he had no choice but to accept it; after all, he was just a squad leader now.

Just as he was inwardly grumbling, there was a knock on the office door.

He immediately removed the book from his face and placed it on the desk, sitting up straight and saying, "Come in."

The door opened, and one of his officers entered, looking excited.

"Captain, congratulations, you've been promoted!"

?

What's going on? How did I get promoted?

Before Glenner could react, the officer jogged up to him and handed him an envelope.

Glenner took the envelope and glanced at its appearance.

It looked like a normal envelope, except for the sealing wax stamp on the lip.

It was an image of a lion and an eagle.

Glenner's eyes widened the moment he saw the stamp.

In Prit, everyone recognized this stamp; it was the royal seal, reserved only for members of the royal family.

The officer added, "You wouldn't believe the expression on the chief's face when he gave me this letter. I was incredulous when I first saw it too, but I can accept your explanation, Captain. By the way, the chief also said this letter must never be seen by anyone else. I'll be going now."

With that, the officer left the office.

After the officer left, Glenner's mouth moved.

"What's this mark? You're staring at it so intently."

This was a question Su Mu asked, though he was trying to control himself.

Glenner snapped out of his daze, but his first thought was that this mysterious person was teasing him.

No one in all of Prit was unfamiliar with this mark; even the children in the slums knew what it represented.

But he didn't dare ask, and could only explain it to Su Mu.

After listening, Su Mu understood; Glenner had managed to get into a better position.

"Then open it and take a look."

Glenner hesitated, but obediently opened the envelope.

After all, he couldn't get rid of this mysterious presence.

Inside was only a folded piece of paper.

Glenner took it out and began to read it.

"Mr. Glenner, you are a superhuman from [The Lamp]. We've been observing you for a long time; you have great potential. However, I want to tell you that it's very difficult for superhumans to fight alone in Dex City. Here, we sincerely invite you to join our Security Bureau. We are affiliated with the nation and can provide you with the best potions and the safest environment for advancement."

Then came some information about the Security Bureau's salary and benefits, but Glenner didn't pay much attention.

He was still somewhat in a daze.

Has his identity as a superhuman been discovered?

When?

He hadn't even noticed.

"Sending me a job offer now is too late. What if this mysterious entity on me is discovered? Won't they kill me as a terrorist?"

Glenner was happy but also not so happy.

Eating royal food was exciting, but his current identity wasn't exactly clean.

"Congratulations, Glenner. Good luck in the future."

Su Mu controlled his mouth to congratulate him.

This brought him one step closer to his plan.

Thinking that Glenner was about to become an official superhuman, and would inevitably encounter secret societies like the Holy Communion Church, Su Mu felt a sense of pleasure.

He didn't need to do anything extra; he could achieve his goal simply by waiting for things to unfold.

Glenner, however, wasn't so happy.

Glenner hesitated for a moment before speaking, "Um, if you're on me, won't it give you away?"

Being treated as a terrorist might not harm the mysterious entity, but he would most likely be finished.

"No problem, your spiritual alarm hasn't gone off yet, has it? The current improved system is still safe."

Glenner was suddenly reminded that the spiritual alarm, which should have only triggered when something unusual came near, hadn't gone off in the past two days despite him being possessed!

Moreover, when he focused on observing himself in the mirror last night, he didn't see anything like Remo's abnormality in his eyes.

Glenner's awe of Su Mu grew even stronger.

Even the most sensitive [Lamp] couldn't detect it.

Glenner didn't believe that other superhumans could detect the problem with him.

Now he had given up, or rather, submitted. The two sides were completely different in strength; there was no hope of resistance. He might as well be a lackey; what if he did well and got a bonus?

Glenner's gaze fell on the lower right corner of the invitation.

"If you're interested, please arrive at Rose Security Company, 66 Mingke Street, at 8:00 PM on March 22nd. We'll send someone to meet you."

He carefully put the letter back into the envelope and tucked it into his pocket.

He took the envelope home and hung it on the wall.

—

8:31 PM, Rose Security Company, 66 Mingke Street, South District.

A stubble-faced 40-year-old man was sitting in the library, reading and drinking coffee.

The library door opened, and a 20-year-old woman with long brown hair walked in.

"Captain, just as you said, the Church of the Holy Communion appeared at the gathering."

The man called Captain put down his book and coffee.

"Vivian, what is their purpose in appearing at the gathering?"

Vivian reported everything that happened at the gathering.

The Captain frowned and asked, "Did you see where they made their transaction?"

Vivian lowered her head.

"I'm sorry, Captain, I originally wanted to use my abilities to see, but the ferryman suppressed it with his spiritual power."

"That's not your fault. After all, as the initiator of the gathering, he wouldn't allow anyone to directly challenge the rules he established to protect the privacy of transactions."

The captain shook his head, indicating that Vivian had done her best.

“At least we can now confirm that the Church of the Holy Communion has indeed established itself in Dex City. Having a target makes investigation easier. Let's see if we can gather more intelligence at the next gathering.”

Vivian finished her report and was about to leave when the captain called her back.

“By the way, we’ve been investigating Officer Glenner from the South District Police Station for three months now. So far, there’s absolutely nothing wrong with him. Even if he became a superhuman, he’s just using his abilities for work, and his background is clean. I’ve sent the invitation over. If he comes tomorrow, could you please introduce him to our Security Bureau?”

After a moment of silence, the captain added, “If he doesn’t come, then he’s a potential threat we need to monitor.”



Chapter 26 Trading


March 22nd, overcast, 7 PM, outside an unnamed factory on Sanko Street.

Ed, under Su Mu's control, was still wearing the mask, but this time he wore a black trench coat, the hood obscuring his face.

Taking advantage of the darkness and the poor lighting of the slums, his suspicious attire made him less conspicuous, almost blending into the environment.

Su Mu leaned against a broken streetlamp, waiting for his transaction partner to appear.

After a while, a figure also dressed in black approached.

The figure stopped about five meters away from Su Mu, first looking around to make sure no one else was there, then raising his hands to indicate he was unarmed.

Then he approached.

Su Mu didn't waste words, asking directly, "Did you bring the stuff?"

The person didn't speak, but simply took out a booklet and a small bottle containing a pale pink solution from under his trench coat.

"And yours?"

The figure's voice made Su Mu hesitate.

Why is it a female voice?

However, Su Mu quickly figured it out. The heads of the purchasing and trading departments were different; it made sense.

Su Mu took out a wooden box from his pocket and opened it.

Three dried fingers still lay inside.

As soon as the box was opened, Su Mu could clearly feel the eagerness in the figure before him.

Su Mu closed the box and extended his other hand.

The figure snapped out of her daze and placed the booklet and medicine in Su Mu's empty hand, taking the wooden box with her other hand.

"This is the advancement ritual for the ninth and eighth tiers of the [Heart] pathway, followed by the ninth-tier potion."

After the figure finished speaking, Su Mu didn't leave immediately but instead opened the booklet to examine it.

Just kidding, what if you sold me counterfeit goods if I went back right away?

The booklet's contents were simple, merely describing the advancement rituals required for the ninth-tier [Listener] and eighth-tier [Empathizer] pathways of the [Heart] pathway.

The ninth rank requires listening to the stories of seven people with different experiences, each for at least an hour, without uttering a sound.

The listening can be done at different times. As long as you've listened before drinking the potion, you can advance. However, doing so might make those with fragile souls more susceptible to the potion's backlash.

And its ability... is the ability to sense the intense emotions of beings within a 10-meter radius.

Su Mu regretted it as soon as he saw this; this ability was essentially a passively degraded version of his own.

"Try to be more positive. I didn't want Alice to be super strong anyway, as long as she's a superhuman, a weaker ability is fine."

Consoling himself, Su Mu continued reading.

The eighth-rank ritual's ability is to absorb and store emotions, which can be used on oneself when needed.

Some aspects are unclear, but it has a second skill.

It allows one to use the stored emotions to resonate with someone else's emotions. After achieving resonance, the other person will subconsciously believe one's words.

The content is short; a quick glance is enough. If Su Mu were to give an evaluation, it would be without question.

In every aspect, it felt less practical than her own innate abilities.

Just use it as a decorative vase.

Thinking this, Su Mu put away the booklet.

The figure waited until Su Mu finished reading before speaking again, saying, "Pleasure doing business with you."

The voice sounded alluring.

But Su Mu immediately sensed something was wrong.

Something's not right; there's something fishy about this guy's words.

As the figure uttered its last words, Su Mu sensed something amiss in Edward's mind.

Despite only meeting once, this figure seemed to have become incredibly important to Edward's subconscious; his body seemed to instinctively want to be closer to it.

Noticing the change, Su Mu ignored Edward's altered state and first checked his own consciousness.

Fortunately, Su Mu's own consciousness was unaffected. It seemed the ability only affected Edward's mind, while he, possessing Edward's mind, remained unaffected.

Su Mu breathed a sigh of relief.

"That scared me to death! I almost thought I'd been hypnotized by the app too."

Su Mu didn't immediately attack the figure. He wanted to see what it wanted to do, so he slightly loosened his control over Edward.

Under the influence of his subconscious, Edward's body slightly raised its hand as if to embrace the person before it, but the figure dodged.

"Hehe, don't be in such a hurry. How about we meet here again tomorrow?"

"...Okay..."

Ed's words were flat, as if he were hypnotized. Su Mu didn't try to control Ed, but simply observed him silently.

And he began to calculate in his mind.

This guy's strength seems to be only at the eighth rank. Judging from the color of his spiritual energy, it's also a [Cup]. How come there are so many [Cups]? And I haven't seen a single [Cup] with the same ability yet.

Are there so many variations of [Cups]?

Thanks to the fact that the black mist on Ed's body came from the crack, Su Mu wasn't weakened as much and still retained the ability to directly see spiritual energy.

After expanding his perception range to a radius of 100 meters, Su Mu discovered new spiritual reactions.

Two more [Cups], also at the eighth rank.

Su Mu understood.

Looks like he's planning to double-cross him.

"Alright, see you tomorrow."

The figure finished speaking and turned to leave.

Ed's body also turned and left as if on command.

After Edward took a few steps, Su Mu regained control of his body and continued walking, though they had strayed from the direction home.

Su Mu planned to lure them back for a reverse double-cross.

Su Mu maintained his senses as he walked, but to his surprise, only one person followed.

Only one person? That's fine. With more people, it's not certain he could control them all.

—

Ensemble watched as Zobert followed Su Mu out of sight, then looked at Miria.

Miria pulled down her hood, looking at Ensemble with a smug expression.

“See? I told you, there aren't many in Dex City who can resist my control.”

Ensemble rolled his eyes at her, retorting, “Don't be too smug. We still don't know if he has someone behind him who can remove your 'seed'.”

Miria nonchalantly tossed her curly hair.

"Heh, someone in his fifties has probably been a Transcendent for over a decade, right? Still stuck at the ninth rank after all this time, what secret society would want him?"

"Even if a society did want him, it'd probably be some third-rate secret society."

Ensemble didn't want to waste any more words with Miria.

"Let's go back and wait for Zobert's news."

"Alright, Ensemble, before Zobert returns, let's do something enjoyable~"

Miria could sense Ens's distrust, but she didn't care. Her judgment over the past twenty years hadn't been wrong; no matter how much Ens distrusted her, he would ultimately be the one to be proven wrong.

Feeling relieved, Miria subconsciously began to seduce Ens.

This was also a side effect of becoming a superhuman.



Chapter 27 Standoff


"Are you free? I need to go out for a bit."

Alice was still cooking in the kitchen when Su Mu's voice suddenly rang in her head.

She was startled, her shoulders trembling slightly, before she silently repeated in her mind, "Yes, I'm free anytime, Lord of the Mist."

Alice was still unaccustomed to Su Mu's sudden voice, feeling that if this continued, it might scare her into a mental breakdown.

"Then lend me your body."

"Eh!"

Before Alice could react, she found her body uncontrollably moving towards the door.

Lorina, sitting on the sofa she had just bought yesterday, holding a novel, saw Alice come out of the kitchen and asked curiously, "Sister, are you going out?"

"Yes."

Alice answered Lorina's question while changing her shoes at the door.

Lorina immediately guessed Alice's situation from her indifferent tone.

"Lord of the Mist? What are you two going out for?"

"To fight against evil forces."

Su Mu wasn't surprised that Lolina immediately noticed something was amiss; he had no intention of hiding anything.

After changing his shoes, he reached back and brushed his bangs, which were obstructing his vision.

"Girls' hair is so troublesome."

Alice, confined to her own dark consciousness, could only watch as Su Mu, controlling her body, made such an impolite comment about her hair.

Even though she was only in a conscious state, Alice still felt her cheeks flush.

She felt both embarrassed and angry.

The Lord of the Mist doesn't understand anything! Long hair is one of a girl's charms!

Su Mu ignored Alice's complaints, which he had squeezed into his consciousness space.

Well, it wasn't that he ignored her; teasing her every now and then was quite fun.

"Oh, by the way, go to the kitchen and keep an eye on things for a while, make sure the vegetables don't burn."

With that, Su Mu left without lingering, controlling Alice to get into the car and start it.

During the drive, Su Mu asked, "By the way, Alice, have you ever listened to anyone's troubles over the years?"

Alice's connection to her body hadn't been severed. She was currently experiencing the bumps and jolts of the car's movement. Hearing Su Mu's question, she was somewhat puzzled but still responded in her consciousness, "Yes, when I was little, my parents once talked to me all night about work stress. After they passed away, the grandparents in the village would sometimes pull me aside and tell me how hard life was. I didn't know how to comfort them, so I could only listen silently."

Su Mu's eyes lit up. A Sacred Body with the Innate [Heart] Path!

"How many times have you listened to people's stories without saying a word for more than an hour?"

"Hmm... about ten? About seven or eight times I didn't say a word. I gradually changed that later, and now I can comfort others."

"Not saying anything is a good habit."

"Huh? Is that so?"

—

7:34 PM.

Zobert had been following Ed for 30 minutes. Although he couldn't go very far in Dex City on foot in that time, Zobert had a feeling that Ed was deliberately keeping him on tenterhooks.

It must be his imagination. This old man hadn't even withstood Miriam's hypnosis, how could he possibly have noticed him?

Suppressing his doubts, Zobert continued to follow him secretly.

Then, Zobert noticed Ed suddenly stop.

He stopped too, and then realized something was wrong. In the 30 minutes he'd been following Ed, they were almost out of Dexterity.

The houses weren't clustered together like in the city anymore; there was a house every few hundred meters, and most of them were abandoned.

He'd been so focused on Ed that he hadn't noticed his surroundings.

That was normal, though. The slums were on the city's edge, so why was he leaving the city?

Was their base outside the city?

Or did he live outside the city?

Zobert wasn't too worried.

Ed hadn't looked back the whole way, and there were no mirrors along the way to give himself away. Besides, it was nighttime; even if a mirror reflected his image, he wouldn't be able to tell.

If, by sheer bad luck, he were discovered, he wouldn't panic.

There couldn't be any ambush in this environment. Surely an eighth-tier [Cup] wouldn't be weaker than a ninth-tier [Cup]?

Zobert relaxed, intending to see what Edward was planning by suddenly stopping.

Just then, a beam of bright light shone from behind him, directly onto Edward.

Zobert immediately became alert, hiding behind a wall while turning to look at where the beam of light was coming from.

He turned around and saw a car swaying along the muddy road.

As the car passed him, he tried to see who was driving.

But that one glance sent alarm bells ringing in his mind.

The car window was open, and in the driver's seat was a very young-looking woman. At the same time he looked at her, the woman turned her head and met his gaze.

He was certain those bluish-green eyes were looking at him.

But how could this be?

He had followed her the whole way without noticing anything unusual, and this suddenly appearing woman had spotted him instantly?

For a fleeting moment, he wanted to rush out and kill the woman to eliminate any future trouble, but he restrained himself.

His current task was to follow the old man and see if there was any secret society behind him, not to kill a woman he'd never seen before.

Just as he was thinking this, Edward, standing in the middle of the road, suddenly spoke up: "How long are you planning to hide? Aren't you going to come out and show yourself?"

He'd been discovered?

When?!

This was the first time Zobert had felt such shock in the 30 minutes he'd been following him.

This shouldn't be happening. Or does this guy possess some kind of supernatural sensory artifact?

Zobert pondered for a while, then finally stepped out.

Since he'd already been discovered, he might as well stop following him.

Since he couldn't gather intelligence without alerting the enemy, he might as well capture a prisoner and extract information from him.

At worst, we can capture him, have Milado hypnotize him, and make him reveal everything he knows. Then, the three of us can raid his secret society, hand the spoils over to our mentor, and he shouldn't punish us.

With a plan in mind, Zobert slowly approached Edward.

"How did you discover me, sir?"

He'd approach slowly, feigning no hostility. Once within 50 meters, he'd be in his advantageous position.

Just as he was pondering how to deal with Ed, the car pulled up beside him.

The car door opened, and the woman stepped out, first fixing her hair, then casually accepting the potion Ed offered her.

They were in cahoots?

Zobert didn't waste any more words and charged directly at Ed and Alice.

He wasn't sure if the woman was a superhuman, and if so, what level.

So he decided to strike first.

At least Zobert was certain the woman wasn't a [Cup].

The entire superhuman pathways related to [Cup] in Dex City were now monopolized by their Holy Communion Church.

Other pathways weren't strong in close combat; as long as he could get close, he could win.



Chapter 28 Drink it directly


Zobert is an eighth-tier [Bone Forger] through the [Cup] pathway, having advanced from the ninth-tier [Blood Cup Body].

Besides the inherent abilities of the ninth-tier [Blood Cup Body]—three times the density of a normal person, skin toughness impervious to normal blades, and rapid wound healing—he has received further enhancements.

He also possesses new eighth-tier abilities: Bone Healing, Bone Spikes, Bone Marrow Regeneration, and a Forged Bone Body.

Bone Healing grants him the ability to actively heal fractures and cracks.

Bone Spikes grant him the ability to control his own bone growth, allowing his bones to "grow" outside his body.

Bone Marrow Regeneration enhances his blood-producing ability, making him recover from blood loss ten times faster than a normal person, and allowing him to gain short bursts of power by actively consuming bone marrow when needed.

A Forged Bone Body further enhances his bone density, allowing his bones to withstand rifle bullets even in their normal state without being penetrated.

These combined abilities make Zobert a true "blood tank."

Not only was his durability astonishing, but his attack power was also considerable; his bone spikes could easily pierce through the bodies of others, even metal.

Zobert didn't want to give Alice any time to react, intending to eliminate this unknown factor first.

In just a few seconds, the distance of over 200 meters was reduced to 50 meters.

Zobert clenched his right fist, and bone spikes sprouted from between his fingers.

From his perspective, Alice remained in the posture of receiving the potion, showing no reaction whatsoever to his sudden attack.

This was his signal of success.

In Zobert's view, Alice's lack of reaction to his actions was because she hadn't even had time to react.

His thinking was correct; as long as he gained the upper hand, he could easily eliminate Alice, this unstable factor.

Zobert's right fist slammed directly towards Alice's head without hesitation.

Just as Zobert was gloating, a hand appeared in his line of sight.

It wasn't a human hand, but a hand covered in thick hair, with sharp nails, resembling that of a wild beast.

A hand reached directly for his head.

Zobert reacted faster, raising his left hand during his charge to collide with the hand.

The force of the hand wasn't as great as Zobert's, and he was knocked back a distance, but Zobert stopped due to the recoil.

"It's almost as strong as me!"

Zobert was somewhat astonished, looking at the owner of the hand as he stopped.

"A werewolf?"

"No, it's that old man. Is this his supernatural ability?"

Zobert was surprised that Ed, a ninth-tier [Cup], could block his attack, an eighth-tier one. This forced him to take it seriously.

"Since you want to block me, I'll break your arms and legs first."

Zobert immediately changed targets and attacked Ed.

Ed, in his werewolf form, was doubled in power by Su Mu's black mist and engaged Zobert in combat.

But it was clear that Ed was at a disadvantage.

Although Zobert also had wounds inflicted by Ed's claws, those wounds healed in the blink of an eye.

In contrast, Ed constantly suffered cuts and punctures from bone spurs. While these wounds were also healing, their rate of healing was clearly slower than the rate at which Zobert had inflicted them.

Alice, initially caught off guard by Zobert's attack within her consciousness, finally understood what was happening.

Looking at the recurring wounds on Ed's body, Alice couldn't help but worry.

Could the Lord of the Mist be unable to defeat him?

Su Mu didn't further enhance Ed; he simply kept Zobert occupied.

He didn't expect Ed to win against Zobert. In his view, Ed's spirituality was less than half of Zobert's.

Normally, Ed shouldn't have been able to withstand even a single blow from Zobert. The fact that he was holding on like this was entirely due to Su Mu enhancing Ed's regenerative and defensive abilities with black mist, but it was clear this wouldn't last long.

Multiple wounds from Zobert's bone spurs had already appeared on Ed's body.

To maximize the damage, Zobert had even broken off the bone spurs embedded in Ed's body, leaving them inside to hinder Ed's regeneration.

"Now, do you want superpowers?"

Su Mu's voice appeared in Alice's consciousness.

Alice didn't understand why Su Mu would say this at such a dangerous time, but she instinctively followed his words, saying, "Yes."

"Then here you are."

Su Mu controlled Alice to unscrew the cap of the potion and drink it all in one gulp.

Zobert, who had already broken both of Ed's arms, was watching Alice's every move.

Seeing Alice drink the potion so readily, he felt like he was watching a madwoman.

He had initially thought she was an unknown superhuman, but it turned out she was a fool.

From his perspective, Alice had just taken the potion and drunk it, without even watching the ascension ceremony.

And this naive Alice dared to drink the potion without any precautions.

She was completely playing the fool.

Zobert's initially anxious mood immediately turned relaxed, and even his attacks on Ed slowed down considerably.

He'd thought Ed was some kind of extraordinary being who had achieved superhuman abilities through other means, but it turned out he was just an ordinary person who hadn't even reached the level of superhuman.

He probably learned about superhumans by chance and wanted to become one himself.

The clues in Zobert's mind converged into one answer.

Ed probably didn't have any secret society behind him; he was simply hired by this unknown young lady to buy potions.

As for the three fingers, he guessed Alice had bought some trinket on the black market and happened to run into Ed, who had some knowledge of their value and a ferry pass. So Alice gave him a reward to sell them.

He'd been so cautious before he acted, thinking it would be a close match where he needed to strike first, but now it seemed like he, an eighth-tier, was beating a ninth-tier and a complete idiot.

Even with this guess, he didn't plan to kill Ed first and then Alice.

The mission was given by his mentor; he should interrogate Ed first and then wait for his mentor's instructions.

"Heh, are you sure you still want to fight me? You should know the consequences of drinking the potion without completing the ritual, right? If you keep stalling until your spiritual energy is depleted, how are you going to dodge an attack from a spiritual creature? Kneel down and kowtow to me now, and I might be able to help you out."

Zobert, leisurely exchanging blows with Ed, tried to undermine Ed's fighting spirit.

However, he was unaware that Ed currently lacked any will, and even if Ed himself were present, he probably wouldn't understand the consequences of drinking the potion without performing the ritual.

Because Ed had become a superhuman entirely through unorthodox means.

Su Mu, of course, knew the consequences, but he wasn't afraid.

He glanced at Alice's soul in his warp consciousness.

Alice's soul was now with him, and he could control it at will. Most importantly, this was his territory.

The territory of an evil god—could a mere potion harm my people?

And even if it could, he had a way to deal with it.

The first semi-mutated werewolf also experienced a similar phenomenon of being corrupted by the potion, and he had directly suppressed the change, restoring him to a normal state.

And there were no side effects. If he had simply released the control, that cultist could have easily regained his consciousness.

Therefore, this is a completely free way to gain superpowers.



Chapter 29 Melts Upon Entry


Alice found the potion's taste strange.

She couldn't quite put her finger on it; it was slightly bitter, yet also cloyingly sweet.

After entering her mouth, it was like melting snow, almost imperceptible.

However, she was certain the Lord of the Mist hadn't lied to her. After drinking it, the extraordinary abilities possessed by the [Listener] automatically surfaced in her consciousness.

Emotional Perception: She could sense the intense emotions of beings within a 10-meter radius.

Emotional Guidance: She could amplify the hidden emotions of beings through eye contact.

Emotional Anchor: She could remember and never forget intense emotions she had sensed.

Alice was still somewhat bewildered.

So, superpowers just appeared directly in her mind?

"How are things? Did you hear any other sounds in your head?"

Su Mu's voice rang out, asking Alice if she seemed alright.

Although he had seen Alice's soul in the subspace and it remained pure and uncorrupted, he still wanted to confirm it just to be safe.

"There are no other sounds besides your voice."

Alice answered truthfully, excitedly telling Su Mu about her abilities.

Su Mu felt both happy and unhappy after hearing this.

Happy that this extraordinary path wasn't so bad, unhappy that he had been tricked again.

What did this mean? Why were they even tricking me with a little note?

Su Mu made up his mind to make the Holy Communion Church pay the price, even though he had never intended to let them off the hook from the beginning.

Their communication was entirely on a conscious level. In the real world, Zobert only saw Alice remain motionless after drinking the potion.

Several minutes passed without any movement.

A sense of unease crept into Zobert's heart.

What's going on? She hasn't been affected by the potion after all this time?

Could it be… she really succeeded in her advancement?

Zobert shook his head, banishing the thought.

What a joke! To succeed in advancement after drinking a potion without performing the ritual? That's impossible!

Even if she had already completed the ritual before, it wouldn't be possible to advance from an ordinary person to a transcendent being so easily.

This kind of advancement without a ritual is practically a death sentence. How could this silly young lady from who-knows-where have advanced so easily?!

Zobert felt jealous.

To complete the ritual for advancing to the eighth rank of [Bone Forger], he had personally broken three bones, enduring the excruciating pain of bones piercing his flesh, and spent eight hours barely recovering thanks to his ninth-rank [Blood Cup Body].

After completing the ritual, he immediately drank a potion, yet even then, he endured half a month of delirium, only gradually stabilizing now with the help of his mentor.

And yet, this woman had advanced unscathed, even quite comfortably!

Whether from a personal or organizational perspective, he couldn't let this woman return alive.

Today's events would definitely make their Holy Communion Church an enemy of this woman.

Zobert didn't hesitate. He opened his right hand, plunged it into Ed's left arm with a chop, and caused his hand bones to grow wildly, his bone spurs piercing Ed's skin.

Then, with a powerful flick of his wrist, he severed Ed's left arm.

Zobert immediately turned and stabbed Alice with his blood-stained right hand.

Alice, within her consciousness, was on the verge of tears.

*Lord of the Mist, he's here!*

This was the first time she had witnessed a battle between superhumans. During the Remo incident, she had only vaguely sensed the ferocity of the fight through the smell of blood in the air.

But seeing it with her own eyes today, she felt terrified.

Ed and Zobert were practically covered in blood.

It was impossible to tell whose blood it was.

Since Su Mu hadn't blocked her senses, she could clearly smell the thick stench of blood in the air.

The instinctive fear within her made her want to get away immediately.

But her body was controlled by Su Mu, and she couldn't move.

Moreover, she had witnessed the entire process of Ed's arm being pierced from the inside and severed.

Alice felt her heart leap into her throat.

Su Mu, on the other hand, was completely unperturbed; he was sensing the changes within himself.

Just as he expected, the intensity of his black mist had recovered slightly after Alice became a superhuman.

“Let me test the waters first.”

As he spoke, the black ring behind Alice’s head began to spin, and black mist emanated from it, obscuring the moonlight and plunging Alice and Zobert into complete darkness.

What kind of ability is this?

Does the [Heart] pathway possess such an ability?

Zobert was astonished, but his movements didn’t stop.

Before the black mist appeared, he was less than 5 meters away from Alice; a direct attack would surely hit her.

But things didn’t go as planned.

Zobert felt he had rushed more than 10 meters forward, but he had no real feeling of hitting anything.

What was going on?

Zobert stopped and began to look around warily, but all he could see was darkness.

Su Mu, however, hadn’t moved at all; he remained standing in place.

What changed was the space shrouded in his black mist.

Within the area covered by his black mist, he could control the size of that space.

The complexities were beyond Su Mu's comprehension; the knowledge he learned in school had long been forgotten in the past thirty million years.

In short, he could distort space.

Just now, he expanded the space in front of Zobert and compressed the space behind him, moving Zobert away from him without affecting Zobert at all.

Wow, black mist has this ability?

That's insane.

Su Mu hadn't discovered this ability before; he hadn't realized he could use it like this when he emerged from the rift.

But now, thinking back, he seemed to use this method of movement in the subspace.

Only, back then, he didn't have a definite body, and there were no reference points around him, so he subconsciously thought he was running like a human.

Isn't this cheat too overpowered?

Su Mu couldn't understand how he could possibly lose with this ability.

He even entertained the idea of ​​becoming a Homelander.

However, he quickly suppressed this thought.

No, one must have some principles, and besides, if he, an evil... Outer God, was already this powerful, he didn't even dare to imagine the strength of the true gods of the Church.

*Lord of the Mist, what's going on? I can't see anything!*

Within her consciousness, Alice asked somewhat nervously. Unlike Su Mu, she couldn't see her surroundings clearly through the black mist.

After all, Su Mu wasn't human; in a sense, the black mist was part of his body, functioning as his eyes.

"Wait, let me adjust the transparency."

?

Transparency...can the black mist become less black?

As Su Mu finished speaking, the black mist that had been obscuring Alice and Zobert's vision became transparent, allowing them to see the ground and sky again...

They couldn't see the sky; it was still black mist above.

But even in an environment devoid of any light source, she could still see the ground and the weeds on it, as if the black mist itself possessed some kind of light source.

Su Mu made all the black mist except for the boundary transparent; now, the black mist no longer obstructed their vision, just like ordinary air.

Su Mu was somewhat impressed.

Is this what they call cheating?



Chapter 30 Spatial Control


Zobert immediately focused his gaze on Su Mu, or rather, Alice, after Su Mu adjusted the transparency of the black mist.

What's going on?

The distance...how did it become so much greater?

Zobert was extremely puzzled and nervous.

The distance had increased so much without anyone noticing; it didn't seem like an ability of the [Heart] pathway at all, but he had personally witnessed Alice drink the potion.

What is this?

A mutation?

Or was his initial guess correct—this woman had indeed been corrupted by the potion and had now become a spiritual being?

But he had never heard of any [Heart] pathway spiritual being possessing this ability.

Zobert dared not act rashly.

Su Mu also noticed a flaw in the black mist.

His control over the black mist hadn't been significantly enhanced; he couldn't control an eighth-tier transcendent being.

While confronting Zobert, he tried to directly invade the latter's consciousness with the black mist, but failed.

The control-type ability was too underhanded, preventing it from receiving any enhancement.

However, it's alright. While I can't control it, I'm still more than capable of fighting.

Besides, it's probably because Alice's current strength isn't enough. The black mist she can release is still a degraded version of her original form. Giving Alice another potion later should allow her to reliably control an eighth-tier opponent.

"What kind of ability is this?"

Zobert asked. He didn't expect Su Mu to tell him directly. He didn't think Su Mu would be that foolish, but the battle just now had depleted some of his spiritual energy. Although not much, he planned to stall for time as a precaution.

Su Mu, however, was stumped.

Indeed, these extraordinary individuals all have skill descriptions after drinking potions. Should his ability keep up with the times and get a name too?

Su Mu rested his chin on his hand, tilting his head in thought.

"I don't know. What do you think would be a more impressive name for my ability?"

Zobert was momentarily stunned by Su Mu's question.

"You're asking me what your ability should be called?"

Alice, within the consciousness space, asked curiously, "Lord of the Mist, what does 'prestige' mean?"

"It means something that sounds high-class."

Su Mu didn't shy away from Zobert's thoughts and answered Alice's question directly.

Zobert swallowed hard, watching Su Mu mutter to himself.

He'd heard that madmen's abilities were stronger than others after becoming superhumans. Could this woman be like that?

"I've got it! Let's call it Domain Expansion."

Su Mu snapped her fingers, naming her skill.

"Hmm, this is just a poetic title; I'll think of a specific name later."

Zobert, seeing the woman completely ignoring him and engrossed in naming her skill, immediately seized the opportunity and charged forward again.

Whatever, as long as he got close, how could an eighth-tier [Cup] not beat him?

Although he didn't know how she did it, the fact that this woman chose to keep her distance proved she was wary of him getting close.

Once Zobert understood this, his thinking became simple.

He would compare whether he could approach her faster or she could move away faster.

Just a few seconds later, Zobert stopped.

His face showed a look of horror.

He was already approaching at full speed, but the distance between him and Alice hadn't decreased an inch since he started moving.

It seemed the closer he got, the farther away she became.

Am I just standing still?

Illusion?

Zobert's mind raced through various possibilities, but he still couldn't figure out what was happening.

He gritted his teeth and snapped the bone in his other little finger.

The excruciating pain jolted his nerves.

It wasn't an illusion; how could he have done that?!

Just then, he suddenly noticed that the once densely packed weeds on either side of the muddy ground in front of him had become extremely sparse, with only clumps visible every three or four meters.

Zobert froze, a bold and crazy guess spreading through his mind.

Could it be...?

How could this be?

He directly altered the terrain?

How could there be a way to directly alter the terrain?

This woman couldn't be "Heart," but it couldn't be any other method either.

In Zobert's understanding, no extraordinary method at a low sequence could directly alter the terrain.

Could this woman's sequence be at least sixth rank?

But that was impossible; he had personally witnessed Alice drink the potion.

Since she didn't turn into a monster, it meant Alice was just an ordinary person before.

Zobert's guess about Su Mu's abilities was partly correct; he guessed Su Mu could alter the terrain, but what he didn't know was that Su Mu didn't do it by controlling the land, but by controlling space.

However, he would probably never guess the truth behind Su Mu's ability; humans cannot imagine things they haven't seen.

Alice, within her consciousness space, also noticed Zobert's inability to approach and the changes in the weeds.

As an observer standing at the center of space, the changes in her field of vision were more obvious than Zobert's.

She could see cracks appearing in the ground between herself and Zobert, as if the land had been forcibly torn apart.

And her breathing was a little labored.

"Lord of the Mist, I feel... a little dizzy..."

After Alice mentioned this, Su Mu noticed that the air had become thinner after he expanded the surrounding space.

In other words, Alice was almost experiencing altitude sickness because of his actions.

For Alice's sake, Su Mu used her body as a reference point and compressed the space above her waist back to its original state, restoring the oxygen levels that had become thin due to the spatial expansion.

As for Zobert, his body had suddenly leaned forward, and before he could react, he fell face-first to the ground.

His body hadn't actually moved, but because the space in front of him had been compressed, the space where his upper body was originally located shifted forward, and his upper body moved along with the space, further shifting his center of gravity, ultimately resulting in him falling to the ground as if leaning forward.

"Feeling better?"

"Yes, much better."

While Su Mu confirmed that Alice was alright, Zobert was experiencing a turmoil of emotions.

He was certain that his body hadn't been affected by any external force, and that he should have been standing upright.

He didn't move, yet his body position shifted?!

Zobert was utterly baffled, his fear of Alice deepening.

She managed to knock me down without me even noticing; this girl is a monster! I can't possibly beat her!

Zobert immediately scrambled to his feet and resolutely turned to charge towards the wall of black mist, attempting to smash through it and escape.

If he couldn't win, he'd run. This black mist shouldn't be able to stop him.

Because his movements within the black mist hadn't been hindered before, Zobert subconsciously assumed it wouldn't be much different from ordinary fog.

This fog can't possibly block my way like a wall, can it?



Chapter 31 Instant Kill


Thump!

Zobert slammed his head against the wall of black mist, imagining that plunging into the mist would bring back the outside world.

But it felt like he'd hit a real wall; his forehead was bleeding, blood streaming down his face.

The cut on his forehead was minor, the abrasion quickly healed, but the fear in Zobert's heart wouldn't dissipate.

Trapped?!

"Why is everyone I see only thinking about running away? Doesn't anyone even try to kill me?"

Su Mu slowly walked towards Zobert, not choosing to compress space to approach him without walking.

He found this leisurely stroll more relaxing.

Lord of the Mist, you're still using my body...

Alice muttered under her breath. Although Su Mu's words sounded more like sarcasm, it was still ironic that he was using her body to say someone didn't want to kill him.

Zobert turned around immediately upon hearing the voice, watching the golden hair sway slightly with the girl's movements, her emerald green eyes looking at him with a mocking gaze.

The girl walked slowly, shaking her fingers as she went, muttering, "As the saying goes, a cornered beast fights, a desperate battle, shouldn't you face me now?"

Alice, perfectly playing the straight man, shouted in the consciousness space, "As expected of the Lord of the Mist, so cultured, although I don't understand what it means."

*It's normal not to understand. I used Chinese idioms. If you understood, I'd be scared.*

Su Mu thought to himself.

Unlike Su Mu's leisurely demeanor, Zobert was now highly vigilant as Su Mu approached.

*There's no other way. I have to take a gamble. She didn't dare let me get close earlier, so her advantageous distance should be around 60 meters. If she gets within 20 meters, I'll have a chance.*

43m

21m

16m

Now!

Without hesitation, Zobert seized the moment when Su Mu shifted her gaze away from him and charged forward.

Without holding back, Zobert unleashed bone spurs from various parts of his body, intending to use this combined offensive and defensive tactic to finish off Alice.

*Thud!*

Zobert stopped.

Alice was only a meter in front of him, not because he didn't want to get closer.

His gaze shifted downwards.

But all he could see was a fist-sized hole in his abdomen.

He couldn't see what caused the wound, but he could see his internal organs through the pierced opening.

But no blood was flowing, and the wound hadn't healed.

It was as if something was blocking the opening.

Blood gushed from Zobert's mouth. He turned his gaze back to Alice, who was now looking at him with a mocking expression.

He opened his mouth and forcefully spat the blood into Alice's eyes, while simultaneously continuing to throw a punch at her with his right hand.

Although the fist couldn't touch Alice, the protruding bone spikes could easily pierce her.

The blood, propelled by Zobert's immense strength, flew towards Alice's eyes at a speed imperceptible to most.

But the blood didn't successfully obscure Alice's vision; it stopped a few centimeters in front of her eyes and remained suspended in mid-air.

The bone spurs growing from Zobert's right fist stopped in front of Alice. No matter how Zobert tried to continue growing them, they couldn't touch her.

Even though they were right in front of him, and he could feel the spurs growing, he just couldn't get any closer to Alice.

Su Mu even had the leisure to reach out a finger and twirl it around a strand of Alice's hair.

It was surprisingly soft.

The principle behind the blood stopping in mid-air was actually quite simple.

Su Mu had restructured this space.

In normal space, the blood's current spatial coordinates would constantly move closer to the planet due to its influence. This wasn't due to any force pulling it, but rather the change in space itself.

Now, Su Mu had restored the spatial coordinates within the black mist to their original state, free from planetary interference, and pushed the blood's spatial coordinates backward to counteract the initial velocity Zobert had added to the blood, thus creating the effect of the blood stopping in mid-air.

This was a method he had just come up with.

As for the hole in Zobert's abdomen... he had pierced it with the transparent black mist.

The powerful brick flew through this area.

And...

Alice's body floated up, hovering in mid-air.

Just as he had thought, by changing the coordinate system, he could achieve a similar effect to flight.

*Lord of the Mist! I... I'm flying?!*

Thank goodness I'm wearing pants today... Wait, what am I thinking?

Alice saw this scene through her vision, feeling surprised, excited, and somewhat relieved in her consciousness space.

Zobert's expression became even more desperate.

His entire face turned pale.

Flying... Flying?! Are there any superhumans who can fly?!

Su Mu looked down at Zobert's terrified expression and felt a pang of emotion.

Sure enough, once you have power, you want to stop eating beef.

*Next, I suggest you close your eyes and don't look.*

Su Mu reminded Alice's consciousness, then extended a finger pointing at Zobert, then pointed downwards to the ground.

Before Zobert could understand the meaning of Su Mu's gesture, his body was riddled with countless holes.

To prevent a possible counterattack later, Su Mu further expanded the space containing his limbs.

Then his limbs were "ripped" off.

"Ah!!!"

A heart-wrenching scream echoed within the space.

Outside the space, Su Mu controlled Edward to pick up the severed arm and reattach it to the wound, then brought the wound close to the edge of the domain's black mist, allowing it to adhere to the wound.

A few minutes later, the severed arm was reattached.

Su Mu glanced at the wall of black mist from the outside.

The range wasn't actually that large, only about 10 meters.

But this wasn't the limit; Su Mu had simply stopped expanding the black mist after confirming that Zobert was completely enveloped.

Just now, given how much he expanded the space, even if he had used the space behind Zobert to contract and control the size of the black mist, it couldn't have been limited to only 50 meters.

The truth is, the space shrouded in the black mist had been detached from reality.

Su Mu's black mist directly separated a section of space from the real world, and then he could freely control that space.

Therefore, from the outside, the size of the black mist appeared unchanged.

Then, the form of the black mist became unstable, began to shrink, and was finally absorbed entirely by the black mist ring behind Alice's head.

However, the land stripped away by the black mist fared much worse. After the black mist disappeared, Su Mu forgot to restore the spatial changes, causing some land to remain torn apart, while other land was crowded together.

The crowded parts were further squeezed together with the land that had originally been outside the black mist's embrace.

Now, beneath Alice's feet was a loose, cracked ground, and 50 meters away was a ring-shaped mound of earth.

In front of Alice was Zobert, whose limbs had been "torn" off. He was now completely unconscious, but the powerful life force granted by the [Cup] kept him clinging to life even in this state.

*Lord of the Mist, may you open your eyes now?*

Alice was very obedient; she had kept her eyes closed ever since Su Mu told her to.



Chapter 32 Welcome Gift


*It's best to wait a bit longer; it's a bit gory now.*

After speaking, Su Mu reached behind his head and brushed his hand across the ring, then released a black mist that enveloped Zobert and his severed limbs.

Although he couldn't control it when he was conscious, Su Mu could control it now in this near-death state.

However, it was a pity that if Zobert fully recovered, he wouldn't be able to control it anymore, but given Zobert's current condition, a full recovery seemed impossible. His internal organs were almost all shattered; he had previously been clinging to life only due to his physical constitution, and now he was only being kept alive by Su Mu's black mist. Su Mu only needed to control the power of the black mist to maintain this state.

After a quick review of his memories, Su Mu lost interest in him.

There was basically no useful information, only the reason for following him. Was he really just a small fry?

Using him as bait to lure out a secret society, what a pity, he never used bait.

It wasn't a complete loss, though; he now knew the names and addresses of the other two members of the Holy Communion Church.

However, the mentor remained shrouded in mystery; it seemed only Ens knew his address.

Was that mentor the leader of the Holy Communion Church?

Probably the branch leader?

These [cups] matched Su Mu's expectations.

They were all serial killers.

The most important memory in Zobert's mind was the scene of him killing his parents with his own hands.

And this was the Holy Communion Church's initiation ceremony.

Ed raised his right hand and glanced at his watch.

7:41.

Seven minutes? Looks like we've been playing for a while.

The entire battle actually only lasted a little over a minute.

The rest of the time was spent Su Mu testing his new ability, or rather, testing the effects of his original ability in the real world.

Overall, it works very well.

The current maximum range should cover a space with a radius of 100 meters.

*Alright, you can open your eyes now. Your body is back to you.*

After finishing his telepathic communication with Alice, Su Mu returned control of the body to her.

Alice blinked, as if waking from a dream.

Then she covered her mouth and ran to the nearby bushes to vomit.

The smell of blood was too strong…

She vomited for a while before staggering back.

Then she saw Zobert standing in front of the car, straightening his tattered clothes, while Edward even pulled a new suit from the trunk.

Why are there things like that in the car?

And how did this guy come back to life… Oh, so he was controlled by the Lord of the Mist. That's not surprising.

—

7:50 AM.

Glenner stood before the building bearing the sign "Rose Security Company," his heart pounding with trepidation.

"Hmph...this is a direct descendant of the Royal Family. Can someone like me, with no status or background, really work here?"

"Speaking of which, isn't joining this kind of superhuman organization dangerous?"

Glenner suddenly thought of the mysterious entity surrounding him and felt a pang of fear.

"If these superhumans are all this terrifying, won't I die on my very first mission?"

*"Hello, can you hear me?"*

Su Mu's voice appeared directly in Glenner's mind, making him shudder.

*"Yes, I can hear you. What are your orders?"*

"You're not going to make me gather intelligence right after I joined the company, are you?"

*Here's a small welcome gift. Do you know the Holy Communion Church? One of their bases is at 134 Berg Street in the West District. Three buildings around it are their bases. There are three important members in the church: a man named Ens, a woman named Miria, and a man named Zobert.*

Su Mu directly told Glenner Ens's address, the three's extraordinary levels, Ens and Miria's extraordinary abilities, and the existence of their mentor all at once.

*In short, Ens and Zobert's [Cup] pathway is at the eighth level, mainly focusing on physical enhancement. Miria, although also at the eighth level [Cup], seems to have control-type abilities, very mysterious. Ens and Zobert have no idea about her specific abilities.*

*Got it?*

*I...I got it...*

*Very good. With this information, I guarantee you'll impress them.*

Glenner stood there blankly for two minutes after Su Mu's voice disappeared.

It wasn't Su Mu's information that shocked him.

It was because he actually knew nothing about the Hidden World, and had never even heard of the Holy Communion Church.

In Glenner's mind, there was only one church in Prit: the Church of the Holy Light of Salvation.

But the information he had just heard from Su Mu made him feel that his progress in digesting the potion had increased dramatically, even giving him the feeling that he was about to be promoted!

It had been over two years since he drank the potion, and the progress he had made in solving all the cases combined was still less than the progress he had made from the information he had just heard!

The progress he had made in finding the murderer was less than the "welcome gift" given to him by this mysterious being?!

Glenner felt a mixture of fear and awe towards Su Mu.

A small welcome gift was so significant; if he proved his usefulness, would he be able to get something even better...?

With these thoughts in mind, Glenner entered the Rose Security Company.

Before he could say anything, a woman walked towards him.

The woman, dressed in a suit, had long brown hair tied in a ponytail.

“You must be Mr. Glenner?”

“Yes, that’s me.”

“Then please follow me.”

The woman turned and led the way, explaining as they walked, “You’ve probably noticed, the security company is just a front. We’re not actually dealing with ordinary armed thugs.”

“We’re dealing with other superhumans, right?”

Glenner followed, observing the interior of the building.

The tiles were pristine white, the walls clean, and he even saw a novelty—electric lights—on the ceiling.

No wonder they work for royalty; the decor alone is impressive.

“That’s right. We’re dealing with dangerous individuals lurking in the shadows, threatening the lives of ordinary residents of Plitt.”

This sounds like she’s talking about that mysterious entity within me… but it’s not the same. At least I haven’t witnessed that mysterious entity killing ordinary people; the only confirmed victim was a murderer.

The woman paused for a moment, then continued, "So I'd like to confirm first, Mr. Glenner, are you really planning to join us?"

"Yes."

Glenner didn't hesitate for a moment.

If he were to consider it, he might hesitate for a moment, but now, he didn't know what kind of punishment he would face if he dared to hesitate.

Joining the Security Bureau was a mission given by that mysterious entity.

The woman glanced at Glenner, her admiration for his decisive attitude growing stronger.

On the way, she introduced him to some extraordinary knowledge, filling in the gaps in his extraordinary development, such as pathways and the existence of multiple pathways under each principle.

The extraordinary pathways under the [Cup] principle, for example, were the most numerous as they currently knew.

At the same time, not every pathway had a complete sequence of levels from low to high.

Some pathways only had high levels, some only low levels, but under the same principle, different pathways could be converted into each other.

And those extraordinary individuals who didn't understand the extraordinary well often mistook principles for major pathways, but the two concepts were actually not the same.

Glenner felt somewhat embarrassed; he was one of those who mistook principles for pathways.

However, he concealed his embarrassment well, and since the woman didn't turn around to look at him, he probably hadn't been noticed.

The two entered the reception room at the back.

A scruffy-bearded man sat in the main seat of the reception room.



Chapter 33 Onboarding


"Welcome, please have a seat."

The man nodded to Glenna, then gestured to the chair in front of him, before saying to the woman, "You've worked hard, Vivian. Go home and rest early."

"Then, Captain, I'll head back now."

Vivian gave a slight bow in farewell, closing the door to the reception room before leaving.

Glenna didn't say much, sitting down in the chair the man had indicated.

The two sat facing each other across the coffee table.

Glenna was now somewhat nervous.

So this is the captain of the Security Bureau?
