Blake instinctively wanted to stop Glenn from breaking down when he saw his actions, but then remembered that this was a sealed artifact; it couldn't be that easily damaged.

Blake was now more worried about Glenn's mental state than the sealed artifact itself.

He'd never heard of anyone's mental state suddenly improving while containing a sealed artifact, and the white light he'd just seen was also suspicious.

He had a very ominous thought.

Could Glenn have been possessed?

Although, according to the headquarters' records, the [Guardian Angel] absolutely couldn't possess such an ability.

Could it be that some kind of spiritual being was already attached to Glenn, and it just happened to resonate with the sealed artifact?

His gaze fell on the portable lamp beside his seat.

No unusual spirituality.

Then what was going on?

Dylant, who had been standing to the side, intending to see how Blake would clean up the mess, frowned when he suddenly stopped, seemingly unfazed.

“Aren’t you going in to rescue your newly recruited guy? Captain, what, is that guy who looks like your son dead inside and you’re just going to ignore him? I will report everything that happened today to my superiors.”

Blake looked at Dylant again, his gaze fierce.

"Ever since I joined the Security Bureau, I've been treated like a beast of burden, I don't even have a wife, let alone offspring!!"

Suppressing the urge to teach Dylant a lesson, Blake decided that checking on Glenna's condition was more important.

"I'll take Glenna to the Church of the Holy Light for a thorough examination later."

Thinking this, Blake pushed open the door.

Then he saw in the interrogation room that the [Guardian Angel] that Glenna had tossed into the air was floating in mid-air.

Cracks appeared on the sculpture's surface, emanating golden light.

Then, with a "crack," the sculpture's surface completely peeled away, revealing its inner form: a faceless, two-winged angel.

No longer a sculpture, it was like a real, living being, its wings fluttering in mid-air.

Vivian's eyes widened, her mouth agape.

Edric was the same; he rubbed his eyes again, wondering if he hadn't recovered from being blinded by the light.

"Perfect containment?!"

Blake couldn't help but exclaim.

Those wings... he'd never seen them before.

That was definitely a change that only perfect containment could produce, something even he hadn't achieved.

Perfect containment meant exceeding the requirements of a sealed object during containment, and satisfying the object itself.

It was only possible because many sealed objects didn't even have the option of exceeding those requirements, like the [Guardian Angel].

The containment condition was to remain conscious for 24 hours under the influence of delirium.

Once the time was up, containment was considered successful; there was no possibility of exceeding those requirements.

The Security Bureau didn't even have a clear answer regarding the possibility of perfect containment for such sealed objects.

But today, he had a clear answer.

Yes.

Edric was also taken aback by Blake's words.

Although he'd mostly forgotten his knowledge of the Security Bureau, he still remembered this basic common sense.

The captain had even mentioned this during a dinner gathering once.

The captain had said he only managed to contain the [Guardian Angel] at the seventh rank, and even then, it was just a normal containment.

"Perfect containment?"

Vivian couldn't help but express her surprise.

Her understanding of sealed artifacts came from internal records; she had never seen one in person, and there was no record of a perfect containment of a sealed artifact like the [Guardian Angel].

Until now, she had only assumed it was a normal containment success, but the captain was telling her it was actually a perfect containment!

Vivian looked at Glenner with a newfound respect.

This ninth-rank transcendent being, who, based on the timeline, might have entered the transcendent realm a year later than her, not only completely outclassed her in intelligence gathering abilities but had even achieved perfect containment...

And it was a perfect containment that had never been recorded in the department before...

Vivian felt that she, as a senior, might never be able to hold her head high in front of this newcomer, Glenner.

"Perfect containment? That's impossible!"

Dylant's astonished voice echoed in the room.

He didn't believe Blake's words.

What a joke! How could a ninth-tier being achieve perfect containment?

Blake was definitely making things up to avoid punishment from headquarters!

"What a joke! Successfully containing a ninth-tier artifact is already a low-probability event.

And this is perfect containment, especially a perfect containment of a sealed object like the [Guardian Angel].

This is simply impossible!"

Dylant could no longer remain calm. He walked straight to the interrogation room, squeezing past Blake.

Then he saw pure white wings fluttering in mid-air.

His mouth dropped open.



Chapter 44 Getting Anxious


It's true...

It was truly perfect containment...

Dylant was certain that this wasn't the kind of scene one would expect from ordinary containment.

He was the one who personally witnessed Black contain the sealed artifact.

Black didn't react like this after his containment.

It wasn't even floating, let alone coming back to life.

Dylant felt a surge of inexplicable anger as he looked at Glenna, who was curiously observing the [Guardian Angel].

What a joke! This sealed artifact should have been his!

It deserved his contribution and seniority!

But Black kept blocking him, preventing him from attempting containment!

Instead, it was given to some newcomer who appeared out of nowhere!

He couldn't accept this outcome!

A voice echoed in his mind.

These things that should have been yours have been taken away by someone else. Are you willing to accept this?

No, how could he possibly accept it?

Then why not take it back himself?

"Yeah, you have to fight for your own interests..."

Completely unaware of anything amiss, Dylant raised his hand, palm facing the table in front of Glenn.

Under his supernatural ability, the previously cold metal tabletop suddenly heated up, its surface glowing with an orange-yellow light.

Then, that orange-yellow area bulged into a cone shape, aiming straight for Glenn's heart!

Wait, what is Dylant doing?!

Edric already sensed something was wrong when he saw the orange-yellow tabletop. He stood up and threw a punch, intending to smash the one-way glass in front of him.

He had worked with Dylant on several missions and immediately recognized it as the result of his supernatural abilities.

But before he could speak, the cone, deformed by the intense heat, was already at Glenn's chest, and Glenn's jacket was charred into ashes from the heat.

The tip of the cone continued its advance, about to pierce Glenn's heart in the next second.

But things didn't go as everyone expected.

A flash of golden light appeared, a faint golden aura protecting Glenner's chest, blocking the red-hot tip.

The originally sharp tip tried to break through this golden light, but ultimately only flattened itself.

No matter how hard it tried, it couldn't go any further.

Upon seeing the tabletop deform, Blake rushed towards Delant, intending to subdue him.

Now, saving Glenner was impossible; he could only try to control Delant to prevent his supernatural abilities from causing Glenner further harm.

Blake now regretted letting Delant in.

"I finally found a treasure; if you hold a grudge against our Security Bureau because of this, who will take responsibility?!"

Bang!

Delant was punched directly in the face by Blake, falling to the ground. His previously fierce eyes regained some of their luster due to the pain.

He was about to look up and glare at Blake when Blake pressed his head down onto the ground with his palm.

"Dylant, are you crazy?! Do you know what you're doing?!"

Dylant's head was pinned to the floor, unable to move, the pressure causing cracks to appear in the ground.

At the same time, a chill ran from Blake's palm directly to his head.

A bone-chilling cold instantly cleared his head.

Then, intense fear filled his mind.

What had I done?!

He wasn't afraid because he had almost harmed his teammates, but because of the lingering fear for his own life.

He had actually attacked a fellow member of the Security Bureau under the influence of his delirium.

If headquarters found out, he wouldn't just be locked up; he'd likely be deemed a threat and eliminated!

The Security Bureau was very strict about internal conflicts.

Because of the potion's corrosive effects, any unusual situation could be suspected of being caused by it.

And the potion's corrosive effects were irreversible; at best, they could only be alleviated.

And for ordinary members like them, once they were deemed severely corroded by the potion, they faced only one fate:

Killed by the Inquisition.

The potion extracted from their bodies would be recycled and reused.

Dylant knew his situation.

He just now perfectly fit the Security Bureau's definition of a severely potion-induced state.

Unable to control his emotions, influenced by the potion's hypnotic pronouncements, he acted extremely.

It's over.

Thinking of the possibility of being killed by the Inquisition in the future, Dylant became completely still.

No, there's still a chance. He just needs to explain that he was only like this because of the immense pressure he's been under lately.

He needs to plead with Captain Black!

He's the one who recommended me; he'll definitely protect me!

Besides, headquarters is so far away. As long as the captain doesn't report it, I'll be fine!

Thinking this, Dylant immediately put on a terrified expression.

"Yes, Captain, I... I was like this because I've been creating extraordinary items every day lately. Captain, this wasn't my intention!"

As he spoke, Dylant seemed to have successfully convinced himself, and his confidence returned.

“I just… I just wanted to test the difference between perfect containment and normal containment, yes, that’s it! Captain, he’s perfectly contained, I knew he couldn’t be hurt by me, I just wanted to test…”

Before Dylant could finish speaking, half his head was forcefully shoved into the ground.

Black’s eyes were icy, his tone cold: “Shut up, go to the solitary confinement cell. I’ll tell Headquarters everything you did today.”

Then Black shouted towards the interrogation room: “Vivian, bring the handcuffs!”

Vivian, outside the door, realized what was happening, swallowed hard, and said nervously: “Shh, it’s the Captain!”

She then ran out of the room.

Although she disliked Dylant’s personality, she never imagined he would dare to attack their teammates so blatantly.

Edric, meanwhile, remained in a punching stance, but the glass in front of him only cracked slightly.

He was, after all, a combatant; his eyesight and reflexes were far superior to Vivian's, a mere clerk. Seeing his captain move, he knew Glenn wouldn't be seriously injured, so he stopped exerting himself halfway through.

He stared at the cracks in the glass in front of him, lost in thought.

Another pay cut...

While the two junior officers had calmed down somewhat, Dylant, pinned down by Blake, wasn't in such a good mood.

Upon hearing Blake's demand to report the truth, he immediately struggled again.

"Yes, Captain! You can't do this! You're the one who recommended me. If something happens to me, you'll be suspended too!"

To prevent Blake from reporting, Dylant's words even carried a hint of threat.

Blake felt like he was about to laugh in exasperation at Dylant.

"If I hadn't saved you from those superhumans corrupted by the potion, would you be where you are today? Now you dare to threaten me!"

The spiritual energy of [Winter] on his hand became even more violent.

Frost began to appear on half of Dylant's head, which he was holding.

"You don't need to worry about me."



Chapter 45 The Seal Exploded


The catalyst for this incident, Glenner...

is still unconscious.

After all, Su Mu's method of controlling him, rather than a believer's, was simple and brutal—direct brain control.

Therefore, the catalyst should be considered Su Mu.

Su Mu was somewhat surprised to see the golden angel floating 50cm in front of him.

He had secretly learned many hidden common sense principles, although the very fact that these principles were still common sense felt strange to him.

But he also knew that the state of this sealed object was not as simple as ordinary containment.

Moreover, Blake behind him had just loudly shouted the words "perfect containment."

Clearly, with the intervention of a god like himself, the containment ritual, which should have taken 24 hours to complete, was finished in less than a quarter of the time.

To be honest, he was also startled when he first arrived.

Originally, he had planned to directly take over his consciousness and then endure the ordeal himself; after all, as a god with 30 million years of experience in solitude, he considered 24 hours to be child's play.

And there was no need to worry about being distracted and causing delays. He was adept at multitasking; he even had the werewolves roaming the forest to prevent any hunters from accidentally wandering into his territory.

Just as he shifted his focus, thinking it was a rare opportunity to have some fun with the sculpture,

the sculpture suddenly exploded, giving him a high-level flashbang. Luckily, it wasn't his own body, and turning off the touch made it feel nothing.

But the unexpected situation still made Su Mu a little nervous. What if the angel sculpture realized he wasn't human and was planning to destroy him?

Su Mu was fine, but Glenner was in a bad way. The black mist Su Mu left on him couldn't provide any comprehensive enhancement.

If he came to help, and even assured Glenner he could rest assured, but ended up hindering him and ending up in the hospital, then he, as a god, would lose face.

Fortunately, the angel wasn't planning to destroy Su Mu; instead, it seemed more like a puppy that had found its master and was eager to follow him.

And there was something very strange.

Su Mu's gaze fell upon the fragments of the sculptures scattered throughout the room.

It wasn't just the outer layer that had peeled off.

Su Mu could tell the fragments were solid, meaning it wasn't a case of perfect containment causing the outer layer to peel away and reveal the true form underneath.

It had genuinely exploded, and the glowing object now floating in front of him...

Although he couldn't see it clearly, when the white light appeared, Su Mu seemed to see a spatial distortion at the original location of the sculpture, and then this glowing angel appeared.

It was very subtle; if Su Mu hadn't witnessed spatial distortion before, he might have thought it was a hallucination.

So, this glowing angel and the sealed artifact called the "Guardian Angel" weren't actually the same thing?

He'd just arrived and already destroyed someone's most valuable asset...

Su Mu ignored the now-cooled metal tabletop resting on his chest and turned to look at Black.

Black was currently coldly suppressing Dylant.

That should be its name.

Blake, noticing Su Mu's gaze, looked at him with apologetic eyes.

"I'm so sorry, encountering something like this so soon after joining the company. Don't worry, I'll give this guy the punishment he deserves. Are you alright?"

Blake tried to soften his tone, hoping Glenn wouldn't become wary of their Security Bureau because of this.

Thinking of this, Blake angrily increased the pressure on his hand.

Damn it, I have to clean up your mess!

Su Mu shook his head and calmly replied, "I'm fine."

Then he looked away from the writhing Dylant on the ground and Blake pressing down on his head.

It seemed he hadn't noticed the spatial rift.

Su Mu looked at the glowing angel, who seemed to want to approach but also somewhat disgusted, with a puzzled expression.

Is this sealed artifact a sucker for good looks?

But Glenn isn't exactly ugly, is he?

And your appearance is just too…abstract!

The angel radiating golden light wasn't humanoid, but rather a single eyeball with two pairs of wings.

While somewhat rude, to be honest, it felt a little disgusting…

Unable to understand it, Su Mu decided to stop thinking about it and simply returned the body to Glenna.

Anyway, he'd done what he needed to do.

After releasing the control, Su Mu engaged in a mental conversation with him.

*The containment is complete. I'm going back now.*

Glenna's consciousness had barely awakened when Su Mu's voice rang in his head. Before he could even feel happy, he suddenly curled up and covered his eyes.

What happened?

My eyes...why do they hurt so much!

Seeing Glenna like this, Su Mu, carrying the clean-clothed Ens, instinctively looked away.

"Haha, this stable is really something..."

"Glenner, are you really alright? I'll get you some medical personnel."

As he spoke, Blake called out to Edric, who was still outside the interrogation room, "Edric, have the medical team come and check on Glenner."

"Yes, sir!"

Edric didn't hesitate and ran out immediately.

"N-no, I'm fine, just my eyes are a little sore..."

Could this be a side effect of being possessed by that mysterious being?

It's never happened before, but changing the sealing artifact every now and then for eye pain seems like a better deal for me.

That mysterious man is truly a benevolent person.

Glenner thought to himself, rubbing his eyes.

At this moment, Blake, who was monitoring Glenner's condition, suddenly noticed something unusual.

"Glenner, where did the [Guardian Angel] you just contained go?"

?

"How would I know? This wasn't something I contained..."

Glenner rubbed his eyes, finally opening them a crack after a long while, and instinctively pointed to the spot where the sealed artifact had originally been in his memory.

"It's right here..."

As his vision cleared slightly, he fell silent.

Aside from a strange cone-shaped object that had appeared out of nowhere, there was no trace of the sealed artifact on the table in front of him.

Glenner tensed up immediately, straining his aching eyes to scan them back and forth.

Then he noticed the fragments on the floor beside him.

...

*Sir! The sealed artifact... is broken?!*

Glenner panicked. He'd thought that although his eyes hurt a little, he'd gained a sealed artifact, but when he opened his eyes, where was the sealed artifact? There were only fragments left!

Sir, are you trying to make me take the blame?!

Meanwhile, Su Mu, located on a remote street in the southern district of Dex City, was also silent.

He knew where the sealed artifact had gone.

His black pupils stared at the golden angel floating before him, and Su Mu was unusually confused.

The golden angel pounced on Su Mu's face upon seeing him.

What does this mean?



Chapter 46: Acquiring a Spiritual Puppy?


The eyeball was pressed against Su Mu's right cheek, and he could feel the eerie sensation on his skin.

Not only was the eyeball pressed against Su Mu, but the angel's two pairs of wings were also caressing his face, making him feel a little itchy.

"Damn it."

Su Mu realized what was happening and took a few steps back, but the eyeball remained pressed against his right cheek.

Suppressing his nausea, Su Mu used his right thumb and forefinger to pinch one of the still-flapping wings and forcefully pulled it off his face.

The eyeball was clearly unhappy.

Although it sounded bizarre,

the eyeball was staring at him with pitiful eyes, while the other three wings seemed quite dissatisfied and continued to flap, while the one Su Mu was pinching was very quiet.

It didn't move at all, like a dog being petted on the head by its owner...

Although describing an eyeball as a dog was strange, Su Mu felt that this was the only description he could think of when he saw it like this.

Then, as if realizing that further dissatisfaction was futile, the other three wings stopped flapping and instead wrapped around Su Mu's thumb and forefinger, rubbing against him.

Su Mu couldn't take it anymore and flung it away.

Realizing he was still on the street, he looked around.

Fortunately, this wasn't in the city center; no one was out at this hour. Just to be safe, Su Mu led Ensi into a side alley.

The eyeball Su Mu flung away rolled twice in mid-air before coming to a stop, its eyes filled with even more grievance. Seeing Su Mu leave, it immediately followed.

As soon as Su Mu entered the alley, the eyeball pounced on his face again, but this time it didn't linger; it just rubbed against him briefly before moving away.

"What are you?"

Not an insult, Su Mu was genuinely curious how this thing could transfer with his consciousness.

But Su Mu quickly realized he'd wasted his time asking.

Could Su Mu really expect an eyeball to answer him?

The eyeball tilted its head...

Did an eyeball really have a head?

Anyway, the entire eyeball tilted to the side a few degrees, its eyes filled with confusion.

*S-Sir, this sealed artifact wasn't...damaged by you, was it?*

Glenner's voice came through, sounding very nervous; he was probably already drenched in sweat.

*Absorbed by you.*

Su Mu decided to employ the traditional skill of an evil god—no, an Outer God—by only saying half of what he meant.

When I controlled you, I absorbed you; isn't that the same as you absorbing you?

Su Mu thought it made perfect sense.

Meanwhile, Glenner, who was drenched in sweat inside the An Yin Bureau, was startled upon hearing Su Mu's voice, then breathed a sigh of relief.

He mustered his courage and said, "I absorbed it."

Blake, who was somewhat puzzled, and Dylant, who was lying on the ground with half his head frozen and nearly unconscious, showed different opinions after hearing Glenn's answer.

Blake simply found it amazing.

In his 15 years working for the Security Bureau, this was the first time he had seen a sealed object be directly absorbed into his body after being contained.

So, sealed objects like the [Guardian Angel] have this ability after perfect containment?

It's definitely much stronger than ordinary containment.

A normally contained [Guardian Angel] must be held in one's hand to block a fatal wound; otherwise, its ability won't activate.

And having one hand free to hold it will significantly reduce your ability to fight head-on.

There's a record within the An Yin Bureau of a senior soldier who had his right hand completely severed while trying to use the [Guardian Angel], resulting in his death.

With perfect containment, it's directly inside the body, so there's no need to worry about that.

But Dylant didn't believe it; he wanted to drag Glenner down with him.

Pressed to the ground, he also noticed the fragments of the sealed artifact and immediately shouted, "You're lying! Glenner, you clearly destroyed the sealed artifact!"

Blake frowned.

"Dylant, use your pig brain! A ninth-tier transcendent being destroyed a sealed artifact capable of withstanding a full-force attack from a seventh-tier offensive pathway? And when you were acting crazy just now, the [Guardian Angel] activated its power. I think you were corrupted by the potion."

"I wasn't corrupted by the potion! It must be because he has the power left behind by a seventh-tier or even sixth-tier transcendent being!"

Glenner and Su Mu, who was eavesdropping through Glenner, both instinctively straightened their backs.

Damn it, how did this guy find out? No, no, it must be a bluff, right? Even the captain didn't notice the mysterious presence on me, he shouldn't have been able to...

Damn it, how come these people always manage to guess me? You guys should open it, right?

Glenner looked at the captain, only to find that he was silent for a second before retorting, "Ridiculous, if he had other spiritual remnants on him, how could the Security Bureau's lights not have reacted?"

Captain! Why were you silent!

Glenner panicked. Although his current behavior made him seem completely innocent, he felt guilty.

Blake was indeed convinced for a moment. While the portable lantern issued by the Security Bureau had the ability to detect spirituality, due to technical limitations, it could only detect five types of spirituality: Cup, Moth, Forged, Winter, and Blade. The other three—Heart, Awakening, and Lamp—remained undetectable.

However, Blake quickly realized that none of these three principles could leave power on someone else, let alone remotely activate and destroy a sealed artifact. Besides, he had personally witnessed the sealed artifact unleash its power; it was clearly Dylant acting erratically.

But Glenner couldn't see Blake's inner thoughts. He could only nervously turn to Su Mu for help.

*Sir, we seem to be suspected!*

Su Mu was also somewhat troubled. The black mist around Glenner couldn't detect his emotions. He didn't know if Blake had spoken sincerely or if he had decided to ignore Glenner's abnormality out of anger at Dylant.

The former is manageable, but if it's the latter, forget about getting Glenn access to the An Yin Bureau's important intelligence in the future.

Su Mu pondered for two seconds and decided to use the simplest method.

Just send the sealed artifact back to make an appearance, and that's it.

Although this eyeball looks completely different from the female angel sculpture, at least its abilities are the same.

Besides, maybe the true form of an angel looks like this?

Thinking this, Su Mu looked at the eyeball.

Upon seeing Su Mu's gaze on it, the eyeball immediately perked up. Although it lacked a waist, it seemed to straighten its back, its eyes looking at him with bright, piercing eyes.

It had an air of "please give me your orders."

"Can...you fly back?"

He saw it blink.

Even more eerie.

The whites of its eyes were normal, but its pupils blinked and dilated twice, just like a human blink.

For the first time, Su Mu truly felt why spiritual beings were corrupted.

Watching it for too long made him feel a little disoriented, although he didn't have any other thoughts besides a bit of disgust.

Then he saw tears streaming down the sides of its eyes!



Chapter 47 Group Mental Pollution


"You don't even have eyelids, where are these tears coming from?!"

Su Mu was deeply shocked to see the eyeball crying so pitifully.

"Why are you crying?"

Su Mu instinctively wanted to comfort it, but didn't know what to do.

The eyeball pressed against Su Mu's face, and then an idea popped into Su Mu's head.

*Don't want to...go...leave...sad.*

It wasn't a complete sentence, just a thought.

But Su Mu understood; it just didn't want him to send it away?

Although he didn't know why the eyeball seemed so determined to follow him, Su Mu still went along with its thoughts, saying, "I won't send you away, just show your face."

*Really?*

"Really."

Only after receiving Su Mu's assurance did the eyeball finally recover from its pitiful state, giving him a reluctant look before disappearing.

The way it disappeared was similar to the way it appeared, both involving a momentary distortion of space.

It's a space-type?

Su Mu rested his chin on his hand, pondering.

Suddenly, I don't find it so repulsive anymore. Although it looks abstract, its mechanism is strong!

I've rarely seen a weak spatial being.

—

Inside the An Yin Bureau on the other side.

Even though he had almost lost all his strength, Dylant was still struggling.

"Let me go! Blake, you actually colluded with a spy from the Secret Society and let them destroy important assets of the An Yin Bureau! Do you know what the consequences will be if headquarters finds out?!"

Blake was so annoyed by Dylant's nagging that he even wanted to just knock him unconscious.

At this moment, the golden light reappeared in the interrogation room.

Blake and Dylant looked up and saw it was that eyeball.

Previously, his attention was either shocked by the perfect containment or completely focused on controlling Dylant. Now, he seriously examined this perfectly contained [Guardian Angel].

...It has a very distinctive appearance.

Blake only looked at it for a while before feeling dizzy, as if little people were talking inside his head.

He shook his head abruptly, snapping out of his daze.

He'd only been watching for a moment, yet he'd already been mentally affected!

No wonder it's called Perfect Containment; now it can not only save lives but also weaken the opponent's condition.

Blake, alert, looked at the eyeball again.

Although he still felt a sound in his head, it wasn't nearly affecting his consciousness like before.

A seventh-tier cultivator could defend against it if they paid attention, but in actual combat, a moment of carelessness could lead to total defeat.

It's a divine skill of information asymmetry!

Blake's gaze lingered on Glenner's face; the more he looked, the more he thought the young man was truly handsome, radiating numerical charm.

A ninth-tier cultivator can achieve this level; I can't even imagine an eighth-tier cultivator!

Meanwhile, Dylant, pinned to the ground, couldn't detect the problem like Blake.

His resolve was shattered. He had been frantically trying to pinpoint Glenna as the culprit who had destroyed the seal, but Glenna, without a word, summoned the seal, once again humiliating him.

He looked up and felt a piercing buzzing in his head.

It felt like cat claws scratching glass, sending shivers down his spine.

Weakling…

Loser…

Trash…

A cacophony of voices seemed to be hurling insults and slander at him.

It wasn't me, it's all his fault!

If he weren't alive, how could I have...?

Dylant stared intently at the eyeball, his eyes bloodshot, an unstable spiritual aura emanating from him once more.

Blake felt a weariness in his heart as he sensed the renewed spiritual energy emanating from Dylant.

“You'd better lie still right now. If you move again, I have the right to cripple you!”

But Dylant remained unmoved, still staring intently at the eyeball, his body beginning to struggle.

Blake noticed this and immediately said to Glenner, “Glenner, put the sealing artifact back!”

But Glenner didn't react; his eyes were fixed blankly on the eyeball.

Blake's words faltered upon seeing this.

So, you're not immune to the influence either?!

Blake's headache worsened. He was about to freeze Dylant solid and rescue Glenna when his eyes suddenly vanished.

Glenna, recovering from his daze, blinked blankly, then paused, looking at Blake apologetically, "Sorry, Captain, I can't control it very well yet."

Blake didn't blame him but comforted him, "It's normal to not be able to control it well at only level nine."

Level nine, being able to both protect himself and control it, was already enough. Although it would likely control himself as well, it was fine; fights in the Security Bureau were always group brawls.

"Captain, the handcuffs are here!"

Vivian shouted as she ran.

Before Blake could give any instructions, she had already nimbly reached the two and handcuffed Dylant.

As the handcuffs were on, the spiritual energy in Dylant instantly dissipated, and he lost strength and fainted.

Blake's open mouth closed again as he glanced at Vivian.

"You haven't been waiting for this day for very long, have you?"

Vivian, on the other hand, was beaming with excitement, the smile on her lips impossible to hide.

Feels good. She used to be constantly ordered around writing reports by Dylant; finally, she'd given her a chance for revenge.

As Vivian got up, she pretended to accidentally step on Dylant's thigh.

Dylant's unconscious body twitched briefly before going still.

Glenner: ......

Blake: ......

Glenner had been almost completely unconscious the whole time, having no idea who was lying on the ground. He only saw the Security Bureau captain pressing down on his head, and Vivian handcuffing him.

He must be an enemy...

To break the silence, Glenner spoke first: "Mr. Blake, what are these handcuffs?"

He had also sensed the spiritual energy emanating from Dylant, so he was curious about these handcuffs that could eliminate the spiritual energy of supernatural beings.

"A spiritual item produced by a sealed artifact from Headquarters. Its effect is to seal the extraordinary abilities of those at or below the eighth rank."

As he spoke, Blake stood up and lifted Dylant.

"Put this guy in solitary confinement first. Further instructions will follow from Headquarters."

"Yes, sir!"

Vivian excitedly took Dylant and dragged him out by the back of his collar.

Blake: ......

Glenner: ......

"Ahem, congratulations! Glenner has become the youngest sealed artifact holder in our department!"

Blake coughed twice to draw Glenner's attention back, then walked up to him and patted him on the shoulder.

"I have to thank Mr. Blake for giving me this opportunity."

"Haha, don't call me 'Mr.' From now on, we're a team. Everyone gets opportunities, but geniuses who can contain ninth-tier sealed artifacts are rare. To celebrate our An Yin Bureau gaining such a genius, I'll treat you to a meal after get off work!"

Glenner keenly sensed something was amiss.

"Captain, is it generally impossible to contain sealed artifacts at the ninth tier?"

"..."

Seeing Glenner's questioning gaze, Blake felt a little guilty.

Damn it, his original purpose of just testing Glenner's mental strength seems to have been exposed!



Chapter 48 Harassing Phone Calls


Ultimately, thanks to Edric's intervention, Black successfully evaded Glenn's questioning.

Although he didn't know why, Edric's immediate apology left him somewhat bewildered. It was only after the captain's explanation that he realized they hadn't fully trusted the information he'd given the previous night and were wary of him.

Glenn said he understood; if someone suddenly told him they'd found the culprit in a case he'd been searching for for over three years, and provided a bunch of detailed information, he wouldn't believe it either.

After an examination by the medical personnel brought by Edric, Glenn was unharmed and in good physical and mental health.

Glenn didn't press further.

Anyway, he'd successfully contained the sealed artifact, and the captain hadn't asked for it back. Even if the captain hadn't initially intended for him to successfully contain it, the captain had ultimately given it to him generously.

Especially after learning about Dylant from Edric, Glenn no longer had any other feelings for Black.

This guy had already gone against the bureau's second-in-command for his sake, clearly showing favoritism. If he still doubted him, he'd be an ingrate.

However, Glenn was still puzzled. Although everyone said he'd successfully contained the artifact, why didn't he feel any change?

—

Su Mu sneezed and rubbed his nose.

The things Glenn said earlier were what he'd instructed Glenn to say.

Su Mu had originally planned to send the eyeball to support Glenn, but he discovered that the eyeball seemed to have a crowd control aura effect; just looking at it affected one's mind.

He had no choice but to send the eyeball back and relay the message to Glenn.

Although he didn't know the principle behind it, even though Su Mu hadn't left any black mist on the eyeball, he could sense its location and transmit some simple commands.

However, Su Mu still felt a little guilty. He'd said he was helping someone contain a sealed artifact, but it turned into him stealing someone else's sealed artifact. Fortunately, Glenn didn't seem to have any intention of questioning him.

And now he has something even more troubling.

Su Mu sat by the roadside waiting for the carriage. Ens stood beside him like a bodyguard, while Su Mu's eyeballs, hidden under his hood, rubbed against his hair, the feathers tickling the back of his neck.

He felt those eyeballs were definitely shedding hair; his neck was probably already covered in it.

To be honest, he was thinking of throwing them away...

Wouldn't an eyeball stuck directly in his hair be painful?

There was another thing bothering him.

*Lord Su Mu, I just saw a really cute plush toy while shopping. Can I buy it?*

*If you have enough money, you can buy it.*

*Lord Su Mu, what do you think would be a good breakfast for me?*

*Eat whatever you want*

*Lord Su Mu, do you think I look better in red or blue?*

*Good-looking people look good in anything.*

*Lord Su Mu, what's the name of our church? Do you need me to help you spread the word?*

*There's no church, don't spread the word.*

*Lord Su Mu...*

Su Mu felt for the first time that the ability of mind-to-mind communication was not very useful.

Who can stand someone calling you every five minutes?

Su Mu felt that Vanya was like a lonely child who had never made friends before, wanting to tell the first friend everything.

...It seems that's true.

Su Mu remembered Vanya saying she had no friends growing up and was bullied.

Sigh, poor child.

*Su Mu, do we really not have a church? Perhaps I can think of one for you! Come to think of it, a church should ideally be founded by believers like me...*

Bad child.

Should I tell these believers that if they dare to call him more than 10...5 times a day, they'll block him?

Alice is much easier to deal with. Maybe I should send Vanya to school too?

Once she starts school, she definitely won't be able to bother me like this every day. But if I send her to school, how can I let her go to the church as a spy?

*Don't even think about proselytizing. I already warned you not to tell anyone you've seen me.*

*Hmm...okay...*

Vanya's voice sounded aggrieved, but Su Mu didn't intend to change his mind.

*You can just focus on being a nun in the Church of the Holy Light now. When you're not working, you can do whatever you want.*

*Then could I...?*

*Don't come looking for me.*

*Ugh...*

Vanya's voice sounded even more pitiful, almost like she was about to cry.

Su Mu felt she might have gone too far and wanted to add that she just shouldn't look for her too often, but her gaze, passing through the black mist beside Vanya, caught a glimpse of her condition.

Although she seemed a little disappointed, it wasn't obvious, and she maintained the proper demeanor of a nun even when encountering strangers.

Su Mu was speechless.

She's even learned to fake cry now.

Seriously, sister, wasn't your persona supposed to be that of a sensitive and lonely child? How come you've suddenly become so calculating?

As someone from modern society, Su Mu couldn't understand how intense the longing of a believer who had lost their faith and then rediscovered it must be to stand beside God.

—

Vanya, following Su Mu's instructions, was shopping with a smile on her face.

The smile was genuine, for it was a divine instruction; she could find fulfillment simply by following it.

But the inner disappointment was also real; God had rejected her approach.

This truly wounded her; for a moment, Vanya felt abandoned by God.

The intense fear and anxiety made it hard to breathe.

But she quickly recovered.

Her soul had been offered to the divine; her soul would never be separated from the divine…

This sense of fulfillment filled Vanya's entire being; she felt happy and at peace.

“Is this young lady shopping alone?”

A magnetic voice sounded behind Vanya, and she turned to look.

It was a young man who looked no more than 20 years old, wearing a bowler hat, an open black double-breasted Ulster coat revealing a white shirt underneath, a dark blue silk scarf around his neck, and black trousers and leather shoes.

He wore white gloves and carried an ebony cane.

The young man had a gentle smile on his face and seemed very approachable, unlike other nobles who often exuded arrogance.

Vanya glanced at him briefly and got a general impression of him.

He was a wealthy nobleman; she'd seen people dressed like that donating stacks of money when she was cleaning the church.

However, he probably wasn't particularly rich. She'd heard at the church orphanage that true nobles never appeared before ordinary people like them.

Vanya maintained a distance of about one meter from the young man, offering a professional, insincere smile and speaking softly, "Hello, sir, is there anything I can help you with?"

Vanya didn't directly answer the young man's question; he gave her a very uncomfortable feeling.



Chapter 49 [The Moth]'s Extraordinary Being


The young man removed his top hat and gracefully bowed, placing it to his chest.

His slightly wavy, golden hair shimmered in the sunlight.

"Let me introduce myself. My name is Henry West. I would like to invite such a beautiful lady to spend the day with me. Would this lovely lady do me the honor?"

The young man spoke clearly and softly.

His emerald green eyes had an alluring quality.

Passersby stopped to stare. Vanya noticed several girls looking at her with envy, and even a few men?

*Lord Su Mu, Lord Su Mu, someone invited me shopping. Should I accept?*

Vanya showed no delight or flattery at being invited by a young man generally considered elegant and handsome.

Ever since seeing Su Mu's face, everyone else seemed lacking.

Besides, she was destined to become Lord Su Mu's Holy Maiden!

A Holy Maiden must maintain purity for the sake of the gods!

That's what the scriptures say.

However, considering she was reading the scriptures of the Holy Light Church of Salvation, Vanya chose to ask Su Mu first.

Besides, this was a good opportunity to speak with Lord Su Mu!

Su Mu, now seated in the carriage, was somewhat speechless. "I'm not your father," he thought, "you decide this yourself."

*"It's your decision. Go if you like it, don't go if you don't. I don't recommend you go. Don't get kidnapped by human traffickers."*

Although, with him as insurance, if any human traffickers dared to kidnap her, he would make them suffer a fate worse than death.

Vanya felt somewhat disappointed by Su Mu's answer.

"Why didn't Lord Su Mu give me instructions? Why do I have to decide for myself?"

"Without divine guidance, I feel so empty..."

Su Mu could sense Vanya's disappointment, but he didn't intend to interfere.

Children like Vanya needed to have their independence cultivated; her mental state was truly worrying.

He was even afraid that if he died one day, Vanya might die with him.

Although dying was practically impossible given his current situation,

that was his original plan. However, when Su Mu saw Henry's attire through Vania's eyes, he felt something was off.

These clothes didn't look cheap; he was probably a nobleman. Why would a nobleman be with a nun?

Considering Vania was only 16, early romance was no good, especially for a minor nobleman to be with a young girl like Vania.

Damn it!

Su Mu's expression turned serious.

He'd heard that nobles at this time of year had particularly perverse interests. Could he be after Vania?

*Vania, you're still young. It's best not to be with men like that. Nobles are a very dangerous group.*

Lord Su Mu doesn't want me to be with other men!

Lord Su Mu really wants me to be His saintess!

Vania precisely gleaned what she wanted from Su Mu's warning.

A bright smile appeared on her face.

A smile full of vitality and happiness.

Henry froze for a moment when he saw that smile.

He felt it was the most beautiful smile he had ever seen in his life.

Suddenly, he felt a pang of reluctance...

Henry thought to himself.

"Sorry, I'd like to walk around by myself."

Henry's smile froze; he hadn't expected Vania to refuse him after smiling at him.

And in front of so many people, he felt a little embarrassed.

And why could this nun resist his charm?

Henry's eyes sharpened.

She didn't seem like a superhuman. Should he use his powers on her?

Vanya smiled and bowed slightly before turning to leave.

Henry watched Vanya's departing figure, a yellow glint appearing in his emerald eyes.

An elderly man dressed similarly to him approached and respectfully whispered, "Young Master, do you need me to help you deal with her?"

There were too many people; he couldn't use his powers directly. He should let the butler handle it.

Henry adjusted his top hat, offering a friendly smile to the onlookers, while whispering to the butler beside him, "Take her home, alive."

"Yes."

The old man left, quietly following Vanya.

Henry continued chatting with the other women in the crowd.

—

He was the West family's old butler, 67 years old this year.

He was no ordinary old man; he was a transcendent, a transcendent of the [Moth] principle.

His transformation into a transcendent was largely due to the West family's generosity.

The West family had a lineage of transcendents; their ancestors were said to have produced a sixth-tier [Moth].

He himself became a transcendent after helping the West family patriarch evade the Bureau of Investigation in his early years, receiving a potion in return. He had served the West family for over 30 years.

He also secretly helped the family eliminate rivals when the family couldn't be out in the open, or helped the family capture individuals.

He was well aware of the West family's dark side.

The West family secretly worshipped an evil god representing the [Moth], believing that offering sacrifices to him would grant them power.

This isn't surprising. Many families in the hidden world with extraordinary lineages worship evil gods, but most receive no response, so often this faith is merely a means to an end.

But the West family is different. Their god truly brings them miracles. Because of this, they have a strong desire to offer sacrifices to their deity. Even during periods of strict control, the old man would help the West family capture sacrifices.

And the characteristics of the [Moth] gave him the confidence to do so.

Although lacking in direct combat ability, mind control is something most humans cannot defend against.

Ordinary people, unaware of the extraordinary, would be easily defeated on their first encounter.

The old man's gaze fell on Vania.

It seems the young master intends to offer this girl as a sacrifice.

—

Vania had a happy morning. At noon, she was thinking of returning to the church for lunch when an old man blocked her way.

Vanya tried to walk around the old man, but found him moving in the same direction, blocking her path again.

"Sir, is there something you need?"

Vanya asked politely.

The old man didn't speak, but instead looked up and met her gaze.

His pale yellow eyes were fixed on Vanya.

"Please follow me,"

the old man said.

He had deliberately chosen this moment to strike.

There were no pedestrians on the street now; luring Vanya back to be sacrificed wouldn't go unnoticed.

While it was indeed quite audacious to directly abduct nuns from the Holy Light Church of Salvation.

But the old man had his own judgment. This nun seemed naive and lacked the wariness expected of a superhuman.

Furthermore, he had just sent someone to inquire about Vanya's background. Learning that she was merely an orphan adopted by the church in her early years, he felt even more reassured. The Church of the Holy Light wouldn't entrust its precious potions to an orphan.



Chapter 50: Awarding a Third-Class Merit


The old man, emboldened by this news, decided to kidnap her.

Vanya tilted her head, politely declining, "Sir, do you need help? I can take you to church; the priest will answer your questions. I'm currently on leave."

The old man's expression froze, and he looked Vanya up and down in astonishment.

Could this nun have a strong innate mental resistance?

The old man perked up.

If that were the case, then this eighth-tier [Moth] old man would be in real trouble.

Although he had been alive for over 30 years, his mental state prevented him from properly digesting potions; he spent most of his time resisting the corrosive influence of delirium.

His advancement to the eighth tier was entirely due to his family's past favors, which had forced him to do so.

But from another perspective, if this nun truly possessed a strong innate mental resistance, then the miracles bestowed by the gods after her sacrifice would surely be even more numerous…

A hint of longing appeared in the old man's eyes as he looked at Vanya.

Vanya keenly sensed the unnaturalness in the old man's eyes and slowly took a step back.

"Sir, what can I do for you?"

Vanya had already begun planning her escape.

The old man's eyes, like the young man's before, gave her an unsettling feeling.

Just then, Su Mu's voice appeared in her mind.

*Vanya, can you run now? Run south.*

Vanya's heart immediately relaxed.

*Yes, Lord Su Mu.*

Vanya turned and ran without hesitation.

—

Su Mu was simultaneously instructing Vanya to escape and sending a message to Glenner.

*Are you there? Just out on a field mission, I'll give you a third-class merit.*

Glenner felt somewhat exhausted after receiving the message.

He had come to the An Yin Bureau at five in the morning to contain the sealed artifact, nearly driving himself crazy. He had thought Su Mu wouldn't finish until the next day.

But it turned out Su Mu had finished containing it as soon as he arrived, leaving him with a mix of emotions.

On one hand, he was awestruck by Su Mu's strength, and on the other hand, he was worried that he might be eliminated one day when he was no longer needed.

He spent the morning listening to Vivian explain his work to him amidst this internal struggle, and finally managed to get to noon, thinking he could take a nap during his lunch break, when he received another message from Su Mu.

This is much more tiring than being a police officer...

*Okay, I'm coming right away.*

*By the way, you should bring someone with you; I feel like you might not be able to handle it.*

...

Mr. Mysterious is really thoughtful.

Su Mu was already back at Ed's house, eating lunch made by Alice.

As for Ens, he had delivered him to Glenner's house, intending to surprise him.

Although he really wanted to deliver him to the Security Bureau immediately, Glenner hadn't left the Security Bureau all day, and delivering him now would be too much of a burden on this undercover agent.

So Su Mu thoughtfully delivered him to his house, waiting for him to hand him over when he went to the Security Bureau tomorrow.

And just now, he sensed that Vanya seemed to have been subjected to a mental attack.

He had originally planned to simply let the black mist descend and resolve the situation.

However, he discovered that Vanya's location was just across the street from the Security Bureau, so he decided to give Glenna another third-class merit.

Let him enjoy the feeling of being fed now.

It's perfectly reasonable for a god to accept offerings from his followers.

Su Mu forked a piece of beef and put it in his mouth.

Meanwhile, Alice and Lorena were curiously poking at the wings on Su Mu's head, the "Eyeball's" head.

The Eyeball's wings fluttered with each poke, and after being poked too many times, it would turn its head and glare at the two of them.

"It's both ugly and beautiful..."

Lorena commented on the Eyeball, and Alice nodded in agreement.

The Eyeball seemed to understand, angrily removing itself from Su Mu's head, spreading its two pairs of wings and glaring at the two, its body beginning to emit golden light.

Then Su Mu reached out and pinched one of its wings, pressing it onto the table. The golden light dissipated, and the Eyeball looked at Su Mu with a hint of grievance in its eyes.

"They said I'm ugly, don't you have anything to say?!"

Su Mu looked away from the Eyeball's pitiful appearance.

It really wasn't beautiful.

While inwardly criticizing it, he still said to the two girls, "Maybe it's already considered high-value among extraordinary creatures."

The two girls thought for a moment and then nodded.

"I've heard that extraordinary creatures are all monsters with tentacles, so it certainly is high-value."

"At least the wings look beautiful."

After receiving Su Mu's approval, the eyeball immediately became confident and perched on Su Mu's head again.

Su Mu could roughly guess where the eyeball came from.

Based on the information Glenner had looked at from the An Yin Bureau that morning.

It probably came from a place called "Woodland."

According to the An Yin Bureau's records, the Woodland is a subspace inhabited by all extraordinary creatures, not connected to reality.

To enter the Woodland, one either needs to master a special method of entering a dream to enter in a soul state, or find a portal to spacetime to enter in a physical body.

But both methods are not only extremely difficult, but also very dangerous.

The woodland is teeming with unknown spiritual creatures. Anyone entering without a strong enough will might be cursed and corrupted, turning into a new spiritual being.

The criteria for mental fortitude are very strict; even most of the seventh-tier transcendents within the An Yin Bureau are susceptible to corruption upon entry.

The good news is that the eyeball's corruption aura isn't passive; it can be turned off manually.

After turning it off, it stopped glowing, which pleased Su Mu.

Judging from the current situation, the eyeball doesn't seem to be planning to return to the woodland.

Besides, this eyeball is so attached to him; it'll probably stick to his face even while he sleeps. If it were still glowing, he wouldn't need to sleep at all.

Although he doesn't really need to sleep anyway.

—

Fanya followed Su Mu's instructions and began to run, while the old man followed leisurely behind her.

Despite appearing hunched over and struggling to walk, he managed to maintain a distance of 100 meters behind the girl.

The old man didn't seem to care which direction the girl was running in.

That direction wasn't towards the church, and he hadn't heard of anything special or supernatural there.

In fact, Dex City didn't have many supernatural forces to begin with.

Running that way, he couldn't even find the police station.

Vanya didn't choose to shout as she ran, but even if she did, it wouldn't matter; that's the most convenient thing about the [Moth].

He could easily have pursued her leisurely while using widespread brainwashing to keep passersby completely unaware of what was happening, or even had them help him capture Vanya.

Therefore, the old man didn't intend to rush things. Running would make his clothes disheveled, and his top hat might fall off.

Running like that would be too unseemly. He was a nobleman's butler, and he had to maintain his elegance at all times.

Maintaining elegance and decorum as a nobleman's servant was ingrained in his very being.

Besides, she was just a minor girl.

Even if a little girl had a bit of mental fortitude, so what?

Could she possibly outrun a superhuman like him?



Chapter 51: Can you catch a superhuman just by going out the door?


The old man followed slowly behind Vanya, and just as he noticed she was starting to pant and was about to pick up his pace,

two men emerged from around the next intersection.

The two were dressed very simply, appearing to be just passersby, but one of them carried a lantern.

Although it was a cloudy day, carrying a lantern at midday was quite odd.

Even more striking was the wooden box one of them was carrying on his back.

It was about half a person's height and looked like it contained a musical instrument.

Musicians in Dex City?

The old man felt a little surprised.

After all, Dex City wasn't a very large city, only slightly better than a town. Artistic professions like music and painting were practically nonexistent in this city.

The patriarch often complained that there were no musicians here, and that staying in such a place was completely unworthy of the West family's long history.

The old man carefully observed the man's features, memorizing them, and decided to try recommending him to the patriarch when he returned.

At this moment, Vanya, who had been running, waved her right hand at the two.

"This way!"

The old man wasn't surprised. It was normal for others to seek help; he had expected this.

A yellow light shone in his eyes, this time much stronger than when he had hypnotized Vanya.

Simultaneously, an imperceptible spiritual fluctuation emanated from the old man, spreading towards the three people in front of him.

Having done all this, the old man casually straightened his collar.

"Now the outside help you wanted will become my helper."

Then his movements froze.

He discovered that when his spiritual sense covered the two people, only the one carrying the lantern hesitated for a moment; the other, carrying a musical instrument, immediately bent down slightly, becoming wary of his surroundings, like an old soldier who had sensed danger.

The old man immediately felt something was wrong, but still pretended nothing was amiss and took a few steps forward.

Vanya also stopped, supporting herself on her knees, panting.

Su Mu, watching this from behind the scenes, decided to bring Vanya more food in the future.

This child is so weak; she gets out of breath after running just a few steps. She should be at her most energetic age, and she hasn't even started school yet, so why is her body so weak? She seems like a college student from his previous life.

Then there's Glenner. To be honest, he doesn't seem worth the effort to recruit...

However, to gather more intelligence from the Security Bureau, Su Mu thought he should give Glenner more experience.

Although, currently, the Security Bureau's intelligence might not even be as extensive as his own.

But the sealed artifact is a valuable item; if he could trick someone out of a bit more…

Su Mu used the black mist to break Glenna's control.

Glenna, regaining his senses, noticed some strange old people and children about 100 meters away.

Is this the third-class merit the mysterious man mentioned?

“Glenner, let’s get closer. It looks like someone just used supernatural abilities.”

Edric reminded him from beside him.

Glenner nodded slightly and walked towards the two of them, pretending nothing had happened.

The lantern’s detection range was only 10 meters; without getting closer, it was impossible to determine who it was.

*The old man behind that nun, he’s your Third-Class Merit Heir; his ability seems to be [Moth].*

Su Mu's words echoed in Glenner's mind, so he said to Edric beside him, "It's that old man. My spiritual intuition tells me he's a transcendent being of the [Moth] class."

Glenner chose to blindly follow Su Mu.

Could the mysterious man possibly harm me? If he says it's the [Moth], then it's the [Moth].

Edric was somewhat surprised.

He could sense it from this distance?

And even know its principles. No wonder he's the [Lamp].

Edric didn't hesitate and charged directly at the old man.

This time, he didn't hesitate like he had this morning; facts had proven Glenner correct.

He had indeed felt somewhat disoriented earlier, his abilities matching those of the [Moth], though not very strong, probably only around the ninth rank.

Then, with his eighth-rank [Blade] strength, a direct charge would be enough to win.

As he ran, Edric opened the wooden box on his back and took out a long sword.

He couldn't afford to waste time with the [Moth].

Three minutes later, Edric's clothes were stained with blood again.

Before him lay the old man.

One of the old man's legs had been severed, and Edric had broken one of his arms; now he could only lie on the ground, groaning in agony.

Edric didn't think much of it. The Security Bureau's attitude towards the "Cup" and the "Moth" had always been the same. These two principles posed the greatest threat to society, and in most cases, there was no need to show mercy; if they couldn't capture them alive, killing them was sufficient.

Glenner, on the other hand, took out handcuffs from his pocket and put them on the old man, whose hands had been broken, feeling a pang of pity.

The old man's appearance was too tragic, but this pang of pity quickly disappeared.

A gray light shone from the lantern at Glenner's feet.

Edric glanced at it and felt relieved.

As expected of Glenner, he wasn't mistaken; it was indeed the "Moth."

Five minutes later, inside the Security Bureau.

Vanya was talking to Vivian, mainly Vivian fabricating stories about the old man's crimes to make Vanya believe it was just a slightly violent law enforcement process.

Also, Vivian was observing Vanya's mental state to ensure she wasn't mentally corrupted. After confirming everything was alright, Vanya was released.

Meanwhile, in the An Yin Bureau's prison,

The captain looked at the old man handcuffed behind bars, giving Glenner and Edric a strange look.

"So, you suggested going on patrol at noon, and then you just happened to stumble upon a [Moth] superhuman attempting to assault a young girl?"

Glenner felt somewhat embarrassed, but still replied seriously, "Yes, Captain."

Blake was momentarily speechless.

Although Glenner's super-powerful detector had greatly improved their bureau's ability to search for superhumans, the efficiency still left Blake feeling uneasy.

Is the An Yin Bureau I lead really that inept at reconnaissance?

However, this thought was quickly dispelled by another idea.

Now my Security Bureau also has a powerful intelligence network.

The old man looked at Glenn and the other two with fear in his eyes.

He had no idea that there was a Security Bureau in this place.

When was it established? He hadn't received any information about it.

His fear of the Security Bureau grew even stronger.

The Security Bureau was truly terrifying. They had established a base in Dex City without anyone noticing. Were they here to investigate the family head?

The old man pondered this, and only this possibility came to mind.

They must have failed to capture the head of the West family back then, and after making inquiries, discovered that he had changed his identity and appeared in Dex City, so they tracked him down!

In his understanding, the powerful West family must have aroused the Security Bureau's suspicion, which was why they secretly established a base here to encircle and annihilate the West family!

But the old man didn't know that the so-called search of the West family head by the Security Bureau years ago was merely a side effect of the Security Bureau's comprehensive crackdown on the Holy Communion Church; the Security Bureau hadn't paid any attention to his family at all.



Chapter 52 The Mentor and the West Family


Meanwhile, at the mentor's home.

The mentor, in her fifties, got out of bed, naked. Her muscles and physique didn't resemble those of a man in his fifties.

Her body appeared to be in its prime.

Milja lay on the bed, her face rosy.

Her complexion suggested relaxation and comfort, but her left hand was strangely twisted.

The mentor had punished Milia for her unauthorized actions the previous night, and she still felt a slight pain in her left hand.

But what annoyed her most was that she had planned to steal Zobert's idea and report it, only to find that Ens had beaten her to it!

But she wouldn't give up so easily.

Rather than let Ens take the credit, she'd rather no one get away with it.

So she chose to directly report to the mentor that the idea was Zobert's.

And the mentor, upon learning this, showed the displeasure she had anticipated.

Although such impersonation is common in the workplace, and most bosses don't care,

the mentor is a very proud person. He'd be furious if he found out he'd been fooled.

"Heh, serves you right for stealing my credit. You'll regret this next time, Ensemble."

The mentor changed his clothes, just as the butler's voice came from outside the door.

"Sir, a letter has arrived from the West family."

Miria, lying on the bed, paused upon hearing the name.

West? That name sounds familiar.

"Oh, I remember, I played with that young master West a few days ago."

Miria was curious about the connection between her mentor and the West family.

The three of them didn't know much about the Holy Communion Church. Most of the time, they could only passively receive messages from their mentor. They didn't even know his real name.

Miria propped herself up, watching curiously as her mentor opened the door and took the envelope.

"Mentor, what's the letter?"

She asked in a cloying voice.

The mentor glanced at her, then said, "Telling you is fine; they're partners of our Holy Communion Church."

Millia was somewhat surprised.

"So the West family also has superhumans?"

Seemingly in a good mood after just finishing exercise, the mentor didn't hide anything.

"That's right. Not only that, the West family has quite a few good things."

"Then what are the principles of their superhumans?"

"【Moth】."

Millia suddenly understood.

No wonder they could be associated with their Holy Communion Church.

The mentor opened the envelope and began to read.

"Honorable Bishop of the Holy Communion Church, we have received what you previously entrusted to us. The Great Seneza bestowed a divine gift upon you through your generous offering: three insect eggs are contained within this envelope. If you give them to the people you wish to consume, they will suffer divine punishment from the Great Seneza upon betraying you."

He set the letter aside and looked inside the envelope.

Among them lay a stack of silk handkerchiefs; inside, three dark green insect eggs, each the size of a fingernail, were revealed.

He took one and placed it in a glass of red wine on the bedside table, then offered it to the curious Miria.

"Drink this, and from this day forward you will be officially a member of our Holy Communion Church, no longer an ordinary believer."

Miriah, beaming with joy, extended her intact right hand to accept the glass.

"Thank you for your recognition, Master!"

She then downed the drink in one gulp.

Miriah saw her master place something into the glass.

But she didn't care. As long as she could enter the church, she could gain more resources and become stronger.

Besides, she had already foreseen this day when she first joined the church.

Joining a cult requires being prepared.

She already had over a dozen lives on her hands; there was no turning back.

The expected pain didn't occur; instead, she felt a surge of power flowing through her body.

Wasn't it one of those specially concocted poisons in movies that kill if you don't take it for a certain period?

Miriah was somewhat surprised. She saw that the bones in her fractured left hand were healing rapidly.

Seeing her drink it without hesitation, her master raised his hand and patted her head.

“Good child, the Lord will watch over you.”

“This is a special, extraordinary item. Consuming it will grant you some of the [Moth]-related abilities and accelerate your recovery.”

Millia looked surprised, then gratefully looked at her mentor.

“Thank you for your grace, Mentor! Milia will follow you to the death! I will dedicate everything to the Holy Communion Church!”

—

After Milia left, the mentor returned to the study.

The insect egg did indeed possess those two functions, but he didn't mention the most important point.

Once someone tried to divulge information related to him, the insect egg would immediately absorb the host's life force and spirituality to hatch.

Millia no longer needed to worry about the possibility of betrayal. He would try to get her to contact the people from the Security Bureau and see if he could successfully control one of them.

He picked up two photographs beside him, one of Edric and the other of Delant.

These two photographs were taken by his other apprentices, two members of the Security Bureau.

He had more than three apprentices in Dex City; he had many others.

However, these three apprentices are currently the fastest at absorbing the potion.

He uses the other apprentices more to develop his intelligence network.

He's been developing his network in Dex City for much longer than the An Yin Bureau, and he has more informants.

Originally, he didn't plan to take action against the An Yin Bureau so quickly, but their location is quite close, both in the Southern District.

Now, he has to plant some spies within the An Yin Bureau to see if he can pass on some information.

He wants them to confront the Church of All Death in the Western District.

If both sides can fight to a draw, then he can make his move.

If all else fails, planting some spies can at least prevent the An Yin Bureau from discovering their Holy Communion Church's stronghold so quickly.

However, he's not too worried that the An Yin Bureau will discover their Holy Communion Church first.

Given the An Yin Bureau's efficiency, even another year might not be enough to find him, especially since he obtained extraordinary items through a trade with the West family.

One of these items could interfere with supernatural perception, rendering the An Yin Bureau's inefficient investigation methods even more useless.

The man's lips curled into a smile.

"Heh, this useless organization, the An Yin Bureau, dares to challenge my Holy Communion Church?

If it weren't for your alliance with other churches to encircle and annihilate us earlier, the entire Kingdom of Plit would have fallen to our Holy Communion Church!"

They actually dare to send people to investigate the influence of our Holy Communion Church? That's incredibly arrogant!

The man was very confident, showing utter disdain for the Security Bureau.

The Holy Communion Church had dealt with the Security Bureau many times and was well aware of their capabilities.

Essentially, it was just a force hastily organized by the royal family a little over a decade ago. How could it compare to a church with such a long and distinguished history?



Chapter 53 Spiritual Creatures


Inside the An Yin Bureau.

"Cough cough, you are..."

Blake was interrupted before he could ask the old man his identity.

The old man coughed up blood and, before Blake could finish his question, said, "I know your purpose. I have the information you want. I'll tell you whatever you want to know. I'm just Wei..."

Before he could finish, the old man's pupils suddenly contracted, his hunched body began to swell, and his eyes bulged.

Glenner was startled by the old man's sudden abnormality, especially since his spiritual perception indicated that the old man's danger level was rapidly increasing.

Edric also gripped his knife protectively in front of Glenner.

"Glenner, run first!"

Glenner obeyed immediately and ran out the door.

Blake also used his supernatural abilities to try and freeze the old man's body.

But the transformation in the old man happened even faster. The next instant, his back burst open, and a blood-soaked insect, resembling both a butterfly and a moth, emerged from his body.

The insect was about 1 meter long, its body divided into two segments. The upper segment had six thoracic legs, while the lower segment had three long, thin tails.

It had two pairs of wings: the forewings were triangular, and the hindwings were nearly oval, their membranes emitting a dazzling array of colors, and yellow powder floated in the air with each flap.

“Vvv!”

A strange cry emanated from the insect's mouthparts.

Edric's body instantly stiffened.

The intense mental shock made his brain tremble, and his hands went limp.

Even Blake, who had intended to use his supernatural abilities, was momentarily stunned.

This was... a spiritual creature!

Blake was horrified.

A spiritual creature has appeared in Dex City! This means someone here has mastered at least one spatial rift and released a spiritual creature from it!

Blake is certain this insect wasn't a mutated superhuman, but rather emerged from the woods.

Spiritual creatures transformed from ordinary superhumans wouldn't possess such strong spiritual contamination.

The undigested potion in his body was stirred up by the insect's hissing, making him restless.

Controlling his superhuman abilities has become increasingly difficult.

Damn it, are we going to be wiped out?

This pessimistic thought suddenly surged through Blake's mind, but he quickly suppressed it.

No, it's the potion affecting me.

Regardless of the old man's identity, he needs to find a way to kill this insect first.

He must deal with it immediately after it emerges; if it escapes from here, we're doomed.

Blake gritted his teeth, forcibly channeling the chaotic spiritual energy within him. He braced his hands against the wall, and ice began to form on the surface, rapidly spreading towards the insects.

The insects seemed to sense the approaching danger, their hissing becoming even sharper.

"VV!!!"

The spreading ice cracked. Blake endured the throbbing pain in his brain, trying to continue, but blood trickled from the corner of his mouth.

The inherent corruption of spiritual creatures was already enough to cause him trouble, but this particular creature seemed to be a type specializing in mental attacks; every sound it emitted caused the potion within Blake's body to become agitated.

He now only felt dizzy and disoriented.

Just as Blake felt he was about to collapse, a golden light appeared in the cell.

Four wings unfurled, and pure white feathers fell from mid-air.

The parts of the insect's body that touched these feathers were instantly burned through!

A hiss rang out again, but this time it was filled with agony.

The insect's blood-stained wings were burned with countless holes. It began to writhe and struggle to escape, but its still-developing body couldn't move freely.

Under the wings' attack, the insect quickly became still, its body rapidly decaying, leaving behind a pool of dark green blood.

Blake leaned against the wall, gasping for breath.

Edric was also in bad shape, curled up on the ground, dry heaving.

Blake felt a chill of fear.

He had never expected such a spiritual creature emerging from the woods to appear in the small town of Dex.

So much so that he hadn't prepared at all and was caught completely off guard.

If he had been prepared, he would have frozen the old man into an ice sculpture before he could even speak!

Looking at the pool of dark green, foul-smelling blood on the ground, Blake wiped the blood from the corner of his mouth.

The damage wasn't high; even an eighth-tier being like Edric could withstand it, only experiencing nausea and retching. He himself had suffered a backlash from the potion because he had forcibly used his ability.

But this spiritual creature was extremely powerful against mental attacks; its mere roar could interfere with their supernatural abilities. If they encountered it, they had to deal with it quickly or kill it from a distance.

So it really was a [Moth] spiritual creature? But where did it come from?

It could even possess a human body.

The old man had appeared because he was about to say something, and before this creature appeared, they hadn't sensed anything unusual.

So either it existed inside the old man as an egg, or it was in a dormant state.

Either way, it's dangerous, proving their Security Bureau's reconnaissance methods have failed...

They can't use lanterns to investigate like they would with ordinary superhumans. They can only confirm that there's an exit within Dex City that allows spiritual creatures to emerge from the woods, but they don't know its location.

It's even possible the entire city has already been overrun by spiritual creatures...

No, it shouldn't be that bad.

Opening such a door to the woods always comes at a price.

Naturally appearing doors usually only last a few minutes, at most allowing one or two spiritual creatures to escape, which they can handle.

And this spiritual creature doesn't seem very strong, so the door likely only connects to the outer edge of the woods.

With proper equipment, their Security Bureau can still deal with these spiritual creatures in the real world.

But if this door is controlled by someone else...

Blake recalled the old man's unfinished words.

"I have the information you want."

What could the Security Bureau need?

It's definitely information about the Holy Communion Church!

In other words, that door might be controlled by the Church of the Holy Communion!

Blake pulled Edric up.

"Are you alright? Go to the logistics department and get some Heart-Soothing Grass powder."

This sudden turn of events was completely unexpected.

"It's alright, Captain, I can manage,"

Edric replied, rubbing his throbbing temples.

After confirming that Edric was unharmed, Blake turned his gaze to the eye that had lost its golden light, and, surprised, looked towards the exit of the underground prison.

Glenner stood by the wall at the doorway, his complexion not looking too good either.

Blake helped Edric closer and asked, "Glenner, why are you back? Are you mentally prepared?"



Chapter 54 A Tense and Exciting Day


Black was happy to see Glenn risk his life to save them.

It proved his judgment was still good; although Dylant was a spoiled brat, the other three were reliable teammates.

However, he was still worried about Glenn's well-being. After all, he was just a rookie, and facing such an opponent right away was quite a challenge.

If something really happened, he would be heartbroken.

Glenn shook his head.

"It's nothing, Captain, I'm just a little dizzy."

Glenn was indeed fine; Su Mu had taken the mental damage for him.

Su Mu had indeed miscalculated. The black mist he left on Vania sensed the spiritual interference, but because its intensity was too weak, Su Mu completely disregarded it and planned to simply use it on Glenn.

Who would have thought that this third-class merit would turn out to be a suicide bomber!

He almost got blown up by the Security Bureau!

He thought that since he was already handcuffed, what danger could there be?

Suddenly, an insect emerged and used a swarm attack.

Glenner, who hadn't run far to begin with, was nearly hit by the potion's backlash. Luckily, Su Mu reacted quickly, taking over Glenner's body and suppressing the backlash.

Just as he was about to rush in and kill the insect, he saw the eyeball follow him and burn the insect to death with just its feathers.

Quite impressive.

If you ignored the eyeball and only looked at the wings, it really did give the impression of an angel descending to slay a demon.

Su Mu's impression of it was completely refreshed.

"Wow, you have this kind of ability?!"

Su Mu had always thought that this eyeball's effect was similar to that of the sealed artifact—blocking one fatal wound plus the mental pollution inherent in spiritual beings. Turns out, it also had combat capabilities!

"So, are you really related to that sealed artifact?"

Su Mu was still pondering this question when the eyeball, along with his returning consciousness, left the Security Bureau.

Blake watched the disappearing eyeball, then patted Glenner on the shoulder, his eyes filled with admiration and gratitude.

“Glenner, you truly are an upright and courageous person. I sincerely admire you. Not only did you remain rational in this situation, but you also reacted quickly to determine the best course of action.”

Edric also patted Glenner on the other shoulder, his face filled with gratitude and respect.

“Thanks. I owe my life to you. If it had dragged on any longer, I might have been affected by the potion like Dylant.”

“I need to apologize again for my earlier doubts. Compared to you, I'm more like a rookie.”

Glenner, however, was somewhat stunned.

Am I that good?

But I didn't do anything!
