So handsome…

This thought crossed both Miriam and Henry's minds.

It was an indescribable beauty; everything in the world paled in comparison to that handsome face.



Chapter 64 As long as I can get close


Miria was swooning; the potion within her urged her to approach the boy before her.

Henry, too, felt an inexplicable urge to get closer.

But this urge was quickly suppressed. Henry looked at Su Mu and Alice warily.

"Who are you?"

Miria snapped out of her daze, taking a few steps back to create some distance between them. She stared warily, but her gaze drifted subtly towards Su Mu, her cheeks slightly flushed.

"Here to deal with you."

Su Mu's tone was calm, his eyes fixed on Henry.

Isn't this the guy who wanted to ask Vania out shopping?

Su Mu's eyes sharpened.

Alice, finally able to stand steadily on the floor after being helped down the balcony railing by Su Mu, breathed a sigh of relief.

She almost fainted from fright!

"Treat us? Sir, we have no grudge against each other, do we? If we have offended you in any way, we can apologize."

Henry frowned. He felt this boy was strange. Although he saw no trace of spirituality in the boy, the strange desire to get close to him was the biggest problem.

Miriah seemed even more off.

"Little friend, we have no grudge against each other, and I'm just an ordinary person. Don't be so hostile. Why don't you come and play with me?"

Although he was already used to Miriaah's unrestrained personality, Henry's lips still twitched.

Alice, seeing Miriaah's infatuated expression, couldn't help but step forward and block Su Mu.

The Lord of the Mist is mine!

Su Mu looked at Miriaah with some curiosity. The sensation transmitted through the bone ring really couldn't detect any spirituality. Apart from the normal human [cup], there seemed to be nothing special about her.

However, Su Mu wasn't stupid. He roughly guessed the reason from the boy's memories before his death.

It was probably the human skin given to him by the mentor that was working.

Thinking this, Su Mu looked again from the perspective of the black mist's true form.

Sure enough, it was the human skin that Miria had placed in the pocket of her left chest that was at work.

The human skin emanating from it was the spirituality of [Winter].

Su Mu pondered for a moment.

[Winter] represents silence, death, and the end; having such a shielding ability seemed quite normal.

Just as the three were facing off, Henry moved first.

He'd deal with this boy first.

Henry had never intended to negotiate from the beginning; his words were merely a delaying tactic.

He slammed his hands on the ground, and a complex magic circle pattern enveloped the four of them.

This was Henry's ability; he wasn't a normal superhuman.

He could open a temporary door connecting to the woods.

He could summon a spiritual creature imbued with the [Moth] principle, but this move came at a great cost; after using it, he would need to sacrifice at least ten living people to Silessa within a day.

Originally, he hadn't planned to use such a deadly move in this operation, but Su Mu gave him a very strange feeling.

Even though it was their first meeting, and in such a hostile manner, he felt a strange sense of closeness!

Looking at Su Mu's motionless expression, Henry's lips curled into a smile.

Although I don't know what ability you used to interfere with my mind, none of that matters anymore. When those spiritual creatures have devoured you down to your head, I'll treasure your skull as my most prized possession. Now, just die in peace.

Milia was startled by this.

What is Henry doing?!

She had originally planned to use the extraordinary item her mentor gave her to lower their guard, then find an opportunity to bite off the boy's flesh.

But Henry's interference had ruined everything.

Fine, since that's the case, I'll just rush in and take a bite. What would it be like to eat someone as obsessed with my potions as you?

A greedy glint appeared in Milia's eyes.

Su Mu, however, casually said to Alice, "Perfect, these two aren't very strong. Try your ability on them and see how much it affects them."

Ability?

Hearing the key word, Henry and Milia immediately became alert.

Miria met Alice's gaze, her wariness increasing.

What kind of superhuman is she?

What are the effects of her abilities?

Is it a close-combat powerhouse?

Won't I fall into a trap if I rush in?

In an instant, countless terrible scenarios flashed through Miria's mind, and her intended charge at Su Mu stopped abruptly.

She froze on the spot.

No, this is her ability!

Miria suddenly realized that her previous thoughts were highly abnormal, far too pessimistic.

This girl also possesses a control-type ability, and the effect looks so familiar…

It's [The Listener]!

It's the potion their Holy Communion Church sold back then!

This girl is from that secret society!

Miria immediately guessed the truth she had assumed.

Clearly, that secret society had discovered her underhanded dealings, investigated her, and successfully tracked her down!

Such powerful intelligence gathering capabilities! They'd uncovered my identity in just two days!

They even managed to anticipate my mentor's plan and ambush me here. This secret society is no ordinary organization!

Milia was shocked, but her actions were swift and decisive.

One of them was a transcendent being of the [Heart] principle, and they'd even obtained the potion from their Holy Communion Church.

Milia knew very well that the [Listener] didn't offer any physical enhancements, nor did their abilities provide any advantage in direct combat.

Therefore, she had a strategy in mind.

Leave it alone!

Milia also had some guesses about Su Mu's transcendent abilities.

The ability to make the potion within her stir and generate the desire for intercourse meant it was definitely a control-type transcendent pathway.

Although she didn't know if the secret society was simply short of manpower, or if they already knew that she belonged to a pathway within the [Cup] principle that wasn't particularly adept at combat.

But even if [Cup] isn't particularly skilled in combat, she's still [Cup], possessing a significant physical advantage among lower-ranking characters.

Therefore, among the four, she's the strongest in close combat!

Moreover, she also possesses the [Moth] ability given to her by her mentor!

Milia and Su Mu exchanged glances, a grayish-white light gleaming in their eyes.

While her strength was certainly inferior to a normal [Moth], even interfering with the boy for just a second would bring her within her advantageous range.

Furthermore, her own eighth-tier [Cup] possessed extraordinary abilities related to control; once she got close, she could exert an even more powerful hypnotic effect.

At that point, she could tear the boy to shreds even with brute force!

It was just a pity; she wanted to try her luck with this boy, but now it seemed she wouldn't have the chance.



Chapter 65 The Door Connecting to the Woodland


In the blink of an eye, Miria, with her formidable physical prowess, charged towards Su Mu.

Su Mu remained motionless, maintaining his stance as he stared at her.

Just as I expected!

As I predicted, these two are merely control-type superhumans, lacking any means to counter control!

Gaining the first strike is victory!

Just as Miria was about to pierce Su Mu's chest with her right hand, Su Mu grabbed her outstretched hand before she could react.

So fast! When?!

Before Miria could react, Su Mu grabbed her right arm and slammed her to the ground with an over-the-shoulder throw.

Her back slammed solidly onto the floor, and Miria let out a painful groan.

How is this possible?

Is he not a control-type but a combat-type?

Then how did he affect the potion within me?

Alice, somewhat ashamed, said to Su Mu, "I'm sorry, Lord of the Mist, I'm so useless..."

Su Mu comforted her by patting her head.

"It's alright, being able to influence anything is already quite impressive."

Alice, having been patted on the head in public by Su Mu, blushed, her cheeks flushing slightly.

The relaxed atmosphere between the two made it seem as if they weren't engaged in a life-or-death battle, but rather on a picnic.

Lord of the Mist? What's that?

Are there any principles related to the mist?

Is it the [Moth]?

Chaos seems to be often associated with the mist...

Before Miria could figure it out, the magic circle beneath their feet began to distort.

Then, a swarm of insects embodying the [Moth] principle emerged from it.

Their gazes locked onto Su Mu.

Their compound eyes revealed an uncontrollable desire.

"VV!"

"V..."

Henry's eyes gleamed.

It's done!

Under Milia's cover, he successfully constructed a complete magic circle!

A confident "plan in motion" gleamed in his eyes as he looked at Su Mu.

Now, prepare to face the contamination from these spiritual creatures!

Even if they weren't as numerous as the ones his grandfather had trained over the years, each one possessed at least the interference effect of a ninth-tier [Moth] level transcendent, plus his own strength, equivalent to an eighth-tier [Moth] level natural transcendent.

Unless you're a seventh-tier transcendent with extremely strong mental resistance, you can't escape unscathed from the contamination and mental interference of at least ten [Moth] level spiritual creatures!

Several insects poked their heads out from beside Milia, who was lying on the ground. Finding her closest, they bit her.

"Ah!"

"Henry, what are you doing! I'm your teammate!"

A chunk of flesh was bitten off Milia's leg, and the stench of blood filled the room.

The insect that had eaten the flesh grew even larger, and the mental contamination it emitted intensified.

Even Miria was affected; several insect antennae sprouted from her left palm.

"Damn it, Henry, are you insane! At this rate, we'll all turn into monsters! Stop it!"

Miriria panicked. The mental pollution from the spiritual creature and the mental infection of the [Moth] principle rendered her completely unable to use her supernatural abilities. She was now just like an ordinary person, or even worse.

The potion within her body was also beginning to show signs of backlash.

But Henry completely ignored her; his eyes were now only on Su Mu.

The siege, the An Yin Bureau's legacy—none of that mattered anymore. All he wanted now was to kill the boy before him and collect his remains!

Come on, let me see your agonizing struggle!

Show me even more humiliation and ugliness!

The inexplicable closeness he felt towards Su Mu transformed into a powerful urge to destroy him.

Su Mu sensed this intense emotion.

What did it mean?

Su Mu felt like he'd encountered a psychopath.

That twisted, intense emotion—like hatred, yet not quite hatred—sent goosebumps up Su Mu's skin.

Alice, seeing this scene, moved closer to Su Mu, somewhat frightened.

These insects looked disgusting, and their cries were awful.

Although she had accepted that the world she lived in wasn't peaceful, Alice was still nervous about seeing beings with supernatural abilities other than the superhumans for the first time.

The eyeballs, aside from Alice's subconscious, were Su Mu's apostles.

And now, comparing them, Alice felt Su Mu's words made a lot of sense.

The eyeballs were already quite beautiful.

Now, looking at the insects' constantly opening and closing mouthparts and their tentacles dancing in the air, Alice wanted nothing more than to shrink into Su Mu's arms.

So disgusting!

That was the only thought in Alice's mind.

As for the mental pollution inherent in spiritual beings?

Su Mu controlled Alice's soul; what could possibly pollute it through him?

Su Mu patted Alice's head to soothe her, then closed his eyes and gently patted his left ear twice.

The movements were steady and elegant, exuding the confidence and nonchalant arrogance of a powerful figure.

"It's impressive that you dared to challenge me, but... that's all you're commendable for."

Then, the black mist began to spread from Alice, covering the entire room.

Suddenly, everyone was plunged into darkness.

What's going on?

Henry was filled with doubt.

I can't see. Is it his supernatural ability?

Considering this possibility, he immediately became alert.

Is obscuring my vision a prelude to a sneak attack?

Where will he attack from, behind?

Henry widened the door behind him, allowing more spiritual beings to emerge from behind him to protect him.

The advantage remained.

No spiritual beings reported being dead; his connection with them hadn't been severed.

A mere obscuring of vision won't win against me. Let them corrupt you and turn you into a monster!

Henry gave the spiritual beings simple instructions, causing them to begin howling incessantly.

"vvv!!"

"v—!"

Suddenly, the hissing of an insect abruptly ceased.

Its link with Henry was completely severed, leaving only 12 insects with which Henry maintained a soul link.

What happened?

Dead?

How could that be? Spiritual beings couldn't be killed so easily!

Even if a spiritual being's head is cut off, it can still live, but just a moment ago, one of the spiritual beings linked to him by soul suddenly died.

In an instant.

The speed was so fast that Henry wondered if it was his imagination; in fact, he had only summoned 12 spiritual beings from the beginning.

"vv—"

Suddenly, another spiritual creature's connection was lost.

Eleven left.

How did they do that?

Henry felt a chill run down his spine.

Is this power really plausible?

Henry was certain it wasn't his imagination, but this information only intensified his fear.

So, it means they can instantly kill a spiritual creature using only brute force?

Is this even human?!



Chapter 66 New Ability Development


“v—”

“Hiss—”

“Squeak—”

The screams of various spiritual creatures rang in Henry's ears as his connections with them were being severed one by one.

He could feel the spiritual creatures dying one after another, starting from a distance in front of him.

He hadn't intended to ambush him at all; he was walking right towards him openly?!

The order of the spiritual creatures' deaths was steadily approaching his location.

7…

5…

3…

1…

Less than three seconds after his vision went black, the shriek of the last spiritual creature in front of him abruptly ceased.

Without hesitation, Henry quickly drew a pistol from his waist and fired wildly at the scene before him.

But he didn't hold out much hope. When he realized that the spiritual creatures he had summoned were instantly killed, he already knew he was going to die; all he was doing now was a death throes.

The magic circle, no longer sustained by Henry's spirit, gradually dissipated. Henry simultaneously released a ranged mental interference attack from [Moth], attempting to harm Su Mu.

The muzzle flash flickered in the black mist, but was quickly swallowed by darkness again; the bullet's trajectory left no trace in the mist.

Henry's vision remained completely black; he could see nothing. He could only confirm that the bullet had been fired through the muzzle flash and the booming sound of the gunshot.

But after he ran out of bullets, the room fell silent again.

Everything that had just happened seemed like an illusion.

Am I still alive?

Have I been spared?

This thought flashed through Henry's mind, then he felt his body suddenly sway.

?

The black mist disappeared, and his vision was finally no longer completely dark.

Su Mu remained standing on the balcony, motionless, while Alice beside him glanced anxiously outside.

The spiritual creatures he had summoned were reduced to piles of minced meat.

His perspective was low, as if he were on the floor.

He struggled to turn his head, and what appeared before him was his lower body.

His body had been severed at the waist.

Dark green blood gushed from his waist.

His handsome face was filled with bewilderment.

I... am dead?

Why is the blood green?

Before he could figure it out, he lost consciousness.

Su Mu, having completed his possession with the black mist, began rapidly reviewing his memories.

From leaving home to dealing with Henry, the entire process took less than two minutes.

......

So he was a self-taught individual, not a superhuman achieved through potions, but not like Edward's self-taught methods either. This Henry had possessed the [Moth] ability since childhood, and the way he enhanced his ability was through contact with spiritual creatures?

From his memories, each time he opened the door connecting to the woods and came into contact with spiritual beings, the upper limit of his spiritual power would increase.

Su Mu glanced again at his lower body, which was spurting green blood.

...

Brother, were you no longer human all along?

Su Mu felt this was quite obvious; this Henry had been corrupted into a spiritual being as a child, but for some reason, he still retained normal human thinking abilities.

So, does this also explain his inexplicable feelings?

Could it be that I possess an affinity for spiritual beings?

Thinking back to the eyeballs, it seemed quite possible, but that wasn't important.

Whether or not I have an affinity, I'll just destroy any hostile individuals I encounter.

What's giving Su Mu a headache now is, what kind of monsters and demons are in Dex City?

There are so many cults!

It feels like I've stumbled into a den of thieves.

Was it a bit too reckless of me to bring two kids to school here?

As for their other life experiences, they perfectly matched Su Mu's imagination of these cult superhumans.

None of them were human.

However, it seems they haven't killed as many as Edward. The West family basically performs sacrifices every few months.

And they're extremely careful in choosing their sacrifices from the slums, only occasionally using families that compete with their business.

You could say they're very vigilant, which explains why the Security Bureau hasn't found any information about this family.

Although Su Mu still harbored doubts, the lack of news might be due to the An Yin Bureau's incompetence…

Su Mu waved his hand, and a cloud of black mist completely enveloped Henry's body. After writhing for a while, Henry's body vanished, leaving only a pale gray ball of light.

The other insects did the same.

This was a new technique he'd devised while shaping his own body, and this time, it actually worked.

Simply put, it involved using black mist to devour the bodies of superhumans or spiritual beings, converting their bodies into spiritual essence and preserving it.

The preserved spiritual essence could be used to shape people or stored as energy. In other words, he now possessed a portion of spiritual essence equivalent to an eighth-tier [Moth], and several portions of ninth-tier [Moths].

However, the difference between eighth and ninth tiers was still quite significant. Even after combining all the spiritual essence, Su Mu only managed to create a spiritual ball roughly at the mid-eighth-tier level.

Feeding it directly could produce a self-taught mid-eighth-tier being.

However, an ordinary person couldn't simply consume this eighth-tier spiritual energy; even a small amount would likely turn them into a spiritual being.

It could also be used as an attack method, though it wouldn't have the unique effects of a superhuman drinking a potion.

But the [Moth's] spiritual energy inherently possesses a chaotic effect, so simply using it to attack would suffice.

Su Mu tried releasing this spiritual energy, then slightly deactivated the protection surrounding Alice's soul.

Alice, gazing anxiously out the window, suddenly swayed.

Su Mu withdrew the spiritual energy, and Alice quickly returned to normal, blinking somewhat dazedly.

The experiment proved Su Mu's hypothesis was feasible.

As for the consumption issue, there was no need to worry. This spiritual energy, like a superhuman's mana bar, could regenerate over time; as long as a little remained, it would continue to regenerate until it reached its maximum.

Although it consumed Su Mu's black mist for regeneration, the amount consumed per second was less than the rate at which his black mist regenerated per second.

The assessment is that it consumes no resources and is a cheap trick.

From now on, even this body, devoid of spirit and only able to activate its domain for 10 seconds, will have an infinitely usable control skill.

Using Alice's body, it's even more possible to control the opponent with the domain while simultaneously using mental interference to annoy them.

And there's no need to worry about this spiritual energy being obtained by someone else under special circumstances.

This spiritual energy is also stored in Su Mu's subspace. It can be brought into reality through the black mist, but even if the black mist surrounding it dissipates, it will simply return to the subspace along with the mist.

"Lord of the Mist, that...that woman ran that way!"

Alice pointed in a direction and turned back to Su Mu.

Su Mu waved his hand.

"It's alright, she can't go far."

Su Mu hadn't used a domain; he had simply filled the room with black mist.

The method for killing those insects was simply and brutally shredding them with the solidified black mist.

Meanwhile, Miria, after being enveloped by his black mist, benefited from the misfortune and successfully recovered from the mental interference of those insects.

The first thing she did after recovering was to escape from the balcony.

Alice, using her [Listener] ability to sense the emotions of nearby creatures, noticed Miria's changed location and immediately peered out from the balcony to search for her.

Although she didn't know why her mind had suddenly gone blank for a moment, Alice still spotted Miria disappearing into the alley and immediately informed Su Mu.

Su Mu appreciated her quick reaction. Less than a week ago, Alice was just an ordinary village girl, but now she was already using her abilities to assist Su Mu in battles against superhumans.

Although not very useful.

When the black mist covered Miria, Su Mu had some of it transfer to her body through her wounds.

Although the control effect was gone, and he couldn't gain vision through control,

he could still locate her through the black mist within her body.

As for why he let her escape,

Su Mu admitted that his playful nature had indeed increased since his transmigration.

Assuming nothing unexpected would happen,

he just wanted to play a little more of the cat-and-mouse game.

Damn it, it must be because I've been an evil... Outer God for too long.

Su Mu refused to admit that this was his true nature.



Chapter 67: Can Reconciliation Be Reached?


"Huff, huff..."

Miriah dragged her weakened body as she ran, her clothes soaked through by the rain, her high heels broken and thrown off.

Now she was running barefoot through the alleyways.

The horrifying wound on her leg was rapidly healing thanks to the [Cup's] self-healing ability; the only difference now was the still slightly red skin.

Although she was barefoot on this rough ground, Miria's feet weren't blistered.

The potion she drank at level eight wasn't a physical enhancement like Ens and Zobert's, but a psychological hypnotic one.

However, the potion she drank at level nine was no different from the other two, so her physical strength was still beyond that of ordinary people.

The plan had taken an unexpected turn.

Zobert was right, that secret society was not simple; she had to tell her mentor!

Miriah was running frantically towards her mentor's house.

She glanced back every now and then, secretly relieved only after confirming no one was chasing her.

Thank goodness, they haven't discovered I escaped.

Henry, you'd better hold them off for a while. I'll come back to help you after I get the message to my mentor.

It's your fault for suddenly summoning a spiritual creature. Don't blame me for running away; I'm calling for reinforcements.

Millia wasn't sure of the boy's true strength, but she was certain the girl was only a ninth-tier [Heart] cultivator.

However, the boy's ability to suddenly throw her over his shoulder when she was already close indicated his close-combat skills far surpassed hers.

Furthermore, the fact that she felt restless just looking at the potion inside him suggested he also possessed some ability to interfere with the mind.

While she wasn't sure if he used a supernatural item or supernatural power,

one thing was certain: she couldn't defeat him.

Therefore, Milia decisively chose to abandon Henry.

Her rapid breathing gradually subsided, and Miria's emotions calmed.

She had run so far and still showed no signs of being caught. Whether it was because they were being held up by Henry, or because the supernatural artifact given to her by her mentor was working and preventing them from pinpointing her location,

the scales of victory were tipping in her favor!

Even if Henry was dead, even if that boy possessed some kind of supernatural artifact that detected spiritual energy, as long as she was protected by the artifact given to her by her mentor, she absolutely could not be tracked!

At this pace, she could reach her mentor's house in another half minute.

Once she reached her mentor's house, it would be her turn to counterattack!

Victory was within reach, and excitement surged within Miria.

Just then, the raindrops that had been hitting Miria stopped.

The rain stopped?

Mria subconsciously looked up.

Then her pupils suddenly contracted, and she stared at the sky in disbelief.

Because she couldn't see clearly, she tripped over some trash and fell forward.

But she had no time to check her injuries; instead, she quickly got up and stared at the sky.

A patch of black mist, not very large, but enveloping her, was about 10 meters above her.

However, the black mist wasn't actually in mid-air 10 meters away.

In fact, the black mist stretched out in a columnar shape downwards from that visible patch.

Miria was now completely enveloped in the black mist.

The reason she couldn't see or feel it was because Su Mu had reduced the transparency of the black mist below, and hadn't increased its density to make it resemble a solid.

From the center of the visible black mist, a figure slowly emerged.

The boy stood upside down in the black mist, his black hair hanging down with gravity.

But the clothes emanating from his body, shrouded in black mist, seemed to defy the laws of physics, unaffected by gravity.

The boy's dark pupils met her gaze, and a gentle smile appeared on his lips.

The smile was gentle; normally, if Miria saw such a handsome boy smile at her, she would have returned the smile.

But now it was different. Now, Miria only felt terror.

Was that black mist a supernatural artifact?

No, this level of power indicated it was a sealed object!

Anything capable of carrying someone through the air couldn't be as simple as a supernatural artifact.

This secret society even possesses sealed artifacts!

Calm down, calm down.

She hadn't committed any irreconcilable feud with them.

Miriah glanced at Su Mu's face again.

Very young, judging from his appearance, probably 16 or 17 years old. From this, he seemed naive.

She still had a chance to negotiate.

Miriah took a few deep breaths, then smiled gently.

"Little brother, I was wrong just now. I apologize. Whatever compensation you want, I can give you. After all, we don't have any particularly deep conflicts. Doesn't your society need the [Heart] potion? Our Holy Communion Church has some. Our two societies can definitely cooperate."

Su Mu simply smiled at her, as if to say, "Go on."

Miriah cursed Su Mu inwardly for pushing his luck, but her smile remained unchanged.

"It was my fault before. I had some ulterior motives when we were trading, but it didn't affect you at all, did it? Let's pretend it never happened, okay? If you're still upset, little brother, you can do whatever you want to me."

Tears welled in Miria's eyes as she looked at Su Mu, feigning hurt.

At the same time, she subtly lifted her skirt, revealing her pale thighs, intending to seduce the seemingly impulsive boy.

Su Mu simply smiled and remained silent.

Who was she trying to seduce? She wasn't even as pretty as my village girl.

But what was our society anyway?

Su Mu pondered for a moment, then realized.

So she thought he was from that secret society she'd made up using Zobert's body?

Su Mu shifted his gaze. Zobert was still on his way to the live broadcast, and Glenner was still waiting. There was still some time before it officially started; he could still play around.

Su Mu's body spun 180 degrees within the transparent black mist, landing normally beneath it.

"You put a bug at my doorstep."

Miliya: ...Looks like there's no possibility of reconciliation?



Chapter 68: End of Pre-meal Snacks


"That...that was an accident, little brother, we really didn't know it was your house!"

Miriah was still trying to make a last-ditch effort.

But she noticed that Su Mu's eyes remained unchanged.

Miriah gritted her teeth, pulled down the straps of her dress, revealing her shoulders and most of her upper body.

"Little brother, let me use my body to appease you, okay? Just let me go, okay?"

This time, her words weren't ordinary temptation; Miriaah activated her ability.

Her ability allowed her to transform her spoken words into a "seed" and implant it into the minds of others.

This "seed" would grow according to the degree to which the other person cared about her.

The more they cared about her, the greater the seed's influence, allowing her subsequent words to have a hypnotic effect, subtly changing the other person's thoughts.

This kind of care doesn't discriminate between good and bad; even hatred can cause the seed to grow, although not as quickly as positive emotions like concern.

Miriah was gambling.

She was betting that Su Mu cared about her, even negatively, and that it would still have some impact on him.

As long as it could affect him for even half a minute…

“I’m afraid of getting sick.”

?

Milia didn’t react for a moment, then her cheeks flushed red.

Angry.

Are you looking down on my [cup] constitution?

Suppressing her anger, Milia began to search for a way to escape.

Just as Milia was about to say something, she suddenly saw a cold light appear before her eyes.

Clang!

Like a flash of a blade, Milia’s left arm was instantly severed.

The insect-like tentacles on her severed arm seemed to sense it and curled up into a ball.

Ouch!

So fast!

Where did that attack come from!

Milia was terrified; in the blink of an eye, her arm flew away.

Blood gushed from the severed limb, and Milia’s face turned deathly pale.

What happened to her felt like a nightmare.

She was a [Cup], possessing skin resilience and physical strength three times that of an ordinary person.

Even with such powerful base stats, she couldn't defend against a single attack from the boy!

She couldn't even react!

"My lord, spare my life! My supernatural abilities are very useful! I can do many things for you!"

Milia completely lost the courage to resist, not even daring to run away.

At this moment, she knelt on the ground, her head hitting the ground heavily with a dull thud.

When she raised her head again, her forehead was slightly sunken, her eyes filled with fear and pleading.

Su Mu looked at her with some disappointment.

She knelt down so easily, not even lasting long.

Should he limit it next time, like not using the black mist?

No, no, against this kind of cult organization, he had to strike hard. Playing around is fine, but going too far is not good.

Su Mu's silence gave Milia a glimmer of hope.

Could it be that they really need my abilities?

She wanted to speak, to say something more.

But she found herself unable to utter a sound.

Before even having time to form a question, she had already lost consciousness.

...

Su Mu felt a bit overwhelmed after going through the memories.

His expression changed several times.

This is pretty perverted...

He had originally given up hope on the moral standards of the Holy Communion Church, but Miria...

What do you mean by "the things you remember most vividly are who you had sex with?!"

Dude, are you even human?

Su Mu rubbed his eyes hard, somewhat regretting looking at her memories.

With a disdainful wave, the black mist carried a ball of red spiritual energy back, along with a palm-sized human skin.

A mid-stage eighth-tier [cup].

If used directly on someone, it would probably amplify inner desires.

Aside from these eyesore memories, there was nothing useful.

Whether the mentor named Bruce knew about his plan or not was irrelevant; he'd already killed all the spiritual creatures, and he was now surrounded by the Security Bureau.

A head peeked out from the edge of the black mist above.

Alice's head, peeking out from the edge of the black mist, was hit by a few drops of rain. She was about to grab Su Mu's umbrella above to shield herself from the rain when she realized the rain had stopped.

Looking up, she saw that the black mist ceiling, which had originally covered the floor beneath her feet, had extended slightly outwards, now completely obscuring her head.

Alice felt a small surge of joy.

The Lord of the Mist is so considerate; if only she were my mother…

No, no, what am I thinking now!

Shaking her head to clear her mind of these inexplicable thoughts, Alice looked at the ground about 30 meters away, her legs trembling slightly.

She was lying prone on the black mist, looking down.

“Lord of the Mist, can…can we go home now?”

“We’ve only been out for a short time, less than five minutes, right? I’ll take you to see another anti-terrorism operation.”

“W-we’re going to fight?”

“What are you talking about? We’re going to ‘mediate’.”

—

7:40 PM, 12 Walter Street.

After confirming that Edric and Berlaut were in position, Black didn’t wait any longer, stepped on the wall, and leaped upwards.

Just as he was about to stop rising, Blake's foot tapped the wall, and a small platform made of ice appeared above him.

This was one of his extraordinary abilities: Ice Freezing.

He could freeze moisture in the air directly into ice crystals; the freezing speed and the hardness of the ice crystals depended on how much spiritual energy he expended.

The hardest ice crystals could withstand a volley of gunfire from a dozen people.

He quickly reached the second-floor window.

Quite coincidentally, as Blake looked into the window, his eyes met those of Bruce, who was sitting in a chair looking out.

Two pairs of light brown eyes stared at each other through the thick glass, and the air was silent for a moment.

Then.

Bang!

Crack!

The glass shattered.

Blake had smashed the window with a knife and jumped in from the outside.

Bruce wasn't idle either; he raised his hand as Blake jumped in.

The stark white bone spurs sliced ​​through flesh and shot towards Blake with incredible speed, even whistling through the air!

Blake only had time to raise his longsword to barely block the bone spurs.

Clang! Clang!

Sparks flew as the bone spurs collided with the longsword!

Also a seventh-tier weapon.

Blake immediately judged by the hardness of the bone spurs.

Then he infused his sword with spiritual energy.

The already silvery-white blade instantly gleamed with a cold light, and the temperature in the entire room plummeted.

Frost even began to form on the floor beneath their feet.

Crack.

The bone spikes that came into contact with the longsword froze and became brittle; Blake simply flicked the blade, shattering them into pieces.

Bruce showed no sign of panic, clearly having anticipated this.

A broken bone wouldn't affect him much.

But his mind was preoccupied with something else.

How did the An Yin Bureau know my location?

Was my initial guess wrong? Had my intelligence already been known to the Church of the End of All Things?!

Even so, what about the bait I prepared?

That insect egg was at the level of an eighth-tier [Moth], and he placed it in a residential area. Why didn't the An Yin Bureau send anyone to deal with it?

Didn't they notice?

Impossible. Although the An Yin Bureau's intelligence gathering capabilities were practically nonexistent, they had [Awakening] members; they couldn't possibly have been unresponsive to such a strong spiritual fluctuation!



Chapter 69: The Direct Clash Between the Security Bureau and the Holy Communion Church


Had it already been resolved?

Bruce was filled with doubt, but his body didn't stop.

Red light emanated from the eye sockets of the skull on his cane.

A continuous stream of spiritual energy surged into his body.

This was the function of this extraordinary item: it could transfer the spiritual energy stored within to him, allowing him to recklessly increase the amount of spiritual energy consumed when using his extraordinary abilities, thus enhancing them.

Currently, the [Cup] of spiritual energy stored in the cane was enough to increase the output of his extraordinary abilities by 1.5 times for an hour!

Such an improvement was already quite considerable.

In a battle between seventh-tier beings, a 1.5-fold difference in output was enough for him to completely overwhelm them.

After that bone spur broke, several more bone spurs immediately grew from his arm.

These bone spurs didn't point directly at Blake, but rather circled several times in mid-air, sealing Blake within a circle in half a breath.

Simultaneously, the blood droplets on the bone spurs began to glow, emitting a dangerous red light.

"Boiling Blood"

Bruce's primary offensive method after advancing to the seventh tier [Blood Boiler].

He can make his blood boil and burn, reaching temperatures high enough to melt metal!

Frankly, Blake's ability is a strong counter to him, because boiling blood to its maximum temperature requires a heating process.

Blake's area-of-effect ice seal can freeze his blood before he completes the heating process, interrupting the skill's animation.

But now it's different. With the enhancement of the extraordinary item, Bruce can forcibly increase his spiritual energy to shorten this heating process, preventing Blake from catching the skill's animation.

Bang!

Bang bang!

The countless blood droplets on the bone spurs boiled and exploded.

Scalding blood splattered all over Blake's body.

Instantly burning through his clothes!

Blake's uniform appeared to melt under the intense heat.

But Blake reacted quickly, immediately covering his body with a layer of ice armor to prevent the blood from splattering directly onto him.

The blood sizzled the moment it touched the ice armor, scalding a small crater and releasing steam.

These blood droplets could actually melt my ice armor?!

Blake was stunned.

This was the first time this had happened. His ice armor was formed from spiritual energy; even if he were thrown into a factory boiler for a day, it wouldn't melt!

Yet, just a brief contact with the blood melted a 2mm deep crater!

His ice armor was only about 5mm thick!

Forced to act, Blake had to increase the spiritual energy supply to the ice armor to fill the crater.

At the same time, he struck a bone spike with his knife, channeling his spiritual energy through the blade.

He planned to use his enhanced ice-sealing ability, amplified by his longsword, to freeze the bone spikes and the blood droplets on them before they fully boiled and exploded.

Normally, those blood droplets should have been completely frozen.

But the situation had reversed.

The blood droplets were hotter than he had imagined; even the ice-sealing ability enhanced by his longsword couldn't freeze them.

Blake felt uneasy; he could sense that the man before him wasn't serious.

But he was finding it difficult to cope.

Damn it, the Church of the Holy Communion, after being besieged once, still has superhumans with such strength?

Blake and Headquarters had been somewhat careless.

They underestimated the Church of the Holy Communion's strength. They originally thought that after the siege, the Church of the Holy Communion was severely weakened, and this branch wouldn't be very strong.

Therefore, Headquarters only dispatched a combat-type superhuman from the surrounding areas to assist in the battle.

But judging from the current situation, defeating this "mentor" of the Church of the Holy Communion would require a considerable price, and they might not even be able to defeat this "mentor."

Bruce kept detonating the blood beads on his bone spikes, while also having the leisure to think.

What exactly is going on?

Could it be that Miria betrayed him?

No, after reaching that point, how could she betray him?

Or was Miria killed?

A fantasy! An eighth-tier [Cup] isn't someone anyone can kill in Dex City. Even if she were truly driven to the brink, she could easily have that boy hatch a spiritual creature to buy time.

Unless he's been targeted by a peak seventh-tier individual.

But where in Dex City is there a peak seventh-tier individual?

She herself is only at the mid-stage of seventh-tier.

So where exactly did things go wrong?

Or is there actually no problem?

The Security Bureau also sent people to deal with that spiritual creature?

But how could the Security Bureau dare send their only seventh-tier individual to fight her one-on-one, while sending other extraordinary individuals, perhaps even those at eighth tier, to deal with the spiritual creature?

Are they crazy?

A spiritual creature appearing in a residential area—if the Security Bureau doesn't concentrate all its forces, they'll be risking the entire area being corrupted into spiritual beings.

Looking at Blake, whom she was suppressing, Bruce had a thought.

Was he overthinking things? He even planned to use spiritual creatures to weaken the Security Bureau's strength. But if they actually faced them, they'd be nothing but a roadside enemy; there was absolutely no need to waste insect eggs…

Was he really being too cautious? For a roadside organization like this, he didn't need to make so many preparations.

Although he didn't know what had happened on Miria's side, he could deal with it first and then go check.

A mere Security Bureau branch captain—he can die here!

Just then, the library door was kicked open.

Two figures rushed in, one after the other.

Bruce turned around and saw Edric and Berlaut, both dressed in black uniforms.

Edric, gripping a knife in both hands, rushed towards him from the right, while Berlaut stood in the doorway, raising his pistol and firing three shots.

Bang, bang, bang!

Three gunshots rang out, bullets flying towards Bruce's head.

Trying to harm me with bullets?

The Security Bureau is indeed a bunch of useless trash.

Bruce didn't even bother to raise his hand to block, instead taking the bullet head-on.

Clang—

The bullet struck Bruce's temple. Bruce was unharmed, but the bullet was deflected.

"Is this all the Security Bureau is capable of?"

He had barely finished speaking when Bruce suddenly felt a moment of dizziness.

In that instant, Edric had already rushed to his side.

A silver-white longsword swung down, cleanly severing Bruce's arm, which was gripping his cane, from the elbow.

Excellent!

Berlaut was overjoyed. The moment he entered the room, he had noticed the unusual nature of the cane. A brief exchange of glances between him and Edric was enough for them to devise a plan.

The bullet that had just been fired at Bruce was a specially made bullet from their Security Bureau.



Chapter 70 The Security Bureau Suffers a Series of Defeats


Although these bullets were practically useless against seventh-tier beings, only causing a momentary disorientation upon a headshot, Bruce was helpless if he simply raised his hand to block or turned to dodge.

For a seventh-tier [Cup], dodging bullets was not difficult.

But Bruce, incredibly arrogant, didn't dodge at all, instead taking the bullets head-on.

However, he certainly had the right to be arrogant.

Those three bullets, even hitting his temples, were like hitting steel plates, leaving no mark whatsoever, not even breaking the skin.

But now, the supernatural artifact had been knocked away by Edric…

Suddenly, several bone spurs emerged from Bruce's wound, piercing his severed limb and rapidly pulling it backward, actually reattaching his hand!

Bruce gripped his cane tighter, and the red light in the skull's eye sockets on the cane intensified.

Then, the blood gushing from his severed limb, still suspended in mid-air, suddenly glowed red.

All of this happened in an instant.

Edric was still in the middle of his downward slash.

The sudden burst of countless red lights before his eyes alerted him to something ominous.

But his body couldn't react in time.

Bang!

The countless red lights exploded simultaneously, scalding blood splattering throughout the room.

The floor, cabinets, books—everywhere the blood splattered caught fire, sending wisps of black smoke rising.

Edric, closest to the blood droplets, could only lower his head further and close his eyes to avoid them being burned through.

The blood splattered on him, just like on Blake, instantly burned through his clothes.

Without the protection of his ice armor, Edric's arms were riddled with countless burn holes.

Some were even completely burned through!

But no blood flowed out, because the skin around the holes was charred, preventing any blood from draining.

The air was thick with the smell of burnt flesh.

Edric's eyes were tightly closed. Thanks to his last instinctive head tilt, his eyes were unharmed, but a spurt of blood grazed his face, leaving a downward-sloping black scorch mark.

Beads of sweat beaded on Edric's forehead, but he remained silent.

Sensing that he was no longer injured, Edric abruptly opened his eyes, his blue pupils locking onto Bruce's neck.

For [Blade], these wounds had no effect; on the contrary, they made him stronger.

An eighth-tier [Battle-hardened Warrior]'s ability was to grow stronger with each battle, lacking many flashy skills, all being passive.

Battle-Scarred Body allowed him to receive comprehensive enhancements to any wounded part of his body after healing.

Blood Battle Instinct allowed him to gain more enhancements the more severe the injury, unless he suffered a fatal blow.

This enhancement was all-encompassing; not only did his strength increase, but his speed and defense also improved.

His only active skill is to imbue his blades with the [Blade] Spirit, increasing their sharpness, hardness, and toughness.

Bruce's attack just now didn't kill Edric, only leaving a wound.

This triggered his Bloodlust Instinct passive ability.

He is now greatly enhanced in all aspects.

Edric's longsword slashed towards Bruce's neck, cutting through the air with a swift, sharp sound.

*Thud!*

The sound of the blade slicing through flesh echoed in the room, but none of the three from the Security Bureau showed any joy.

Edric's powerful, two-handed swing had somehow become stuck in Bruce's neck!

The longsword had slashed diagonally into Bruce's neck, but the blade stopped less than an inch in.

No matter how hard Edric tried, he couldn't penetrate any further!

Only then did Edric show a look of astonishment.

It was as if he had struck iron.

That was Edric's first thought.

Then he tried to pull the sword out, only to find it impossible!

Completely stuck!

In less than a second of exchange, Edric was certain of one thing: he could not defeat this man.

A full-force attack, so easily parried, he didn't even bother to defend himself.

Bruce's eyes shifted to Edric, his gaze filled with contempt and indifference.

"That's it?"

The next instant, several bone spurs emerged from the arm holding his cane, piercing through Edric's limbs and abdomen!

Edric flew backward, crashing into the wall, his longsword slipping from his grasp.

"Cough..."

He coughed up blood.

But Bruce's attack wasn't over.

The bone spurs embedded in Edric's body began to grow outwards again, and in the blink of an eye, countless smaller bone spurs sprouted from the pierced areas!

Just as Bruce was about to kill Edric outright, a coffin made of ice suddenly trapped him, physically snapping the protruding bone spurs.

"Ice Coffin"

One of Blake's seventh-tier extraordinary abilities, but this move is quite costly. His Ice Armor, with its rapid spiritual regeneration, can easily last a day, but he can only create a few Ice Coffins per day.

It can create a coffin made of ice within a 15-meter radius around him, with the temperature inside reaching as low as -100 degrees Celsius.

This is Blake's strongest offensive skill.

The instantaneous -100 degree Celsius temperature can freeze a person's skin in seconds, inevitably freezing the respiratory tract, lungs, and blood vessels as well.

Severing the semicircles formed by the bone spurs that had been severed from Bruce's connection, Blake warily watched Bruce.

But Bruce remained calm.

Water vapor continuously rose from his body.

Blake felt a sense of foreboding.

He was heating the temperature of his blood.

Like himself, he could also control his body temperature.

Thanks to [The Cup's] incredible physical abilities, Bruce could remain unharmed even if he heated his blood to an attack-ready temperature.

Even extremely low temperatures, deadly to humans, couldn't harm Bruce.

This was [The Cup's] most formidable weakness: its incredible survivability.

Blake was now filled with despair; his strongest offensive skills were completely ineffective against him.

Was the Security Bureau about to be wiped out?

Bruce calmly raised his hand from inside the ice coffin, grasped the blade of the longsword still lodged in his neck, and pulled it out.

The blood that followed splattered onto the ice coffin, releasing a puff of steam.

The longsword melted and deformed in his hand.

Bruce raised his fist and slammed it into the ice coffin in front of him.

The ice coffin then began to melt.

Not daring to be negligent, Blake immediately increased the spiritual energy supplied to the ice coffin.

Bruce, in turn, increased the spiritual energy supplied to Boiling Blood.

The two entered into a contest of spiritual energy quantity.

Seventh-tier superhumans who advanced through magical potions wouldn't have a significant difference in the amount of spiritual energy they possessed.



Chapter 71 The An Yin Bureau's Defeat is Inevitable


Unlike unorthodox superhumans who directly absorb spiritual energy, legitimate superhumans cannot store more spiritual energy simply by absorbing more.

Legitimate superhumans have a limit to the amount of spiritual energy they can store.

While their internal spiritual energy is below its limit, it slowly regenerates over time, stopping once the limit is reached.

Simply put, legitimate superhumans have a cap on their mana bar, while unorthodox superhumans have no limit, but the drawback is that they cannot regenerate on their own and can only store it by absorbing external spiritual energy.

Furthermore, the "quality" of spiritual energy is also superior to that of unorthodox superhumans.

A single point of spiritual energy from an unorthodox superhuman might only strengthen an arm, but from a legitimate superhuman it could strengthen the entire body.

Similarly, for the same volume of spiritual energy, legitimate superhumans have a higher density.

Therefore, if they were to continue this direct confrontation, the advantage would likely tilt towards the Security Bureau.

Blake shared this view, which is why he chose to compete with Bruce in terms of stored spiritual energy.

But things didn't seem to be going as he expected.

His face was already showing signs of fatigue from the depletion of his spiritual energy, while Bruce remained completely relaxed.

Could this guy not have become a superhuman through potions and rituals?

But so many superhuman abilities, it didn't seem like he'd acquired them through any unorthodox means… Was it because of that cane?

Without time to think further, Black gave a wink to Berlaut at the door, then, while Bruce's attention was focused on himself, he made a small hole in the ice coffin behind him.

The hole was directly in front of Bruce's heart.

—

At the street corner outside, Glenner's hand, gripping his pistol, was slightly sweaty. He wiped his hands on his trousers and continued staring at the house.

His spiritual alarm had been going off constantly since earlier, warning him of danger.

But at the same time, the curiosity belonging to the [Lamp] made him want to go inside and see what was happening.

Just then, someone tapped him on the shoulder.

Glenner turned around and saw Zobert, his face ashen.

“You…”

“Don’t shout, I’ve controlled this one too. Make way, I need to go in and take a look.”

Still devoid of emotion, like a machine, Glenna immediately realized that the person in front of her had been possessed.

Obediently, Glenna moved aside to make room for him.

Then a loud “bang” came from the second floor of the building, and a figure was sent flying, landing a few meters in front of Glenna.

The huge impact kicked up smoke in the street, and Glenna squinted for a while before realizing that the person who had landed in front of her was Blake.

Glenna was shocked and immediately went to help him up.

"Captain, what's going on?"

"Cough, cough, Glenner, there's no time to explain. You immediately tell everyone to hide, then immediately go back to headquarters and send a telegram: our operation has failed. Tell everyone to hide quickly and wait for reinforcements from headquarters."

Blake grabbed Glenner's shoulder, quickly finished giving instructions, and then shoved Glenner.

"Hurry."

But reality didn't give Glenner a chance to escape.

A crimson beam of light shot out from a hole in the second floor of the building.

A thin beam of intense heat shot towards Glenn's head, distorting the air around him.

Bruce had unleashed his own boiling blood as a bullet.

Blake tried to push Glenn away, but it was too late. He could only barely create an ice wall with his abilities.

Blocking the attack was impossible; he could only hope the ice wall would cushion Glenn's fall and save him.

The heat from the blood easily melted the ice wall in front of Glenn, the blood's momentum undiminished, poised to pierce through Glenn's body.

Blake felt a pang of despair.

If Glenn died now, how many of the other members of the Security Bureau would survive without the prior warning to hide?

Then a golden light barrier appeared in front of Glenn, blocking the incoming blood bead, but the powerful kinetic energy of the bead still sent Glenn flying backward several meters.

Blake breathed a slight sigh of relief.

Thankfully, Glenner had contained the [Angel of Protection]. That attack shouldn't have reached its maximum damage tolerance; the [Angel of Protection] wouldn't exit its successfully contained state yet, and Glenner could use it to block a few more attacks.

"A sealed artifact? What a pity. This thing might have been more troublesome if it were on you."

Bruce, carrying the unconscious Berout, leaped down from the second floor and slowly walked towards Blake.

Blake didn't answer.

Indeed, if he had brought the sealed artifact, he might have been able to use the [Angel of Protection's] defense to unleash all his spiritual energy for offense, thus suppressing Bruce.

But the [Angel of Protection] could only be contained once in a lifetime. If one greedily tried to contain it again, it would physically devour them.

Blake had already contained it while working at headquarters.

He looked warily at Bruce, summoning the last vestige of spiritual energy within his body for a final struggle.

A long sword, piercing through Bruce's left chest from behind, was still embedded in his chest.

Clearly, the sneak attack had failed.

Although Berlaut's sword had successfully pierced Bruce's heart, a steady stream of spiritual energy continued to heal his body, even making the fatal wound less fatal.

Bone spikes burrowed into Berlaut's body, absorbing his blood.

Although he was perfectly capable of absorbing the spiritual energy in his blood, he adhered to the principle of not wasting any, absorbing as much as possible.

"I was too cautious after all. That bunch of useless people from the An Yin Bureau even wasted one of my insect eggs."

"If something had really happened to Miria, it would have been better; at least the insect egg could have been recovered."

"Oh well, let it be. When all those mortals become spiritual beings, it'll be good. I can kill the weaker ones and collect some spiritual materials to recoup my investment."

The battle with the An Yin Bureau dispelled Bruce's concerns.

He had no information on other secret societies, but as things stood, Bruce could practically rule Dexter City.

After collecting all the loot, should he just hold a ritual and sacrifice the city?

That would also replenish the spiritual energy his cane was consuming.

As he thought this, he noticed Zobert standing not far away.

"You're here? Pretty punctual. Though you're useless now. Oh well, we can't keep the Dex City outpost anyway, might as well turn you back into a potion and take you away."

"Zobert, come here."

His words carried an arrogant air of entitlement, as if he saw Zobert as nothing more than a tool to be used at will.

Black turned and saw Zobert, his despair deepening.

It's over. Can Glenner really escape?

But this time, Zobert didn't obediently come forward.

Bruce looked at him with some confusion, only to see him raise his right hand, palm up, hand half-clenched, index finger extended.

Then a faint black mist appeared above his index finger, followed by a pale gray sphere of light emerging from the mist.



Chapter 72 An Yin Bureau delivers victory speech...?


It started as the size of a fist, then was compressed into a ball the size of a fingernail.

But its brightness noticeably increased.

What was that?

The same question popped into Bruce and Blake's minds.

Judging by its color, it resembled a moth, but wasn't Zobert a cup? And it was only in its early stages. Where did this moth-like spirit come from?

Before Bruce could ponder this further, a beam of light suddenly shot out from the ball of light and hurtled towards him.

It took a total of 3 seconds from the appearance of the ball of light to the release of the beam.

This wasn't a long time, but it wasn't too long for Bruce to react. However, he made absolutely no preparations.

The main reason was that, in Bruce's view, the dull and rigid Zobert wouldn't dare to attack him.

And after the beam was fired, he couldn't react at all. Before he could even blink, the beam had already reached his head.

In an instant, the spirituality within him began to become chaotic, and his ability to think was stripped away by the state of confusion.

He was completely paralyzed, his gaze vacant.

The hand gripping Berlaut's neck loosened, and the bone spur was pulled from Berlaut's body.

The hand holding the cane also instinctively loosened, and the bone cane fell to the ground and bounced a few times.

The long knife embedded in his left chest finally had a chance to inflict damage.

His heart, pierced by the long knife, lost the spiritual nourishment of the [Cup] and began to beat slowly due to excessive blood loss.

Blood also trickled from Bruce's mouth.

Blake stared wide-eyed at Zobert, who remained in a raised-arm position.

What was that attack all about?

Wasn't this guy one of that mentor's subordinates?

Did the Church of the Holy Communion have such a deadly move?

And why were they fighting?

Did they turn to the light?

An undercover agent of the organization?

From another secret society?

An enemy of the Church of the Holy Communion?

Countless questions flooded Blake's mind; he couldn't understand.

The intelligence clearly stated that Zobert, an eighth-tier being, could injure a seventh-tier being—something even he, a fellow seventh-tier transcendent, couldn't do!

And judging from Zobert's current state, without even blinking, this attack might have been completely painless for him!

So, what is the purpose of such a powerful individual joining the Holy Communion Church?

Or is there a problem with Glenner's intelligence?

He couldn't figure it out, but Blake was certain that figuring this out wasn't his primary objective.

He turned to look at Bruce, who was still standing there as punishment.

Using his last bit of spiritual energy, he conjured a 10cm-long ice spike in front of him.

He'd deal with this guy first, then worry about the rest. Since Zobert had come to his aid, he was at least on his side now.

If he could just drive this ice spike into his body and freeze his heart...

Thump.

A heavy thud sounded behind Blake. Still condensing the ice spike, Blake instinctively turned around to look.

Zobert lay face down on the ground, a large pool of blood gushing out from beneath him.

...

?

Blake opened and closed his mouth again, his lips moving several times as if he wanted to say something, but nothing came out.

"You didn't risk your life for that move, did you?"

Blake felt like he wasn't fully awake.

What did it mean that the followers of the Holy Communion Church were actually its enemies, willing to risk their lives to buy time for the Security Bureau in revenge for killing their superior?

Although he felt extremely uneasy, Blake's actions didn't stop. The ice spike was fully formed, and without hesitation, Blake fired it directly at Bruce's heart!

---

Meanwhile, 50 meters above the ground, roughly the height of an 18-story building, Su Mu stood on the black mist, somewhat unsatisfied.

Although it seemed a little late, and he hadn't seen how the four were fighting inside, their condition after emerging showed that the Security Bureau was in a sorry state, practically being beaten senseless.

But Su Mu tried it and found that compressing the spiritual energy and then using it to smash people was surprisingly effective.

Since spiritual energy itself has almost no physical mass, when Su Mu compressed it and fired it, the speed was even close to 50% of the speed of light!

The only drawback was the relatively small amount of black mist remaining on Zobert, and the time it was detached from his body couldn't exceed 10 seconds. Otherwise, Su Mu felt that increasing the pressure a little longer would allow it to go even faster.

But even this value still shocked Su Mu himself.

Goodness, if this could be given some physical mass, wouldn't it be enough to instantly kill a bunch of superhumans with a mere "passing by"?

If this speed had mass, even a fifth-tier being couldn't withstand it, right?

Even with mass, it might spontaneously combust before it even flew off.

This move was inspired by the blood arrow Bruce shot.

However, it was quite dangerous back then, especially for Glenner. If Su Mu hadn't had his eyeball pop out to provide a shield, Glenner would probably be half-dead too.

But just now, his curiosity got the better of him, and he used up all the black mist on Zobert. Now, he reckons Zobert is definitely dead.

Alice's legs were so weak she could barely stand. She clung tightly to Su Mu, her arms wrapped around his waist, her eyes too terrified to open.

"Lord of the Mist, it's...it's too high!"

Su Mu patted Alice's head.

"It's alright, you won't fall. Are you coming down with me, or waiting for me up here?"

Alice stared wide-eyed at Su Mu, her eyes filled with despair. "Is...I'm going to jump?!"

How could he say that? How could this be called jumping?

—

Below, the ice spike successfully pierced Bruce's body and began to melt rapidly, but it still slowed his heartbeat, eventually stopping it completely.

Success!

Blake, who had been tense all along, suddenly relaxed, a wave of exhaustion washing over him.

He slumped back down, his chest heaving as he gasped for breath.

The battle, which had lasted less than two minutes, had drained him of all his spirit and strength; he couldn't even lift a finger.

At this moment, other personnel who had heard the commotion nearby rushed over.

Vivian, noticing her captain lying on the ground, quickly ran to his side.

"Captain, what happened?"

He asked her while gripping his pistol tightly, his other hand reaching for a smoke grenade at his waist.

“It’s alright, it’s over. Go check on Berlaut, and Edric in the house. Their condition is much worse than mine. Oh, and that guy with the knife in his chest, go up and give him a few more shots to prevent him from coming back to life.”

“Yes, Captain!”

Without hesitation, Vivian turned and walked towards Bruce, who was also lying backward on the ground. The long knife in his chest had pierced through his entire body, and blood was gushing from the wound.

Vivian called a few more men over. Two men moved Berlaut aside and gave him basic first aid, while the others fired several shots at Bruce’s head. Finding they couldn’t penetrate his skull, they emptied the magazines into his neck.

Even the [Cup] probably won’t survive this, right?

Vivian felt slightly relieved seeing Bruce’s face riddled with bullets.

His airway was now riddled with bullet holes.

Just as Vivian turned to go upstairs to check on Edric, Bruce's fingers trembled, and his heart, replenished by spiritual energy, began to beat again.

He had only just recovered from the chaos.

Upon recovering, he felt a sharp pain in his brain and a chill in his heart.



Chapter 73 Is this guy still alive?


My head hurts so much...

A second later and my heart would have completely failed.

Bruce's chest began to rise and fall slightly.

But he was still alive; he hadn't lost yet.

Because he had been using the spiritual energy stored in his cane, his own body was still almost at full spiritual energy. With the continuous consumption of the [Cup's] spiritual energy, his body began to heal itself.

His heart started beating again, and the flesh on his neck began to regrow, pushing all the bullets out.

Vivian, who had only taken a few steps, stopped and turned to look at Bruce lying on the ground in disbelief.

She had sensed spiritual energy fluctuations on Bruce through her abilities.

Impossible?

He's still alive?

Under the attack of three superhumans?

And his heart had stopped beating just now, hadn't it?

Then, she saw Bruce actually stand up straight; the bone spurs growing from his back had supported him.

"Everyone, quick, keep attacking!"

Vivian immediately shouted, signaling the other members of the Security Bureau to return to defense.

The sound instantly drew everyone's attention in the quiet night.

Glenner, who had been knocked away in one blow, stopped rubbing his chest upon hearing this. He stared at Bruce, who had stood up again, as if he'd seen a ghost.

Wait, he's still alive?

Are all the [Cup] monsters?

Although he hadn't witnessed the battle inside the house, he'd seen Bruce pierced by a knife, hit in the head by an unknown beam of light from Su Mu, smashed in the heart with an ice spike, and finally even subjected to a prolonged desecration by Vivian and her group!

Comparing himself to this, Glenner suddenly realized that one superhuman wasn't always the same.

He was still someone who would be dead with a single bullet…

Then fear crept into his heart.

"It's over. The captain's already collapsed from exhaustion, Edric's fate is unknown, and Berlaut has been drained unconscious by the Holy Communion's mentor.

And Zobert, controlled by the mysterious figure, is bleeding profusely after firing a beam…

If only he had waited a few days and asked Headquarters to send more reinforcements...

He was genuinely panicking now. After taking a bullet to the face from an unknown beam, he had almost been killed by the Security Bureau captain with an ice spike.

Having escaped death, Bruce became cautious, completely unwilling to rely on his body to withstand these attacks again.

Damn it, where did Zobert get such a terrifying attack method?

Bruce picked up his cane from the ground, his spiritual energy returning to his body.

Vivian, who had initially intended to suppress the enemy with her pistol, immediately signaled to her nearby teammates to quickly evacuate with the wounded.

The teammates, realizing that lingering would likely result in a total wipeout, immediately dispatched three to run towards the house, intending to escape with Edric inside.

At that moment, the semi-circular bone shield began to shrink, eventually transforming into a suit of armor that Bruce donned.

Except for his eyes and joints, he was completely covered.

The first thing Bruce did upon regaining his sight was to look in Zobert's direction, simultaneously controlling the blood flowing from his body to move to his joints, preparing to unleash a saturation attack on him.

Then he saw Zobert lying in a pool of blood.

Was he…dead?

Bruce was somewhat taken aback. Seeing Zobert's calm and collected demeanor, he'd assumed the attack wouldn't have cost him anything.

So, it turned out to have come at the cost of his life?

Well, after all, for a mere eighth-tier opponent, an attack capable of harming him certainly required such a price.

Bruce completely relaxed.

All the enemies who posed a threat to him were now incapacitated; what followed was a one-sided slaughter.

Bruce's gaze shifted to Blake lying on the ground.

With his blood already boiling, Bruce decided to switch his focus to Blake.

"Die."

?

He never intended to let me go!

Finally, a cross was sliced ​​across his left chest; he could feel his heart being cut into four pieces.



Chapter 78 Confiscation


Bruce, terrified, tried to beg for mercy, but before he could speak, his vision began to blur.

*Thud.*

His pupils trembled violently as he stared at the scene before him, but the light quickly faded from his eyes.

"Ugh, vomit..."

Some of the An Yin Bureau members with weaker mental fortitude turned pale and vomited.

Before Su Mu lay Bruce, neatly cut into 10cm squares.

The square pieces of meat fell to the ground with a slightly sticky sound.

Su Mu frowned; cutting him like this seemed a bit disgusting...

Looking at the minced meat, Su Mu felt even his human side starting to feel nauseous.

Although cutting it was stress-relieving, watching it afterwards really made him lose his mind.

However, it didn't affect Su Mu much, but it was rather unfriendly to the ordinary onlookers.

Su Mu glanced at the An Yin Bureau members who were nearly vomiting themselves unconscious, then crouched down and used an invisible black mist to break down the shredded flesh into a spiritual cluster, which he then absorbed into his body.

Combined with the spiritual energy of the other [Cup] fragments, it reached a quantity roughly equivalent to the late-stage seventh rank. Each rank advancement in the extraordinary path resulted in a several-fold increase in spiritual energy; the combined spiritual energy of Miriam and Zobert barely amounted to a fraction of Bruce's. The bulk of this spiritual cluster still came from Bruce's donation.

Although this quantity was far greater than the [Moth] fragments in Su Mu's hand, using the [Cup] fragments to directly attack someone was somewhat like giving the opponent a buff.

However, the [Cup] wasn't only for directly attacking people.

Su Mu extended a finger, and a small portion of the [Cup] spiritual energy cluster appeared above his finger. The cluster then began to shrink, disappearing in the blink of an eye, leaving a drop of blood in its place.

Su Mu compressed the blood droplet with black mist, then used the mist to provide a thrust from behind. The compressed droplet shot out instantly, even creating a sonic boom at Su Mu's fingertip!

Su Mu had cleverly copied Bruce's ultimate move, the principle being the same as using the [Cup]'s spiritual energy to shape a body. Although it didn't have a heating function yet, the kinetic energy alone was terrifying.

The flying blood droplet punched a small hole in the outer wall of Bruce's house, then cracks appeared around the hole, and the entire wall shattered.

Su Mu: ......

An Yin Bureau members: ......

"Wait, is this... real?"

Someone couldn't help but ask.

Su Mu turned to look at the An Yin Bureau members, but they all turned away, unable to meet his gaze when stared at by the two crimson beams of light.

"We didn't see anything."

"This ground is really... ."

"Damn it, who was that who spoke? I'll dock their pay when we get back!"

Su Mu looked away.

"Forget it, they probably can't give me a ticket anyway."

The blood bead that Su Mu had been forcibly controlling with a transparent black mist was no longer effective; Su Mu simply released the mist, letting it fall to the ground.

Now, what intrigued Su Mu the most was still that cane.

Although he didn't know its exact purpose, seeing Bruce's panic as soon as it left his hand, it must be something valuable.

Su Mu picked up the cane and tossed it in his hand.

The feel of the staff wasn't much different from a normal one, but the fact that it was made of human bone made Su Mu feel somewhat uneasy.

Su Mu blinked, and the faint red light that had previously been only deep within his pupils filled his entire eye when he opened it again.

Switching back to his original perspective, Su Mu could see that the staff still contained some of the [Cup]'s spiritual energy.

Su Mu tried to infuse it with his current spiritual energy, barely filling it halfway; the remaining energy was roughly equivalent to three mid-stage seventh-tier cultivators.

This amount even made Su Mu wonder if it was some kind of sealed artifact.

But it didn't have the same special feeling he had when the [Guardian Angel] gave him.

Of course, it was also possible that his charging efficiency was too high; normally, filling the staff's energy reserves would probably take a lot of lives and time.

No doubt, this world really is a cannibalistic society.

Literally.

Getting up and carrying the staff, Su Mu walked towards the people from the Security Bureau.

Blake nervously swallowed as Su Mu approached, a few beads of cold sweat forming on his forehead.

"Calm down, calm down. This young man didn't accept the Holy Communion Church's deal and even saved the An Yin Bureau twice. By all accounts, he's at least a friendly and potentially recruitable individual."

Then another possibility occurred to Blake.

The corpse of Zobert had been directly turned into a spirit, and the Holy Communion Church's mentor had also been killed and absorbed as a spirit.

Judging from their conversation, this young man seemed to be a member of some secret society.

Considering the current situation, this young man seemed to be collecting spirits. Why?

Blake didn't know, but he immediately spoke up: "Sir, if you want spirits… I can give you mine, but the others aren't actually superhumans. Killing them won't grant you any abilities. Please spare them. Also, our bureau has two vials of potions that we can give you."

His expression remained unchanged as he spoke, but his heart pounded.

This was a lie. Blake was gambling that the boy hadn't witnessed the entire battle and didn't know how many superhumans the Security Bureau had.

Vivian looked at her captain in surprise, wanting to say something, but was silenced by his stern gaze.

"What potions?"

"Ninth-tier [Forging] and eighth-tier [Awakening]."

[Awakening] was originally intended for Vivian's future advancement, but there was no time to consider the future now.

Survival was the priority.

Su Mu couldn't feed the eighth-tier [Awakening] to anyone right now, but potions were always a good thing, and they would come in handy later.

"Chichu, I'll take both bottles, and this one too. Any objections?"

Su Mu waved his hand, and the longsword that Blake had been using flew into his hand. Su Mu brandished the blade in front of everyone.

“No problem. If you want it, we have some more in our department that you can have.”

Blake readily agreed. Although the knife was borrowed from the An Yin Bureau in the neighboring city, and it felt a bit unethical to just give it away like that, his life was at stake, so even if it was a sealed artifact, he had no choice but to give it away.

However, because their department was rather remote, they only had the one on Glenner, so it would be a bit difficult if this young man wanted it.

“Then help me search Bruce’s estate.”

Su Mu naturally began ordering the An Yin Bureau personnel to help him search for the stolen money.

Searching himself was too much trouble; it was better to hire someone for help, and it wouldn't cost him anything.

"What are you standing there for? Didn't you hear this gentleman's order! Get moving!"

Blake roared at his team members, who were standing there dumbfounded.

Although this guy seemed easy to talk to, which superhuman didn't have some mental issues? If he upset him, everything would be over.



Chapter 79 The Legacy of the Holy Communion Church Branch


At 8 PM, inside Bruce's mansion, the rain outside had stopped without anyone noticing.

Su Mu lay sprawled on the sofa, his head resting on his hand.

This cult was incredibly extravagant; the sofa was softer than his own bed.

Alice sat on the small sofa to Su Mu's left, her legs together and slightly bent, her hands resting on her knees. Beside her lay the longsword originally made for Black, and her face was covered by a black mist mask identical to Su Mu's.

Although it was called a mask, it was more like a blurring, obscuring her facial contours and any discernible features.

Her clothes were a classic blue and white dress.

Alice had bought this after Su Mu casually remarked that she felt this style suited her better.

This outfit gave Su Mu a very welcoming feeling.

Alice was put down in a corner after Su Mu and the others from the Security Bureau had entered the house.

Alice, who was afraid of heights, was constantly radiating unease, so they decided to let her come down and experience the atmosphere of dividing the spoils.

However, Alice was still too reserved, sitting upright and silent.

Half an hour later, Vivian ran down from the second floor and began her report in front of the sofa in the living room.

"Sir, Captain, the inventory is complete. The assets are close to one million, including three bottles of [Cup] Rule potions, three [Cup] Spiritual Fingers (which, according to the records, seem to be materials for their potion-making), and three secret manuals."

Upon hearing the report, Alice's eyes widened in disbelief.

She didn't understand the supernatural, but she understood money!

One million! From what Alice had learned these past few days, even a white-collar worker in the capital would have to work for at least 40 years without eating or drinking to accumulate that much money!

One million was even enough for her family to buy two to four townhouses after moving to the capital, enough to live comfortably for the rest of their lives just from rent!

Alice felt dizzy.

One million...one million soldiers, how much is that in ling? I think it's 12:1.

And what about in sovereigns?

It seems 1 sovereign = 20 ling...

Even selling myself wouldn't get me that much. Does this mean I'm about to become a noble?

Am I dreaming?

Killing superhumans is so lucrative...?

Alice's determination to become stronger surged.

I...I want to become stronger too, and then I'll do this!

Maybe I can even keep the Lord of the Mist as a mistress someday...No, no, Alice, what are you thinking?!
